TL;DR: 本文提出Ex-Omni-2D，一个全模态对话框架，能够生成包含文本、个性化语音和参考条件视频的协调响应。框架通过视觉思维计划（VTP）和共享声学-时间接口，将语音解码与视频帧在线对齐，并通过教师-学生蒸馏实现流式增量生成，以1.293的RTF在四GPU上实现端到端运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态对话模型虽能理解多模态输入并合成口语回复，但回复在视觉上是"无形体"的（visually disembodied），缺乏与语音同步的视觉化身或视频输出，用户体验受限。

Method: 提出Ex-Omni-2D框架：(1) 预测结构化视觉思维计划（VTP）描述场景、情感和运动；(2) 生成响应文本和原生多码本语音单元；(3) 语音单元构成共享声学-时间接口，解码为语音并与视频帧在线对齐；(4) 通过异构数据学习响应与化身路径，避免大规模四模态监督；(5) 全序列视频生成器作为教师，蒸馏为少步块因果流式学生，采用前缀流（Prefix Streaming）机制保持干净潜变量以缓解累积迟滞劣化。

Result: 在四步推理下，完整四GPU流水线在400×720/720×400分辨率下实现端到端RTF为1.293，展示了实用的质量-效率平衡点。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D通过结构化规划、共享声学-时间接口和教师-学生蒸馏，实现了可流式生成、视觉具身的全模态对话响应，为实际部署提供了可行的质量-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [62] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Tree-of-Ideas（ToI）框架，通过两个阶段——EvoTrace重建跨引用的分支学术轨迹，EvoAgent跨轨迹推理生成接地气的研究想法。在六个AI研究主题上，ToI得分（6.27/10）远超最强基线（5.36），接近人类论文参考（6.29），在创新性和接地性上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究想法生成方法要么将论文视为无结构上下文，要么将学术演化建模为孤立的引用链，忽视了研究轨迹之间的交互作用。作者认为有效的研究构思需要超越对先前工作的静态理解，追踪研究问题和解决方案在文献中如何演化，以发现跨轨迹的汇聚问题和互补解决方案。

Method: 提出两阶段框架ToI：第一阶段EvoTrace从引文中重建分支性的学术轨迹，追踪演化方法、已解决问题和未解决空白；第二阶段EvoAgent跨轨迹进行推理，识别汇聚性问题与互补性解决方案，从而生成有根据（grounded）的研究想法。

Result: 在六个AI研究主题上，ToI在自动方法中得分最高（6.27 vs 最强基线5.36，10分制），其中创新性得分6.36、接地性得分7.00，整体得分接近人类论文参考分数（6.29）。

Conclusion: 跨路径演化推理有效提升研究想法生成质量，证明了将学术演化建模为交互式轨迹而非孤立引文链的实用价值，其生成的想法的接地性和整体质量已接近人类研究者的水平。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [63] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准生成与评估框架，从扁平的单标签动作语料库合成四层级层次化意图基准（动作、活动、低层意图LLI、高层意图HLI），保留动作层的真实预提取特征。通过转换模型在主体一致性约束下组装片段，并利用覆盖感知采样器将主体使用Gini系数从0.566降至0.248。通过将序列生成规则与评估所用的一阶逻辑规则保持不相交来规避循环监督风险，最终生成15,002个片段。四个参考基线（含图感知模型）均出现0.13-0.17 macro-F1的组合式保留差距，表明这是基准的结构属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为识别语料库存在两类局限：大规模语料只提供孤立的原子化标签片段，缺乏时间组合信息；而记录式复合活动语料库的层级浅、领域窄、层级固定。需要一种能从扁平单标签语料合成层级化意图基准的方法，以支持跨抽象级别的人类行为识别研究。

Method: 从扁平单标签动作语料出发，在转换模型下通过主体一致性约束组装片段，合成四层级（动作、活动、LLI、HLI）的层级意图基准。采用覆盖感知采样器改善主体使用均衡性。为规避循环监督风险，隔离序列生成规则与评估采用的一阶逻辑规则。发布本体、转换模型和生成器支持基准再生成与扩展。

Result: 合成产生15,002个片段，主体使用Gini系数从0.566降至0.248。所有四个参考基线（含识别效果最佳但未闭合差距的图感知模型）均表现出0.13-0.17 macro-F1的组合式保留差距，表明这是基准的结构属性而非模型伪影。逻辑无关基线仍违反保留语义规则，顺序破坏对照在种子变异范围内影响macro-F1，作为生成器一致性检查。

Conclusion: 该方法证明可从扁平单标签语料合成多层级意图基准，且主体平衡性和循环监督风险均得到有效处理。组合式保留差距的结构性存在说明该基准能有效反映层次化推理难度，适合作为跨层级人类行为识别的评测平台。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [64] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉技能卡（VSCs）这一状态条件化的记忆表征，将可复用的GUI操作流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定；并基于此构建SkillLens框架（通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互痕迹中构建VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并选择性扩展所需证据）及CardDistill方法（利用VSC证据作为教师上下文训练无需运行时检索的学生模型）。在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym上分别将冻结GPT-5.4-mini执行器的Step SR提升11.6点、Overall提升2.9点，CardDistill将Qwen3-VL-2B学生模型指标分别提升12.0和3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体虽然能感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策缺乏视觉程序性记忆：能够识别单个控件，却无法判断当前激活的是哪个熟悉的工作流、接下来哪个控件重要、或什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹虽保留此类信息但过长且含噪声，而纯文本技能常常遗漏使过程可适用的视觉状态，两者均不理想。

Method: 提出视觉技能卡（VSC）作为状态条件化记忆表征，绑定可复用程序与适用性线索、视觉证据及验证信号。SkillLens通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互经验中构建VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并由固定视觉语言模型执行器选择性扩展所需证据进行接地GUI动作预测；CardDistill利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文，训练一个无需运行时卡片检索的学生模型。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web上，SkillLens使冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器Step SR提升11.6点；在WebLINX-BrowserGym上Overall提升2.9点。CardDistill分别将学生模型Qwen3-VL-2B对应指标提升12.0和3.2点。

Conclusion: 视觉技能卡作为状态条件化记忆表征，能有效弥合交互轨迹与文本技能之间的鸿沟，为GUI智能体提供可验证、可检索的视觉程序性记忆；同时该表征既支持在固定执行器上的检索增强推理，也支持蒸馏至轻量学生模型实现无检索的运行时部署。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [65] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ChemWorld，一个可编程化学环境，将可复用的过程和观测组件编译为可执行世界，实现可控、可回放、可审计的自主化学实验研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主化学研究需要代理能够反复行动、观测和适应的环境。物理实验室提供真实材料证据但成本高且难以进行严格匹配的干预实验，而大多数数字环境保持底层实验世界固定不变，缺乏灵活性和可编程性。

Method: ChemWorld将公开实验契约（agent可见）与评估者拥有的化学和材料法则（agent不可见）分离。研究者可改变世界组成和操作条件，或在公开任务与交互条件不变的情况下修改单个隐藏法则。系统通过事务性执行记录操作、失败、资源变化和状态转移，实现完整轨迹的精确回放与审计。

Result: 全普查资格测试覆盖参考注册表、52个生成组成以及模块、接口、编译和无效操作测试。8个确定性实验案例展示共享生命周期语义、故障恢复和精确回放；6对父子世界分叉对在匹配公开条件下隔离了单一私有法则干预的效果。独立代理通过相同公开接口在非参考世界中完成了完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 在声明的组件和模型域内，ChemWorld为跨系统变化的化学世界中的实验研究提供了可控且可回放的基底，是对物理实验室证据和校准的补充。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [66] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMem 是一种为基于LLM的进化程序搜索设计的持久记忆架构，能够在运行结束后提取成功的变异策略并以结构化、任务感知的建议形式存储，在后续进化过程中检索相关指令以指导变异，从而减少冗余探索并提升搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的进化搜索框架在每次运行后丢弃了成功的变异知识，导致跨运行和跨任务的重复发现与学习机会被浪费。作者希望捕捉并复用这些可迁移的知识，以减少冗余探索。

Method: EvoMem采用两阶段架构：运行后阶段从成功的变异事件中提取并存储带来源（provenance）的候选变异建议；进化阶段则根据当前任务和程序上下文检索一小批相关指令来引导变异过程。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化及相关基准测试中，EvoMem在大多数评测设置下获得了目标指标或搜索速度的正向平均改进，同时也揭示了任务间的变异性。

Conclusion: EvoMem证明了持久记忆能够减少部分冗余探索，改善LLM驱动的进化搜索中成功策略的复用与适应。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [67] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hypothesis Frontier，一个验证器引导的神经符号框架，用于一阶概念合成任务。该框架对每个 LLM 生成的公式在所有训练对象上进行精确评估，逐步保留最强已验证假设，并利用剩余错误指导后续生成；符号处理修复无效公式并简化有效公式。在匹配的模型、问题集和 LLM 轮次预算下，该方法比重复原始提示生成能解决更多问题，且精确符号推理既帮助解决更多归纳问题，又压缩了所得公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成任务需要从有限的多个关系结构中推断出能一致分类对象的单一公式。虽然每个候选公式都可以精确评估，但量化一阶公式的搜索空间极其庞大，且 LLM 的输出往往语义上有前景但并非完全正确。这促使研究者构建一个结合 LLM 生成能力与精确符号验证的框架，以更高效地合成正确的概念公式。

Method: Hypothesis Frontier 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 验证器引导：每次迭代对 LLM 生成的公式在所有训练对象上精确评估，动态保留最强的已验证假设；(2) 错误引导生成：利用当前最强假设的剩余错误信息指导后续 LLM 生成；(3) 符号处理：修复无效公式（保持锚定于 LLM 生成假设），并在不改变任何训练预测的前提下简化 train-valid 公式，最后阶段执行精确简化以压缩最终公式。

Result: 在匹配的模型、问题集和 LLM 轮次预算条件下，Hypothesis Frontier 解决了显著多于重复原始提示生成的归纳问题。此外，最终公式选定后的精确简化步骤在保持所有训练预测不变的前提下，显著缩短了许多 train-valid 公式。

Conclusion: 精确符号推理在概念合成任务中发挥双重价值：既能帮助解决更多归纳问题（通过验证与错误引导提升 LLM 生成的准确率），又能压缩最终的公式表达（通过无预测改变的符号简化）。这验证了神经符号方法在一阶逻辑概念合成中的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [68] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用节点坐标的几何信息来增强约束传播的过滤算法，并应用于欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP）及其推广形式欧几里得广义TSP（EGTSP），实验证明该方法相比现有方法具有计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的约束规划方法处理欧几里得TSP时，通常先计算完整距离矩阵并将其视为一般TSP案例，忽视了点坐标所携带的几何信息。作者希望利用这些几何信息实现更强的约束传播，以提升求解效率。

Method: 在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下设计了新的过滤算法，这些算法利用欧几里得几何属性（如坐标位置）进行更紧凑的约束推理，并进一步将方法扩展到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）等变体。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于几何信息的过滤算法在计算效率上优于现有的仅依赖距离矩阵的约束规划方法。

Conclusion: 利用坐标几何信息可以显著提升约束传播的强度，从而在欧几里得TSP及其变体（如EGTSP）中获得更好的求解性能，该方法在实际路由和物流应用中具有潜在价值。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [69] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（复合具身智能体）范式，旨在弥补现有Agentic AI中数字智能体（transform软件状态）与具身智能体（transform物理状态）都无法以人的持续状态和主体性为核心进行建模、干预和评估的结构性缺口。该范式以人为中心，通过事件感知、可修正的长期记忆、个人世界模型和适度的干预策略形成闭环，将智能体AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体（如用药提醒软件、送药的实体机器人）只能改变软件或物理状态，却无法理解老年人错过服药究竟是遗忘、困惑、副作用还是主观拒绝，也无法判断何种支持最为合适。这暴露了Agentic AI的结构性缺陷：无论是数字智能体（Digital Agents）还是具身智能体（Embodied Agents），都没有将人的不断演化的状态和主体性作为建模、干预与评估的主要对象，因而缺乏真正以人为本的智能体范式。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents范式，构建闭环框架：（1）基于事件的多模态感知重构有意义的个人事件；（2）纵向、可修正的记忆提供时间上下文；（3）Personal World Models（个人世界模型）在替代决策与干预下估计未来个人状态与结果；（4）合规干预策略在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性与用户控制约束下选择适度支持；人与环境反馈持续更新闭环。框架采用目的受限、不确定性感知、用户可修正的表征，而非穷尽的Human Digital Twin。设计空间按人类状态目标、关系情境与智能体角色组织，并提出场景中心评估、主体性保持指标、基准需求、边缘原生个人模型与治理方向。

Result: 该框架统一了个人助理、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人机系统等分散能力，形成闭环机制；通过目的受限且可修正的表示避免了构建完整数字孪生的不切实际需求；并系统化了设计空间（人类状态目标、关系情境、智能体角色）及评估方法（场景中心评估、主体性保持指标、基准要求）与治理方向。核心结果是将Agentic AI的范式从"外部任务完成"转向"持续的人类益处"。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents为Agentic AI提供了一个以人为中心的新范式，将人的动态状态与主体性置于建模、干预与评估的核心，弥补了数字智能体与具身智能体之间的结构性缺口。通过对感知、记忆、世界模型与干预策略的闭环整合，以及治理与评估方向的系统化，该框架为构建真正服务人类持续福祉的智能系统奠定了概念与方法基础。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [70] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力战役的AI驱动框架，能够在受控模拟平台中端到端追踪策划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量与适应全过程，支持多达10万个智能体的可复现研究与红队分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操控威胁已从单语言模型生成的劝说性文本扩展到AI智能体集群（AI swarms）——即持续存在、相互协调并适应平台反馈的智能体群体，它们将组织化的战役伪装成普通社交互动。由于这类战役无法从孤立消息中识别，必须跨连续谱系（策划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量、适应）进行分析，现有框架缺乏端到端的可追踪模拟能力。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内构建完整的战役流程表示，连接角色角色、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化模型评估与模拟人群的配置变化。架构支持运行配置最多10万智能体的战役，并记录每次运行的参与者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径与测量规则。

Result: 实验表明IO Factory能够按规模执行战役时间线，并产出可检查的曝光证据以及配置信念变量的测量变化。该框架在多达10万智能体的配置下验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过记录关键要素，IO Factory支持可复现的研究与针对协调性影响力的红队分析，为理解和防御AI驱动的信息操控提供了受控、可审计的仿真基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [71] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ThinkRetrieve框架，通过在推理轨迹中动态注入检索到的已解决问题示例来增强大型推理模型（LRMs），改善测试时扩展的收益递减问题，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）通过分配额外推理时计算来生成扩展思维链以提升性能，但研究发现顺序式测试时扩展往往带来递减甚至负面的回报，因为更长的推理轨迹会伴随不确定性增加、错误累积和偏离原始问题。因此需要一种新的测试时扩展方式来克服这些局限。

Method: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，在LRM推理轨迹的每个推理步骤中动态检索此前解决过的示例（来自包含问题及逐步解决方案的外部语料库），并将检索到的示例直接注入到思考轨迹中，为模型提供"如何推理"的指导，而不仅仅是"什么事实相关"的信息。

Result: 在五个推理模型（参数规模1.5B-8B）上，对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ四个基准进行实验，ThinkRetrieve相较标准测试时扩展持续提升准确率，在AIME 2025上相对增益最高达60%。

Conclusion: ThinkRetrieve通过按步骤动态注入检索到的解决示例到推理轨迹中，有效缓解了顺序式测试时扩展的收益递减问题，为LRMs提供了更有效的推理引导策略。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [72] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedCGR是一个联邦生成式跨域推荐框架，通过将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID序列，在共享词汇表而不是私有交互数据上实现跨域对齐。该框架通过可靠性感知的语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号，并采用原型个性化生成器按域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，解决了联邦场景下跨域对齐困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域推荐在各联邦客户端间传递偏好知识时面临挑战：传统用于对齐物品空间的行为锚点（如重叠用户、共享交互信号）在联邦场景中往往稀疏、不可用或涉及隐私。现有方法无法在不交换私有交互信息或对齐域特定嵌入的情况下实现有效的跨域物品对齐。

Method: FedCGR包含三个关键设计：(1) 利用公共物品侧元数据将物品表示为离散语义ID（SID）序列，使跨域物品对齐通过共享词汇表实现，而非交换私有交互；(2) 通过可靠性感知的语义接口将本地协同过滤信号注入到SID生成器中，缓解语义-only瓶颈；(3) 训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域特定参数本地化，避免域异质性带来的负迁移。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域推荐场景中，FedCGR持续优于联邦生成式基线方法，并在全排序和采样两种评估协议下，与强序列推荐方法和联邦跨域推荐方法相比取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: FedCGR验证了将联邦CDR重新构想为"在稳定语义物品语言上的生成"这一范式的有效性，表明在联邦环境下通过共享语义词汇表而非私有行为信号实现跨域对齐是可行且高效的路径。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [73] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出XCoT-VLA，将冗长的自然语言思维链替换为紧凑可执行的XCoT标记，通过Reason-Action监督学习，实现自动驾驶中紧凑、可执行且直接连接轨迹生成的驾驶推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型能连接场景理解与轨迹生成，但基于自然语言的CoT推理冗长开放、解码成本高且难以作为面向动作的表征优化，不适合实时自动驾驶控制。

Method: XCoT-VLA包含三个核心部分：(1)从自动构建的Reason-Action监督中学习紧凑可执行CoT标记(XCoT)，轨迹数据提供动作证据，场景上下文提供因果语义；(2)XCoT序列通过共享多模态自注意力与固定轨迹查询保持上下文关联；(3)确定性token函数路由，对XCoT token应用Reason FFN，对轨迹查询应用Control FFN进行流匹配轨迹生成；另提出XCPO作为可选的精化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布集上纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323，变道场景横向FDE从1.616降至0.648；仅用2-6个可执行XCoT token即可大幅降低自回归推理开销，并在实时规划预算内运行。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接连接轨迹生成，在保证实时性的同时提升轨迹预测精度。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [74] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-FiLLM是一个从可执行计算树生成金融推理基准的框架，基于真实表格构建，通过符号化求值获得地面真值并以自然语言渲染问题，可实现零标注成本的任意规模基准生成，并支持四个独立可控的难度轴（计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小）。实验表明模型推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，对抗性数值扰动下下降高达47%；轻量LoRA微调可将准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上超越基座模型5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域评估LLMs方面取得了进展，但针对结构化数据的金融推理仍探索不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个可规模化、无标注成本且不继承生成器错误率的金融推理基准，以系统评估和推动LLM在金融表格推理上的能力。

Method: V-FiLLM框架从真实表格出发，构建可执行的计算树，通过符号化求值获得地面真值，再将计算树渲染为自然语言问题，完全排除模型参与标注流程。框架提供四个独立可控的难度维度（计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度、上下文大小），并验证了轻量LoRA微调在验证过的链式思维轨迹上的效果。

Result: 在开源模型上，推理深度增加时准确率最高下降51%；对抗性数值扰动下准确率最高下降47个百分点。LoRA微调将持有问题准确率从81.1%提升到85.6%，并在FinQA基准上比基座模型高出5个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM在结构化金融数据的稳健推理方面仍面临显著挑战，尤其是在推理深度增加和数值扰动场景下。针对性的低成本参数高效微调（LoRA）结合验证过的链式思维轨迹，是提升金融组合推理能力的有前景方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [75] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip 是一种免评估的无评估压缩方法，通过寻找技能最短的忠实结构解释来压缩自进化智能体的技能。其核心思想是"一次解释，多次引用"：在适用的作用域中只声明一次重复规则，将重复动作序列因子化为共享过程，并仅保留差异作为显式异常。该方法形式化为对技能契约和残差的类型化最小描述长度目标，并受到覆盖约束，支持单次模式和持续 Zip-on-Write 模式，在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在累积技能时，相同需求常被重复表述于多个分支、示例和警告中，通用动作序列被复制而非复用，导致技能注入昂贵且难以维护。通用提示压缩不适用于此场景，因为技能并非平坦段落：名称描述定义适用条件、工作流控制执行、工具和输出契约约束有效性、罕见异常即使无样本激活也需保留。评估引导压缩虽有帮助但引入回滚、成本和评估集依赖。

Method: SkillZip 将技能压缩形式化为一个类型化最小描述长度（MDL）目标，优化对象为技能契约与残差，并施加硬性覆盖约束以覆盖每个提取的触发条件、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段。该方法提供简单的共享阈值，通过构造保留独特的罕见规则，支持高效的局部更新。包含单次模式（一次结构化提取调用加确定性优化）和持续 Zip-on-Write 模式（整合每个自进化补丁，无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史）。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，SkillZip 在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销方面均展现了有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip 通过免评估的结构化压缩方案，成功解决了自进化智能体技能膨胀问题，在保持技能完整契约（触发、工作流、工具、义务、输出）的前提下实现高效压缩，并支持持续更新模式，为长时自进化智能体的技能管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [76] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个轨道交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，统一建模不同城市实体（如站点、道路段、兴趣点）之间的空间与语义交互，以支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测、站点区域商店推荐）。通过链接数据形式发布，实验验证其在多个任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测）依赖大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织上往往忽略了各类城市实体之间复杂的空间和语义交互关系，导致建模不充分。

Method: 构建RTSKG（轨道交通站点知识图谱）数据集，设计统一的schema来集成异构城市实体（轨道交通站点、道路段、兴趣点等），显式建模它们之间的空间与语义交互，并以Linked Data形式发布（可通过w3id.org访问）。

Result: 在站点区域商店推荐和知识增强的客流预测两项任务上的评估验证了RTSKG的有效性，表明其能够支持城市级轨道交通站点分析。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为一种显式建模城市实体间交互关系的知识图谱数据集，为城市级轨道交通站点相关任务提供了有效的知识支撑，具有广阔的应用潜力。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [77] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了agentic coding README（如CLAUDE.md）中指令无限增长的现象，称之为"灾难性记住"（catastrophic remembering），与持续学习中著名的"灾难性遗忘"相对。作者通过1,867个仓库中247,694条指令生命周期的实证分析，发现指令无界增长（生命周期内增长226%），并证明提示词注释（prompt comments）可以停止这种增长，减少99.3%的多余指令，并在真实场景中提升agentic指令跟随性能达23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic coding README在实际仓库中无界增长，直到仓库退役或被整体重写。作者将其归因于不完美的记忆机制：追加指令总是廉价的，但删除指令需要O(2^|D|)的验证成本，导致指令只增不减。这一现象是持续学习中的"灾难性遗忘"的反面，但尚未被系统研究，值得深入刻画并提供解决方案。

Method: （1）大规模实证分析：收集1,867个仓库中247,694条指令生命周期，量化指令增长速率（+226%，每commit净增4.9条）和删除概率的对数风险（log-hazard -0.032/commit）。（2）理论构建：定义"灾难性记住"概念，并解释删除成本O(2^|D|)的机制。（3）方法设计：通过反转IFEval构建可知最优提示（verifiable worlds），在提示词中添加编码潜在推理过程的注释，验证其能否移除多余指令。（4）迁移验证：将同一方法应用于WildIFEval，测试真实场景下的agentic指令跟随性能提升。

Result: （1）agentic提示词确实无界增长，生命周期内增长+226%，每commit净增4.9条指令；指令越老越不可能被删除（log-hazard -0.032/commit）。（2）在反转过IFEval构建的可验证世界中，提示词注释编码潜在推理后，可移除99.3%的多余指令（+211.3%降至+1.4%）。（3）在WildIFEval上，提示词注释可使真实场景的agentic指令跟随能力提升最高23.1%。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统刻画了agentic提示词的"灾难性记住"现象，论证了提示词注释（prompt comments）作为解决方案的有效性——它通过编码指令的潜在推理过程来降低删除成本，从而打破指令只增不减的恶性循环。作者以"如果英语是新的代码，为什么我们还没有注释？"收束，呼吁为agentic提示词建立类似代码注释的最佳实践。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [78] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN（structural Logic Tensor Networks），是逻辑张量网络（LTN）的扩展，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为一阶语言中的一等公民引入，使时间、序列和关系约束能够直接在逻辑层面表达。sLTN在无结构维度时退化为原始LTN，并提供了基于PyTorch的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架基于张量操作解释一阶逻辑，但仅适用于平铺的个体数据集合，无法显式捕获时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构信息。这限制了LTN在时序推理、序列建模和关系学习等任务上的表达能力。

Method: (1) 将结构维度定义为与领域特定组织相关联的命名张量轴（如时间步、序列位置、图节点），使其成为语言中的一等公民；(2) 形式化sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义；(3) 结构维度可被显式量化、通过结构关系关联，并在逻辑层面直接表达时态、序列和关系约束；(4) 在无结构维度时证明框架恢复原始LTN语义；(5) 提供基于PyTorch的声明式签名、公式解析和张量化解释的实现。

Result: sLTN扩展了LTN以支持结构维度，使结构成为语言的一等要素，并在无结构维度时退化为原始LTN作为特例。提供了完整的PyTorch实现，并在代表性时序与序列推理示例上进行了说明。

Conclusion: sLTN作为一种神经符号框架，将结构化组织纳入LTN的逻辑语言中，扩展了LTN在时间、序列和图结构数据上的适用性，并配套开源了sltn库以供社区使用。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [79] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过改进Grothendieck常数K_G的已知界限这一具体案例，深入分析了AI在数学研究中的实际应用效果，展示了AI系统能够产生被领域专家视为新颖的见解，并详细讨论了AI在数学研究中的优势、劣势以及为AI创造突破性见解所需的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI智能体越来越多地被用于数学研究，但如何有效使用它们仍然不清晰。作者希望通过一个深入的案例研究，展示AI在实际数学问题上的应用效果，并总结经验教训，为将来使用AI进行数学研究提供指导。

Method: 以一个具体的大规模案例研究为基础：利用AI研究系统改进Grothendieck常数K_G的上下界。K_G刻画了组合问题与其连续松弛之间的难度关系，作者将K_G的已知最佳界限从原有的结果分别收紧为6π/11 ≤ K_G ≤ π/(2log(1+√2)) − 10^−4。整个研究过程详细记录了AI系统的推理过程、产生的见解以及领域专家的评估反馈。

Result: 成功将Grothendieck常数K_G的已知界限收紧为6π/11（下界）和π/(2log(1+√2)) − 10^−4（上界），并且这些改进是通过AI研究系统实现的，该系统提出的见解被领域专家认定为新颖且有价值的。

Conclusion: AI在数学研究中能够产生突破性见解，但需要创造理想条件才能实现。作者详细总结了使用AI进行数学研究的经验，包括其优势和局限性，以及如何为AI创造能产生突破性见解的环境条件。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


### [80] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了六个前沿模型在显式引导压力下的行为可引导性，发现模型不仅在引导幅度上存在差异，更在响应模式（mode）上存在质的区别。通过300对配对项目（价值观冲突、推理诱发、推理抑制）和24,480次盲审评估，作者发现GPT-5在拒绝披露推理过程上表现独特（99%对抗其他模型0%），Claude Opus 4.7与GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。研究进一步通过线性探针（0.87准确率）和方向注入干预（0%到86%驱动行为）将Llama的最大行为分裂追溯到其内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型在训练数据、目标和安全管线上存在差异，但这些差异是否会在显式引导压力下产生可测量的行为差异，这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用300个配对基线与引导项目加40个验证项目，跨越三个类别（价值观冲突、推理诱发、推理抑制），对来自六个开发者的六个前沿模型进行行为可引导性评估。所有六个模型作为盲审裁判，基于固定行为量规对每个响应进行分类，产生24,480个判断，通过留一法共识评分。利用Llama作开放权重模型，用线性探针从残差流解码行为（0.87保留准确率），并通过方向注入进行干预扫描。

Result: 模型在引导下不仅行为改变程度不同，给出的响应模式也不同，有些模式仅出现在一到两个模型中。GPT-5在拒绝披露推理的同时保留答案完整（99%对比其他模型的0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。线性探针在Llama内部以0.87准确率解码行为，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动至86%。所有发现通过token预算补救和假设盲审提示控制实验验证。

Conclusion: 不同模型的行为可引导性存在质的差异而非单纯量的差异，这些差异可追溯至模型内部表征并可被干预性操控，提示模型间架构或训练差异导致不同的行为模式。研究为理解前沿模型在压力下的行为分化提供了基础，并为安全管线的设计干预提供了可能的方向。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [LEGO: Leveled Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10057)
*Yuning Peng,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Yipeng Lu,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEGO是一种用于开放词汇3D场景理解的新方法，核心创新在于从多视角2D分割中自适应地重建3D一致的概念语义层级（如"花盆→花束→花蕾→花瓣"），并通过CLIP嵌入和多模态场景图实现层次化分割与空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型（如SAM）虽能在2D中识别多粒度结构，但分割结果严格受视角限制、缺乏跨视图一致性；而3D场景理解需要统一的、跨视图对齐的多层次语义层级，以支撑复杂的场景理解与空间推理。

Method: LEGO首先利用SAM在多个2D视角下获取多粒度的分割结果，然后通过自适应重新分级将视角依赖的粒度统一为3D一致的概念层级；接着以CLIP嵌入为这些层级分割进行开放词汇语义标注，最后融入空间关系构建逐层语言场景图（level-wise language scene graphs），赋能大语言模型进行空间推理与视觉定位。

Result: 实验表明LEGO在可提示（promptable）和开放词汇（open-vocabulary）3D分割基准上均达到了新的最先进性能，展示了先进的层次化场景分解能力和上下文感知的空间推理能力。

Conclusion: LEGO通过将多视图2D粒度分割统一为3D一致的语义层级，成功弥合了2D基础模型与3D场景理解之间的鸿沟，证明了层级场景图可以显著增强大语言模型的复杂空间推理与精确视觉定位能力。

Abstract: We introduce LEGO for advanced open-vocabulary scene understanding. Beyond basic concept recognition, its core innovation lies in capturing the intrinsic semantic hierarchies within the scene, such as the "flowerpot -> bouquet -> bud -> petal" lineage. While foundation models like SAM can identify multi-granular structures in 2D, their partitions are strictly perspective-bound and lack cross-view consensus. LEGO self-adaptively re-grades volatile multi-view SAM granularities into a unified, 3D-consistent hierarchy. This provides precise supervision for the structurally coherent, multi-level segmentation of 3D scenes. By grounding these segments with CLIP embeddings, LEGO recovers open-vocabulary semantic logic across hierarchical levels. Furthermore, by incorporating spatial relationships, we elevate these segments into level-wise language scene graphs, effectively empowering Large Language Models to perform complex, context-aware spatial reasoning and precise visual grounding. Experimental results demonstrate that LEGO establishes new state-of-the-art performance across both promptable and open-vocabulary 3D segmentation benchmarks, exhibiting advanced hierarchical scene decomposition and context-aware spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Signpost Watermarking: Joint Optimization for Visual Watermark Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10091)
*Shruti Agarwal,Vishal Asnani,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练方法，使不可见视觉水印能够与其它独立训练的水印系统更好地共存。作者发现这种共存性天然存在于图像和视频水印中，并进一步提出一种解码器感知的训练目标来显式优化水印间的共存，从而支持分层内容溯源信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究表明独立训练的图像水印模型可以共存（即水印集成），但这种共存是偶然性质而非显式优化目标，导致干扰不受控，可能降低解码鲁棒性或视觉质量。作者希望将这种共存从偶然性转变为可控的优化目标，并扩展到视频水印领域。

Method: 首先通过实验验证视频水印同样具有共存性；然后提出一种解码器感知（decoder-aware）的训练目标函数，在训练水印时同时考虑其他水印解码器的存在，通过联合优化来显式改善图像和视频水印之间的共存表现。

Result: 实验表明该训练方法能有效改善图像和视频水印间的共存性，优于独立训练的天然共存结果，在保持解码鲁棒性和视觉质量的同时，实现了更可控的水印共存。

Conclusion: 这项工作为分层溯源信号提供了一条实用路径：可以训练"指示性水印"来标记独立部署的溯源水印系统的存在，支持内容真实性和权利保护的分层溯源机制。

Abstract: We present a method for training imperceptible visual watermarks to coexist with other such watermarks. Recent work has shown that independently trained image watermarking models can coexist with surprisingly limited interference, enabling watermark ensembling. However, this coexistence is a serendipitous property rather than an explicit optimization objective, leaving interference uncontrolled and potentially reducing decoding robustness or visual quality. We first show empirically that the same coexistence property extends to video watermarking. We then show that both image and video watermarks can be trained with a decoder-aware objective to improve coexistence. Our results suggest a practical path to signpost watermarks that indicate the presence of independently deployed provenance watermarking systems, supporting layered provenance signaling for content authenticity and rights.

</details>


### [83] [4D-WAM: 4D Consistent World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10107)
*Jiacheng Fu,Yibo Yuan,Meng Tian,Yue Li,Jiangtong Zhu,Jianhua Han,Yueyi Zhang,Jianwu Fang,Jianru Xue,Hang Xu,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4D-WAM通过利用几何基础模型提供训练期监督，使世界-动作模型(WAMs)能够理解并预测4D一致的驾驶场景，在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型(WAMs)通常仅基于2D视频数据进行训练，无法理解底层4D驾驶场景的结构，从而生成视觉上看似合理但4D不一致的未来预测，误导下游轨迹规划。

Method: 1) 将WAM预测的未来帧输入几何基础模型，利用4D感知响应定义4D一致性损失，在训练期间监督模型理解并预测物理一致的4D场景，且不增加推理成本；2) 识别出WAM中的"早期决策"现象，提出面向决策的时间步采样策略，强调在早期高噪声阶段（驾驶决策主要形成的阶段）强化4D监督。

Result: 4D-WAM能够有效建模4D一致的场景演变，并在具有挑战性的NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将几何基础模型引入训练监督并结合决策导向的时间步采样策略，可以在不增加推理开销的情况下使WAMs获得4D一致的场景理解能力，从而显著提升自动驾驶轨迹规划性能。

Abstract: Emerging World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated promising performance in autonomous driving by jointly modeling future driving scene evolution and trajectory planning. However, existing WAMs are typically trained with video data, which is only 2D projections of the underlying 4D driving scene. Consequently, WAMs fail to understand and capture the structure of 4D scenes and thus generate visually plausible yet 4D inconsistent future predictions that mislead downstream planning. To alleviate this issue, we present 4D-WAM, a model that leverages geometric foundation models for training-time supervision to enable 4D consistent world modeling. Specifically, we feed WAM-predicted future frames into a geometric foundation model, and use 4D-aware responses to define a 4D consistency loss. This loss encourages the model to understand, represent, and predict physically consistent 4D scenes during training, without additional inference cost. Moreover, we identify an early-decision phenomenon in WAMs and propose a decision-oriented timestep sampling strategy that emphasizes supervision at early, high-noise stages, where driving decisions are primarily formed. By propagating 4D supervision to this critical decision-formation phase, the proposed strategy further improves trajectory planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that 4D-WAM effectively models 4D consistent scene evolution and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [84] [P3CA: Encoder-Agnostic Interpretation of Vision Foundation Model Embeddings via Spatial Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10131)
*Amoon Jamzad,Dilakshan Srikanthan,Faranak Akbarifar,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出位置提示PCA（P3CA），一种与编码器无关的方法，用于在通道丰富的空间张量上进行局部探测。通过用户选择的空间提示估计特征归一化和主导协方差方向，并将得到的投影应用于整个张量，可视化局部信息方向在整个空间中的表达情况。该方法无需修改编码器、重训练或任务标签，并集成在3D Slicer工作流（EmbedVision）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型广泛用作医学图像计算的复用编码器，但其高维空间嵌入难以检查——现有方法只能通过下游任务性能或全局降维来理解特征，无法对特定局部区域进行细粒度的语义探查，缺乏区域条件化的表征可视化和解释工具。

Method: P3CA方法：输入用户选择的空间提示区域，在该区域内估计特征归一化统计量和主导协方差方向（局部PCA），然后将这一投影应用于整个空间张量，生成区域条件化的可视化。该方法编码器无关，无需重训练或任务标签。同时开发了基于3D Slicer的交互式工作流EmbedVision。

Result: 在自然图像、结直肠病理基础模型嵌入和空间转录组张量三类数据上评估：提示投影能揭示全局PCA所抑制的局部结构；从冻结三维投影中改善提示匹配的病理判别性能；支持学习得到的表征与实测空间表征之间的对比分析。

Conclusion: P3CA提供了一种区域条件化的表征透镜，使得医学图像计算中复用视觉基础模型的嵌入可以被交互式、局部性地理解和分析，弥补了全局降维方法的不足，有助于医学基础模型表征的解读与对比。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are increasingly used as reusable encoders in medical image computing, yet their high-dimensional spatial embeddings are difficult to inspect beyond downstream task performance or global dimensionality reduction. We propose position-prompted PCA (P3CA), an encoder-agnostic method for local probing of channel-rich spatial tensors. Given a user-selected spatial prompt, P3CA estimates the feature normalization and dominant covariance directions within that region, then applies the resulting projection to the full tensor to visualize where locally informative directions are expressed. This produces a region-conditioned representation lens without modifying the encoder, retraining, or requiring task-specific labels. We implement P3CA in EmbedVision, an interactive 3D Slicer-based workflow, and evaluate it across natural images, colorectal pathology foundation-model embeddings, and spatial transcriptomic tensors. Across these settings, prompted projections reveal local structure suppressed by global PCA, improve prompt-matched pathology discrimination from frozen three-dimensional projections, and support comparison between learned and measured spatial representations.

</details>


### [85] [MAD-HOI: Masked Autoregressive Diffusion for Generating Articulated Hand Object Interactions from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10162)
*Ananya Bal,Kartik Sharma,Ethan Lai,Samyak Tiwari,Liza Dahiya,Chaitanya Chawla,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAD-HOI方法，通过掩码自回归+扩散生成手物交互（HOI）序列，支持变长生成、复合动作、动作补全/插值及终止预测，在ARCTIC和GRAB数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成HOI序列的方法（扩散模型和自回归模型）各有局限：扩散模型仅支持原子动作的文本到运动生成且需预先指定序列长度，自回归方法虽具序列级灵活性但依赖离散运动码，丢失接触敏感的细节信息。需要一个统一框架同时支持变长生成、复合动作序列、运动补全/插值及可靠终止，且不牺牲物理合理性。

Method: MAD-HOI包含两阶段：首先在连续潜空间中编码手部和物体运动并保持解耦（disentangled）以维持流级控制；然后使用掩码自回归Transformer预测上下文特征，条件化一个流匹配（flow-matching）头。单一训练目标即可实现原子/复合动作生成、条件运动补全与插值，以及EOM（End of Motion）终止预测。

Result: 在ARCTIC和GRAB数据集上进行全面评估，实验表明MAD-HOI相比其他开源基线方法能生成更多样且物理合理的手物交互序列。

Conclusion: MAD-HOI通过结合掩码自回归与连续空间扩散的优点，以单一训练目标统一了多个任务（生成、补全、插值、终止预测），证明了在多样化、物理合理性方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: Methods for text-based generation of hand-object interaction (HOI) sequences primarily focus on producing smooth, physically plausible trajectories. A truly utilitarian method should additionally support variable-length generation, composite motion sequences, motion completion and infilling, and reliable termination without compromising physical plausibility. Standard diffusion models for HOI generation are typically trained only for text-to-motion generation on atomic motions and require the motion length to be specified a-priori. Autoregressive (AR) methods provide greater sequence-level flexibility, but commonly depend on discrete motion codes, which can lose contact-sensitive motion detail. To address these key limitations, we present a model performing Masked Autoregression with Diffusion for HOI generation (MAD-HOI). Our method starts by encoding hand and object motions in a continuous latent space while keeping them disentangled to maintain stream-wise control. This is followed by a masked autoregressive transformer to predict context features that condition a flow-matching head. MAD-HOI is capable of motion generation for atomic and composite articulated sequences, conditioned motion completion and infilling, as well as EOM (End of Motion) prediction from a single training objective. We provide comprehensive evaluations for these capabilities and benchmark our method on the ARCTIC and GRAB datasets. Our experiments demonstrate that our method generates more diverse and physically plausible interactions compared to other open-sourced baseline methods.

</details>


### [86] [Motion Artifact-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for 3D Brain MRI Motion Artifact Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10170)
*Mojtaba Safari,Shansong Wang,Zach Eidex,Matthew Goette,Tonghe Wang,Zhen Tian,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSRL-MAR，一种无需配对数据或运动标签的3D脑部MRI运动伪影校正框架。通过三阶段训练策略（对比学习、伪影合成网络、自监督生成器），在模拟和临床数据集上均实现了优越的运动伪影去除性能，且不依赖临床难以获取的配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑部MRI中患者运动会导致信号丢失、模糊和几何形变，严重损害定量分析。现有深度学习方法依赖配对（干净-污染）数据或k空间采集信息，这些在临床环境中几乎不可获得，因此亟需一种不依赖配对数据的运动校正方法。

Method: SSRL-MAR采用三阶段训练策略：(1)对3D patch进行对比学习，通过对比干净图像与合成污染图像提取运动表征；(2)训练运动伪影感知的合成网络，从干净扫描生成运动伪影；(3)训练运动伪影感知生成器，利用学习到的降质模型进行自监督监督以恢复干净体积。此外支持无监督域自适应以提升临床数据性能。

Result: 在in-silico数据集上，PSNR达到23.81dB，SSIM 91.55%，NMSE 0.79%。在in-vivo MR-ART数据集上，相比仅用源域监督训练的模型，无监督域自适应后PSNR提升最高达2.0dB，与需要实际不可得配对数据的最优监督模型差距仅0.25-0.47dB。在轻度运动水平下，胼胝体和脑室系统等结构的体积误差降低超过50%。

Conclusion: SSRL-MAR为3D脑部MRI运动校正提供了稳健且可扩展的图像域解决方案，无需前瞻性采集配对数据或采集特定校准，可实现大规模神经影像研究中可靠的结构定量分析。

Abstract: Patient motion remains a source of image degradation in brain MRI, leading to signal loss, blurring, and geometric distortion that compromise quantitative analysis. Existing deep learning methods for motion correction typically rely on paired clean-corrupted data or k-space acquisitions, which are rarely available in clinical settings. We propose SSRL-MAR, a motion artifact-aware unpaired representation learning framework for motion artifact reduction that requires neither paired training data nor explicit motion labels. SSRL-MAR employed a three-stage training strategy: (1) contrastive learning on 3D patches to extract motion representations by contrasting clean and synthetically corrupted images, (2) a motion artifact-aware synthesis network to generate motion artifacts from clean scans, and (3) a motion artifact-aware generator to restore clean volumes using the learned degrader for self-supervised supervision. On in-silico dataset, SSRL-MAR achieved PSNR 23.81dB, SSIM 91.55%, and NMSE 0.79%. On in-vivo MR-ART dataset, the pretrained model reduced motion distortion, and unsupervised domain adaptation further improved anatomical fidelity. Against a source-only supervised model trained on the same simulated pairs, SSRL-MAR improved PSNR by up to 2.0 dB on MR-ART after unsupervised domain adaptation, and remained within 0.25-0.47 dB of an oracle supervised model that requires real paired data unavailable in practice. At the milder motion level, volumetric error in structures such as the corpus callosum and ventricular system decreased by more than 50%, confirming improved neuroanatomical consistency. These results indicate that SSRL-MAR provides a robust and scalable image-domain solution for 3D brain MRI motion correction, enabling reliable structural quantification in large-scale neuroimaging studies without requiring prospectively acquired pairs or acquisition-specific calibration.

</details>


### [87] [DoseBridge: Denoising Diffusion Bridge Model for Dose Prediction in Lung Intensity-Modulated Proton Therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10173)
*Zerun Zhang,Xiaoda Cong,Xiangkun Xu,Peter Y. Chen,Xuanfeng Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DoseBridge，一种用于放射治疗剂量预测的去噪扩散桥模型，利用CT作为结构化桥端点并编码束流几何信息，在52例晚期肺癌IMPT患者上优于两种深度学习对比模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放疗剂量预测模型大多仅依赖CT图像和解剖结构，忽略了调强质子治疗（IMPT）剂量对束流几何的强依赖性；同时临床数据集往往较小，需要更适应小样本且能感知束流的预测模型

Method: DoseBridge是首个用于放疗剂量预测的去噪扩散桥模型：以患者CT作为结构化桥接端点，将计划特定的束流几何编码为空间对齐的束流掩膜（beam mask）；采用多尺度融合将CT、靶区、危及器官和束流掩膜表示结合，仅增加1.95%的参数量

Result: 在52例晚期肺癌（60 Gy/30次）回顾性评估中，42例训练、10例测试。DoseBridge达到MAE 4.170 Gy、PSNR 23.06 dB、SSIM 0.798，均优于两个对比深度学习模型。靶区D95偏差0.62±1.6 Gy，危及器官平均剂量差异-0.32至0.24 Gy，NTCP差异分别为急性食管炎-0.40±2.2和放射性肺炎0.52±3.4个百分点。仅更改束流掩膜即可重定向预测的低剂量入射区域，同时保持高剂量靶区

Conclusion: DoseBridge作为首个去噪扩散桥放疗剂量预测模型，具有作为肺癌IMPT束流感知规划先验的可行性，但仍需在更大规模的外部队列中进一步验证

Abstract: Most radiotherapy dose-prediction models use only CT images and anatomical structures, although intensity-modulated proton therapy (IMPT) dose also depends strongly on beam geometry and available clinical datasets are often small. We present DoseBridge, a denoising diffusion bridge model that uses the patient CT as a structured bridge endpoint and encodes plan-specific beam geometry in a spatially aligned beam mask. Multiscale fusion combines CT, target, organ-at-risk, and beam-mask representations with 1.95% additional parameters. DoseBridge was retrospectively evaluated on single-institution CT images and treatment plans from 52 patients with advanced-stage lung cancer treated with 60 Gy in 30 fractions; 42 cases were used for training and 10 for testing. Performance was assessed using image-similarity, dose-volume, and Lyman-Kutcher-Burman normal-tissue complication probability (NTCP) metrics and compared with two deep-learning models. On the test cohort, DoseBridge achieved a mean absolute error of 4.170 Gy, peak signal-to-noise ratio of 23.06 dB, and structural similarity index of 0.798, outperforming both comparison models on these metrics. Clinical target volume D95 differed from the reference dose by 0.62 +/- 1.6 Gy; signed organ-at-risk mean-dose differences ranged from -0.32 to 0.24 Gy, and NTCP differences were -0.40 +/- 2.2 and 0.52 +/- 3.4 percentage points for acute esophagitis and radiation pneumonitis, respectively. Changing only the beam mask redirected predicted low-dose entrance regions while preserving the high-dose target region. To our knowledge, DoseBridge is the first denoising diffusion bridge model for radiotherapy dose prediction. These results support its feasibility as a beam-aware planning prior for lung IMPT, pending evaluation in larger external cohorts.

</details>


### [88] [Human versus Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10181)
*Elena Sirotkina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对3023名美国成年人、1140万真实网络摄像头注视点数据的大规模受众侧测试，发现所有经过训练的计算机视觉显著性模型在实际表现上均不如一个未经训练的中央标注器，且剩余精度存在系统性偏差，偏向年轻、白人、温和派的观众。作者提出了基于群体自身注视行为来评估模型能否学习该群体的方法，并构建了衡量模型"看见所有人"能力的标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前商业化的预测注意力行业以显著性模型替代真实观众测量，但现有模型的声称能力从未从真实观众侧进行大规模验证。作者质疑：这些模型是否真的能预测不同人群的注视行为？模型的预测偏差是否会在人口统计学维度上系统性地倾向某些群体而歧视其他群体？

Method: 作者招募3023名符合美国全国配额的成年人，观看循环播放的新闻照片，收集1140万个网络摄像头注视点作为ground truth。将多个领先的显著性模型（以及一个未训练的中央标记基线）的输出与这些真实注视数据进行对比评估，分析预测精度及其在不同人口统计学群体（年龄、种族、意识形态等）间的偏差分布。进一步提出了一种基于群体自身注视行为判定模型是否可学习该群体的方法，并在所有支持的人口统计学维度上应用验证。

Result: 所有经过训练的网络模型在实际受众注视预测上均不如一个简单的、未训练的中央位置标记器。模型在中心位置之外的附加内容恰好落在目标受众从不关注的位置。模型仅存的预测精度存在系统性人口统计学偏差：对年轻、白人、意识形态温和的观众预测更准确，而对老年、黑人、意识形态极端的观众则表现更差。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了现有显著性模型在真实、多样化受众面前的严重失效与系统性偏差，指出需要以真实群体注视数据作为评估基准。作者提出了基于群体自身注视行为的学习/评估框架，并建立了衡量系统能否"看见所有人"的评判标准，为该领域的模型评估提供了新的规范基础。

Abstract: Computer vision saliency models predict where people will look, one map per image, and a billion-dollar predicted-attention industry sells those maps in place of measuring real viewers. I test the leading models from the audience side, against 11.4 million webcam gaze points from 3,023 US adults recruited to national quotas, viewing circulating news photographs. I show that an untrained central marker outperforms every trained network, because the content the networks add on top of the center falls where these audiences never look. What accuracy remains is systematically biased, favoring younger, White, and moderate viewers over older, Black, and ideologically extreme ones. I propose a way forward and build on what a group's own gaze reveals about whether a model can learn that group at all, and I apply it across every demographic axis this sample supports. Ultimately, I show how systems that decide what people see can learn to see everyone, and this study supplies the standard by which such a claim should be judged.

</details>


### [89] [More Accurate, Less Human: Gestalt Grouping in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10195)
*Sudhanva Manjunath Athreya,Sai Phani Kumar Malladi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个行为测试套件（behavioral battery），用人类感知研究中已发布的四类分组任务数据（标记颜色奇偶性、颜色序列计数、剪影识别、物体奇偶性）对45个视觉模型进行评分，检验模型是否像人类一样进行格式塔式（Gestalt）视觉组织。结果显示与人类反应的一致性捕捉了常规性能指标无法区分的感知组织方面，且多个闭源模型的对齐度远低于其基准精度所暗示的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉通过格式塔操作（同色点成组、相似标记聚类、形状补全为物体）来组织视觉内容，可视化设计正建立在这类操作之上。然而，视觉模型是否以这种方式组织视觉内容尚未被系统检验过，需要一种可复用的方法来审查进入可视化管线的模型是否如其人类受众那样组织所看到的内容。

Method: 构建一个行为测试套件，基于先前感知研究中的人类数据，对四类分组任务（标记颜色奇偶性、颜色序列计数、剪影识别、物体奇偶性）进行评分。将该套件应用于45个模型，涵盖五个训练家族：监督学习、自监督学习、对比视觉-语言编码器、开源权重VLM和闭源基础模型。

Result: 该测试套件揭示，与人类反应的一致性能够捕捉到常规性能指标无法区分的感知组织方面；多个闭源模型的对齐程度显著低于其基准准确率所暗示的水平。

Conclusion: 针对已发表的感知数据进行评分，为可视化研究提供了一个可复用的标尺，无需进行新的用户研究，即可审查进入可视化管线的模型是否像其人类受众一样组织视觉内容。

Abstract: Human vision organizes what it sees into wholes: same-colored points group into series, similar marks cohere into categories, and shapes complete into recognizable objects. These are the Gestalt operations that visualization design builds on. Whether vision models organize visual content this way has not been systematically tested. We introduce a behavioral battery that scores models against human data from prior perception studies on four grouping tasks: mark-color odd-one-out, color-series counting, silhouette recognition, and object odd-one-out. We apply it to 45 models across five training families: supervised, self-supervised, and contrastive vision-language encoders, open-weight VLMs, and closed foundation models. The battery reveals that agreement with human responses captures aspects of perceptual organization that conventional performance metrics fail to distinguish, with several closed models exhibiting substantially lower alignment than their benchmark accuracy would suggest. Scoring against published perception data therefore gives visualization research a reusable yardstick, requiring no new user study, for auditing whether the models now entering visualization pipelines organize what they see the way their human audience does.

</details>


### [90] [A Convolutional Layer Activation Dimensionality Reduction for Out-of-Distribution and Adversarial Attack Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10203)
*Leandro de Souza Rosa,Lorenzo Capelli,Clara Nunes Barrancos,Mauro Mangia,Riccardo Rovatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对卷积层的新型可控高压缩维度约简方法，并将其扩展到两种最先进的OOD和对抗攻击检测方法中，在保持或提升检测性能的同时显著降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络虽在图像分类中表现出色，但对分布外样本和对抗攻击的脆弱性引发安全性担忧。基于中间激活分析的检测方法需要维度约简来处理高维激活，但现有针对卷积层的维度约简方法要么缺乏对压缩/信息损失权衡的控制能力，要么产生过大的表示，因此需要一个可控且高压缩的约简方法。

Method: 作者仔细分析了两种最先进的检测方法及其对卷积层的维度约简机制，提出了一种具有可控高压缩级别的新型约简方法。同时，他们将这两种检测方法进行扩展，使其能够使用任意维度约简方法，并在OOD检测和对抗攻击检测任务上评估了性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，采用所提出的维度约简方法的检测模型在性能上持续优于或与最强基线相当；同时，由于该方法在所有对比方法中具有最高的压缩率，其在计算和内存占用方面也显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的可控高压缩维度约简方法在不牺牲检测性能的前提下，有效降低了计算与内存开销，为基于激活分析的OOD和对抗攻击检测提供了更具实用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the success of convolutional neural networks in image classification tasks and their general application in multi-modal models, their susceptibility to out-of-distribution and adversarial attack samples raises concerns regarding trustworthiness and safety. Among the approaches to tackle such issues, detection methods that analyze the model's intermediate activations to estimate a confidence score are a promising family that evaluates the decision process, relying on a dimensionality reduction step to enable efficient downstream processing of the high-dimensional activations. However, when considering convolutional layers, the dimensionality reduction methods in the literature either lack a mechanism to control the compression/information-loss trade-off or yield large representations. In this paper, we carefully analyze two state-of-the-art detection methods and their dimensionality reductions for convolutional layers and develop a novel reduction method with a controllable high-compression level. We extend these two state-of-the-art detection methods, enabling the usage of any dimensionality reduction, and evaluate their performance on out-of-distribution and adversarial attack detection. Results show that the detection methods with the proposed dimensionality reduction consistently perform better than, or comparable to, the strongest alternative. Furthermore, the proposed method is shown to reduce computation and memory footprints, given that it has the highest compression among the compared methods.

</details>


### [91] [Chain of Spatial Thoughts: Modality-Agnostic Spatial Grounding for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10278)
*Hunter Schofield,Mohammed Elmahgiubi,Mohammad Mahdavian,Richard Shi,Jinjun Shan,Amir Rasouli,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Space Tokens，一种轻量级、架构无关的框架，通过将场景级3D几何和物体级空间属性蒸馏为连续潜在token，显式地将空间表示嵌入VLM的思维链推理过程，无需额外的推理时模块或架构修改。在VSI-Bench上使Qwen3-VL-8B提升4.3%，SenseNova-SI-1.3提升1.3%，并在物体尺寸和房间尺寸估计上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理基准上表现优异，但SOTA方法通常依赖额外的空间编码器或推理时架构修改，增加了计算成本。亟需一种轻量、高效且无需额外推理模块的空间理解增强方案。

Method: 1) 将场景级3D几何和物体级空间属性蒸馏为连续潜在token；2) 这些连续空间token被直接融入chain-of-thought (CoT)推理过程；3) 学习到的表示可被显式解码以验证其编码了有意义的几何信息；4) 统一的token接口支持扩展到其他模态。

Result: 在VSI-Bench上，Qwen3-VL-8B提升4.3%，SenseNova-SI-1.3提升1.3%；在物体尺寸估计(79.2%)和房间尺寸估计(75.7%)上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 连续空间token为将几何推理集成到大视觉语言模型中提供了一种有效、可解释且计算高效的机制，证明了无需额外推理模块即可显著增强空间推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is fundamental to embodied intelligence, underpinning applications such as robotic manipulation, embodied navigation, and autonomous driving. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks, state-of-the-art approaches typically rely on additional spatial encoders or architectural modifications during inference, increasing computational cost. We introduce Space Tokens, a lightweight, architecture-agnostic framework that equips VLMs with explicit continuous spatial representations without requiring additional inference-time modules. By distilling scene-level 3D geometry and object-centric spatial attributes into continuous latent tokens, our method enables these modalities to be directly incorporated into a chain-of-thought reasoning process, thereby improving the VLM's spatial reasoning capabilities. At the same time, the learned representations can be explicitly decoded to verify that they encode meaningful geometric information, while the unified token interface remains extensible to additional modalities. Experiments on VSI-Bench improve Qwen3-VL-8B by 4.3% and SenseNova-SI-1.3 by 1.3%, while achieving state-of-the-art performance on object size (79.2%) and room size estimation (75.7%). These results demonstrate that continuous spatial tokens provide an effective, interpretable, and computationally efficient mechanism for integrating geometric reasoning into large vision-language models.

</details>


### [92] [TRACE-GS: On-Policy Trajectory Distillation with Privileged Geometric Conditioning for Sparse-View 3DGS Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10286)
*Linlian Jiang,Yuchen Xi,Sadman Rakib Pinon,Ruigang Yang,Yang Wang,Xinxin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE-GS 提出了一种基于策略内轨迹蒸馏的框架，利用训练时的特权几何条件（LUPI 设置），将扩散先验适配到稀疏视角 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）恢复任务中，对齐学生模型自身 rollout 过程中的去噪方向，从而克服传统扩散方法因监督状态与推理状态不匹配而导致的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复方法存在一个根本性局限：在独立噪声状态下的监督无法覆盖推理阶段实际到达的状态。在稀疏视角条件下，欠约束的几何结构从一开始就使去噪过程产生偏差，且这些偏差在 rollout 过程中不断累积放大。作者认为问题不在于网络架构的复杂度，而在于训练与推理状态分布的不一致。

Method: TRACE-GS 采用 on-policy 轨迹蒸馏策略：以额外训练视角提供更丰富几何信息的 teacher 模型，为学生模型在自身 rollout 过程中访问到的状态提供监督目标，从而在每一个访问状态上对齐去噪方向与跨视角响应。该训练阶段仅使用特权几何信息（LUPI 设置），部署时仅保留稀疏视角学生模型，其恢复结果作为伪观测用于 3DGS 精化。

Result: TRACE-GS 在不同数据集和多种稀疏视角设置下均取得一致的性能提升，并展现出良好的泛化能力。这是第一个从特权几何中推导出 on-policy 监督用于稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复的方法。

Conclusion: 通过在训练时利用特权几何信息进行策略内轨迹蒸馏，TRACE-GS 有效弥合了扩散模型训练与推理状态分布的鸿沟，为稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复提供了一种更根本性的解决方案，且部署时无需额外开销。

Abstract: We present TRACE-GS, an on-policy trajectory distillation framework that leverages privileged geometric conditioning at training time, thereby adapting a diffusion prior to sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) restoration. Rather than pursuing increasingly sophisticated restoration architectures, we identify a more fundamental limitation shared by existing diffusion-based approaches: supervision at independently noised states does not cover those reached during inference. In sparse-view 3DGS, under-constrained geometry biases denoising from the outset, and the resulting deviations compound along the rollout. TRACE-GS instead performs on-policy trajectory distillation: a teacher conditioned on richer geometry from additional training views supplies targets along the sparse-view student's own rollout, aligning denoising directions and cross-view responses at each visited state. This training-only geometry places TRACE-GS in the learning using privileged information (LUPI) setting. At deployment, only the sparse-view student is retained, and its restored renderings serve as pseudo-observations for 3DGS refinement. To the best of our knowledge, TRACE-GS is the first to derive on-policy supervision from privileged geometry for sparse-view 3DGS restoration, achieving consistent gains and strong generalization across datasets and sparse-view settings.

</details>


### [93] [SeFaR: Semantic Feature-aware Robustness Testing of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10289)
*Nusrat Jahan Mozumder,Divya Gopinath,Corina Pasareanu,Matthew Dwyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SeFaR框架，用于系统性地测试视觉模型的语义鲁棒性。通过分层概念模型、扩散模型和视觉语言模型生成逼真的语义保持扰动，以发现影响模型行为的未知特征和故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域作为感知模块被广泛部署，但其失败通常由罕见和代表性不足的场景引起，因此亟需评估感知模型的语义鲁棒性，即模型行为对真实世界感知变异的高层需求符合程度。

Method: SeFaR框架包含：(1) 分层概念模型，实现特征空间的结构化探索并支持用户自定义概念融入领域知识；(2) 利用最先进的扩散模型和视觉语言模型生成逼真的语义保持扰动；(3) 反馈驱动的自适应过程，生成可解释的诱发失败的语义概念及相应测试输入。

Result: 案例研究表明，该框架能有效满足需求前置条件，同时识别出影响模型决策的、与需求无关的特征，既能发现故障又能将故障归因于这些特征。

Conclusion: SeFaR通过语义特征中心化的系统性测试方法，在满足需求前条件下成功揭示并解释视觉模型的隐性故障，为安全关键领域的视觉模型验证提供了一种有效的鲁棒性评估手段。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains as perception modules, where failures are often caused due to rare and under-represented scenarios. This necessitates the need to evaluate the semantic robustness of perception models; conformance of behavior to high-level requirements over real-world perceptual variability. To address this, we propose SeFaR, a framework for systematic semantic-feature-centric testing of vision models. Given a natural-language requirement and a set of satisfying inputs, SeFaR evaluates robustness with respect to diverse realistic semantic variations that preserve requirement satisfaction. The approach employs a novel hierarchical concept model enabling structured exploration of the feature space and incorporation of domain knowledge via user-defined concepts. State-of-the-art diffusion and vision-language models are leveraged to generate photorealistic semantics-preserving perturbations and identification of previously unknown features impacting behavior. A feedback-driven adaptive process is adopted to generate interpretable failure-inducing semantic concepts along with corresponding test inputs. Evaluation on case studies demonstrates that the proposed framework effectively satisfies requirement preconditions while identifying requirement-independent features that influence model decisions, enabling it to both uncover faults and relate them to such features.

</details>


### [94] [MRIComp4Flow: Compression of 3D Brain MRI for Training Multi-Modal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10291)
*Lisa K. Fischer,Mykhailo Riabets,Daniel Rueckert,Benedikt Wiestler,Anke Meyer-Baese,Sandeep Nagar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模多模态MRI数据集的存储和I/O瓶颈问题，探索了使用JPEG2000和JPEG-LS等标准图像编解码器对3D脑肿瘤MRI进行有损压缩后，训练3D MRI生成模型（Wavelet Flow Matching）的可行性。实验表明在20:1压缩比下，压缩数据训练的生成模型在合成质量上与未压缩数据训练的模型统计等效（PSNR和SSIM均落在预设等效边界内），验证了JPEG2000压缩作为可扩展3D MRI生成建模的实际可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多模态MRI数据集带来巨大的存储和I/O开销，限制了3D生成模型在普通基础设施上的训练。虽然有损压缩已被证明能保持判别式分割网络的精度，但生成模型需要学习完整数据分布而非决策边界，其受压缩影响的程度尚未被探索。

Method: 采用JPEG2000或近无损JPEG-LS管道对每个3D体积进行压缩，然后基于BraTS图像序列（T1n, T1c, T2, T2f）训练Wavelet Flow Matching生成模型，并在验证集上评估模型性能，通过配对TOST检验验证统计等价性。

Result: 在20:1压缩比下，压缩数据训练的模型与未压缩数据训练的模型在合成质量上统计等效（ΔPSNR<1dB, ΔSSIM<0.02）：跨模态平均PSNR为27.3dB对比27.0dB，平均SSIM为0.95对比0.96。

Conclusion: JPEG2000压缩是向可扩展3D MRI生成建模迈出的实际可行一步，能在不降低合成质量的前提下大幅降低存储和I/O成本。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-modal MRI datasets impose substantial storage and I/O costs, limiting the training of 3D generative models on commodity infrastructure. While lossy compression is known to preserve accuracy for discriminative segmentation networks, its effect on generative models, which must learn the full data distribution rather than a decision boundary, is unexplored. We study whether standard image codecs can effectively compress semantically rich brain tumor MRI while preserving the fidelity required to train and deploy a 3D MRI generative model. Each 3D volume is compressed with JPEG2000 or a near-lossless JPEG-LS pipeline. Next, a Wavelet Flow Matching model, conditioned on BraTS image sequences (T1n, T1c, T2, T2f), is trained on compressed data, and the resulting models are evaluated on the validation set. At a 20:1 compression ratio, synthesis quality is statistically equivalent to a model trained on uncompressed data within a pre-specified margin ($Δ$PSNR $<1$,dB, $Δ$SSIM $<0.02$; paired TOST $p=[[p]]$): mean PSNR is 27.3,dB vs. 27.0,dB and mean SSIM is 0.95 vs. 0.96 across modalities. Our results indicate that JPEG2000 compression is a practical step toward scalable 3D MRI generative modeling without degrading synthesis quality. The codebase is available at https://github.com/lisafis/MRIComp4Flow .

</details>


### [95] [Frozen Brain-MRI Foundation Models Are Site Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10295)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CV
