Its annotations use a compact vocabulary of 20 direction-aware labels, and nearly half of the segments contain compound camera motion, with multiple movement primitives active simultaneously. Recognizing such fine-grained, compositional motion is hard for current MLLMs, whose visual encoders emphasize semantic content rather than the geometric evidence on which camera motion depends. Directly injecting features from a frozen 3D foundation model addresses this gap, but requires running the expensive geometry model on every input; we refer to this baseline as CamInject. We instead propose CamDistill, which distills the same geometric knowledge into lightweight camera tokens during training and removes the 3D model at inference. CamDistill matches the accuracy of direct feature injection without running the 3D teacher at inference. Together, CamChoreo and CamDistill advance camera-motion understanding from clip-level labeling to temporally grounded, compositional recognition. Project page: https://ddz16.github.io/cammotion.github.io/.

</details>


### [163] [SafeCA: Safe Cross-Attention Localization and Regulation for Text-to-Video Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10933)
*Siyuan Liang,Yupeng Qiu,Junfeng Fang,Rong-Cheng Tu,Jiaxing Huang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeCA是一种针对文生视频（T2V）扩散模型的特征级防御机制，基于对干净样本与越狱样本在交叉注意力特征空间中线性可分离性逐步增强的发现，通过注意力掩码、能量归一化和轻量级语义适配器来抑制异常激活，并通过反向传播特征异常信号检测恶意token，在不显著增加推理开销的情况下将越狱成功率降低约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对T2V模型越狱攻击的防御方法多依赖输入过滤或重构，不仅计算延迟高，而且容易扭曲语义。作者希望提出一种在特征层面进行防御的机制，既能够有效抵御越狱攻击，又不引入明显推理开销和语义损失，从而满足商业部署的需求。

Method: 首先通过实验系统分析干净样本与越狱样本在交叉注意力特征空间中的差异，首次发现两者在扩散过程中存在累积分离效应和线性可分性逐步增强的趋势。基于此，SafeCA提出：(1) 利用单次推理中干净提示词收集的交叉注意力特征进行注意力稳定性分析，识别关键防御区域和数值；(2) 通过带能量归一化的注意力掩码缓解异常激活，并引入轻量级语义空间适配器重定向异常语义流；(3) 通过将特征异常信号反向传播至输入提示词中的token，检测并抑制潜在恶意token，增强在商业模型中的可部署性。

Result: 实验结果表明，SafeCA在主流T2V模型上将越狱成功率降低约20%，推理开销几乎可以忽略（仅增加+0.1秒），同时保持良好的文本-视频语义一致性。

Conclusion: SafeCA为T2V生成模型提供了一种架构级、可部署的防御范式，在不牺牲推理效率和语义保真度的前提下，有效提升了模型对抗越狱攻击的安全性。

Abstract: Text-to-Video (T2V) generative models are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in real-world deployment, leading them to produce harmful or inappropriate content. Existing defense approaches mainly rely on input filtering or reconstruction, which not only incur high computational latency but also tend to distort semantics. To address these issues, we experimentally and systematically analyze the differences between clean and jailbreak samples in the cross-attention feature space, revealing for the first time a cumulative separation effect and a progressively increasing trend of linear separability between the two during the diffusion process. Based on this insight, we propose SafeCA, a feature-level defense mechanism for safe cross-attention localization and regularization. Firstly, we identify key defensive regions and values through attention stability analysis using cross-attention features collected from clean prompts within a single inference. Secondly, SafeCA mitigates anomalous activations via attention masking with energy normalization and introduces a lightweight semantic-space adapter to redirect abnormal semantic flows. Furthermore, we detect and suppress potentially malicious tokens by back-propagating feature anomaly signals to the input cue words, thereby enhancing the deployability of the defense in commercial models. Experimental results show that SafeCA reduces the jailbreak success rate by about 20% on mainstream T2V models, adds almost no inference overhead (+0.1s), and maintains good text-video semantic consistency. Overall, SafeCA provides an architecture-level, deployable protection paradigm for T2V generation models.

</details>


### [164] [GS-CPE: Unified 6-Degree-of-Freedom Camera Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10938)
*Huaiyuan Weng,Chul Min Yeum,Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-CPE是一个用于6自由度相机位姿估计的粗到精框架，融合了几何粗位姿估计与基于3D高斯泼溅的扭曲优化精化，在室内外基准上实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉定位方法（从场景坐标回归到直接相机位姿回归）虽然取得了显著进展，但在鲁棒泛化性和高精度之间难以兼得，需要一种既能保证泛化能力又能实现高精度的位姿估计方法。

Method: GS-CPE采用粗到精两阶段框架：首先通过检索引导的几何位姿估计在3DGS场景表示上获得粗略位姿；然后通过最小化具有可见性感知的掩蔽RGB扭曲目标函数，在多尺度优化框架中结合自适应重渲染进行位姿精化。

Result: 在7Scenes、Cambridge Landmarks、FAST-LIVO2数据集以及自定义数据集上的大量室内外实验表明，GS-CPE在精度和泛化性上均一致地超越现有方法，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: GS-CPE通过统一几何粗位姿估计与3DGS扭曲精化的粗到精框架，有效解决了视觉定位中泛化性与精度之间的矛盾，为相机位姿估计提供了新的强基线方法。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in visual localization, from scene coordinate regression to direct camera pose regression, achieving both robust generalization and high accuracy remain challenging. This study introduces GS-CPE (Gaussian Splatting based Camera Pose Estimation), a coarse-to-fine framework for 6-DoF camera pose estimation that unifies geometry-based coarse pose estimation with robust 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) warping based pose refinement. GS-CPE first estimates a coarse pose via retrieval-guided geometric pose estimation on a 3DGS scene representation, then refines it by minimizing a visibility aware masked RGB warping objective in a multi-scale optimization framework, with adaptive re-rendering. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks including 7Scenes, Cambridge Landmarks, FAST-LIVO2 datasets, and a custom dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming in both accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [165] [Mixture-of-Experts-based Entropy Model for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10947)
*Jonas Brenig,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将Mixture of Experts (MoE)方法引入学习型图像压缩领域，提出基于MoE的熵模型（MoEE），通过选择性激活输入图像所需的参数子集，在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了-16.85%的BD-Rate提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图像压缩近年来取得了显著进展，端到端学习模型已超越传统方法的压缩效率。然而，现有模型对所有输入图像都使用相同的参数进行编码，缺乏针对不同图像内容的自适应能力。作者受到MoE方法在NLP和计算机视觉任务中成功应用的启发，希望将MoE引入学习型图像压缩，使模型能够根据输入图像选择性激活参数，从而提高压缩效率。

Method: 提出MoE-based Entropy model（MoEE），将Mixture of Experts机制引入学习型图像压缩的熵模型部分。通过MoE结构，模型在推理时只激活与输入图像相关的那部分参数，实现条件计算（conditional computation），从而提高模型的表达能力和压缩效率。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上，该方法相比VVC（Versatile Video Coding，最新一代视频编码标准）实现了-16.85%的BD-Rate改进，表明该模型在压缩效率上显著超越了当前最先进的传统编码方法。

Conclusion: MoE方法在学习型图像压缩中具有有效性，通过选择性激活参数子集的熵模型设计，可以在不增加全部参数计算开销的情况下显著提升压缩率，为学习型图像压缩领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Learned image compression has seen significant progress in recent years with the development of end-to-end learned models that achieve better compression efficiency than state-of-the-art conventional methods. Recently, Mixture of Experts (MoE) approaches have seen promising results in NLP and computer vision tasks. In this paper, we introduce the MoE approach to learned image compression. We propose a MoE-based Entropy model (MoEE) for learned image compression, allowing the model to selectively activate only the subset of parameters required for the input image. Our model achieves a BD-Rate improvement over VVC of -16.85% on the Kodak dataset.

</details>


### [166] [StreamFlow: Dynamic Memory Flows for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10949)
*Muxin Fu,Yifan Zhang,Wentao Zhang,Fangming Guo,Qian Chen,Guibin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamFlow 是一个高效的视觉记忆框架，通过结合动态感知的中期记忆（过滤时间冗余）和潜在长期记忆（将历史视频内容整合为视觉潜在表示），并利用注意力引导的检索机制，在StreamingBench上达到67.73%的整体准确率，同时将视觉注意力得分提升59.1%，端到端延迟和峰值内存分别降低50.4%和21.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法存在两大局限：基于模型的方法需要侵入式骨干网络更新，而基于记忆的方法在视觉编码上浪费大量计算于时间冗余内容，且对视觉历史访问方式固定僵化，缺乏动态、按需访问历史视觉信息的能力。

Method: StreamFlow包含三个核心组件：1) 轻量级、动态感知的中期记忆，在视觉编码前过滤时间冗余；2) 潜在长期记忆，将历史视频内容整合为视觉潜在表示供后续推理使用；3) 注意力引导的检索机制，在生成过程中当模型对视觉证据的依赖减弱时，动态注入相关的视觉潜在表示。

Result: 在StreamingBench上达到67.73%的整体准确率，取得最佳性能；在离线长视频基准上也表现优异。相较原设定，视觉注意力得分（VAS）提升59.1%，端到端延迟降低50.4%，峰值内存降低21.1%。

Conclusion: StreamFlow通过设计两级记忆架构与注意力引导的检索机制，成功实现了流式视频理解中高效、动态、按需的视觉历史访问，兼顾理解性能与计算效率，使推理更加视觉锚定且高效。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to preserve relevant evidence from continuously evolving streams under strict causality and bounded memory. Yet existing paradigms remain limited: model-based methods require intrusive backbone updates, while memory-based methods expend substantial visual-encoding computation on temporally redundant content and rely on rigid access to visual history. To address these limitations, we introduce StreamFlow, an efficient visual memory framework that enables dynamic, on-demand access to historical visual information. StreamFlow combines a lightweight, dynamics-aware mid-term memory that filters temporal redundancy before visual encoding with a latent long-term memory that consolidates historical video content into visual latents accessible to subsequent reasoning. During generation, an attention-guided retrieval mechanism injects relevant visual latents when the model's reliance on visual evidence weakens. StreamFlow achieves state-of-the-art streaming video understanding performance, reaching 67.73% overall accuracy on StreamingBench, while also delivering strong performance on offline long-video benchmarks. Relative to the vanilla setting, it improves the visual attention score (VAS) by 59.1% while reducing end-to-end latency and peak memory by 50.4% and 21.1%, respectively, enabling more visually grounded and efficient reasoning.

</details>


### [167] [Multiple Scale Latents for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10952)
*Jonas Brenig,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层潜变量表示的图像压缩方法，通过在不同空间尺度上使用多个潜变量并各自配备独立的熵模型，更高效地捕捉图像的空间结构，相比VVC在Kodak上实现了17.9%的BD-rate降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型图像压缩系统通常仅依赖单一潜变量表示并配以超先验，这限制了其跨空间尺度高效捕捉图像结构的能力，因此需要更灵活的多尺度表示来提升熵模型的效率。

Method: 提出分层潜变量表示方法，在不同空间尺度上使用多个潜变量，每个潜变量配备独立的熵模型，从而更精细地建模不同尺度上的空间结构信息。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了17.9%的BD-rate降低，验证了多尺度潜变量表示的有效性，且该方法与已有的学习型图像压缩进展正交，可作为通用模块集成到现有方法中。

Conclusion: 多尺度分层潜变量表示能够显著提升学习型图像压缩的率失真性能，并且具有很好的通用性和可扩展性，可作为一种灵活的补充模块应用于现有压缩框架。

Abstract: Most learned image compression systems rely on a single latent representation combined with a hyperprior, which limits their ability to efficiently capture image structure across spatial scales. In this work, we propose a hierarchical latent representation to improve the efficiency of the entropy model. By using multiple latents at different scales, each with its own entropy model, we better capture the spatial structure of the latent representation. Our experiments show that this approach achieves a 17.9% BD-rate reduction over VVC on Kodak, demonstrating the effectiveness of multi-scale latent representations. Furthermore, the approach is orthogonal to other advances in learned image compression, making it a versatile addition to existing methods.

</details>


### [168] [Evidence-Grounded Trustworthy Multimodal Reasoning and Evaluation Benchmark in Complex Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10954)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Bowen Jiang,Xumeng Han,Jiashu Li,Xuehui Yu,Yuling Liu,Guorong Li,Zhenjun Han,Jianbin Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AD2-Bench基准和EGVOR方法，针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在复杂恶劣场景下认知可靠性下降的问题。AD2-Bench通过层次化视觉诊断框架将推理分解为证据链（CoE），揭示推理失败源于空间模糊性和语义不确定性两类证据缺陷。基于此，EGVOR用显式的证据原子（结构化空间-语义三元组）替代隐式推理，并通过从反思性监督构造到强化学习的分层课程训练提升推理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在良性场景表现优异，但在复杂恶劣条件下认知可靠性显著下降，模型常在没有充分视觉证据的情况下进行隐式推理，导致感知与推理脱节。同时，现有的结果导向基准只评估最终预测，无法诊断底层推理过程的失败原因。

Method: 提出AD2-Bench，引入层次化视觉诊断框架，将推理分解为结构化的证据链（CoE）；从概率视角分析推理失败的两大主因：空间模糊性（目标与背景混淆导致定位错误）和语义不确定性（退化视觉特征导致理解错误）；在此基础上提出EGVOR，用显式的证据原子（空间-语义三元组）约束定位与语义理解的紧密对齐，并通过分层课程训练（从反思性监督构造到强化学习，显式奖励减少推理方差）进行优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，EGVOR在恶劣条件下显著提升了推理稳定性，为可信的多模态认知提供了更鲁棒的框架。同时，细粒度诊断证实了鲁棒多模态推理本质上依赖于准确的证据获取。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的多模态推理必须建立在准确的证据获取之上，通过显式结构化的证据生成和分层的训练策略，可以有效弥合感知与推理之间的鸿沟，提升MLLMs在复杂恶劣场景下的认知可靠性和可信度。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance in benign scenarios, their cognitive reliability deteriorates significantly in complex scenes under adverse conditions. In these settings, models often rely on implicit inference without sufficient visual evidence, leading to a disconnect between perception and reasoning. Meanwhile, existing outcome-oriented benchmarks evaluate only final predictions and fail to diagnose failures in the underlying reasoning process. To address this gap, the authors propose AD2-Bench, which introduces a Hierarchical Visual Diagnosis framework that decomposes reasoning into a structured Chain of Evidence (CoE). This fine-grained diagnosis reveals that robust multimodal reasoning fundamentally depends on accurate evidence acquisition. Building on this perspective, the authors formulate reasoning from a probabilistic viewpoint and identify two primary causes of reasoning failure: Spatial Ambiguity, where models fail to distinguish target objects from background clutter, resulting in localization errors; and Semantic Uncertainty, where degraded visual features lead to incorrect semantic interpretation, resulting in understanding errors. To overcome these evidence deficiencies, they further propose Evidence-grounded Visual Reasoning (EGVOR), which replaces implicit reasoning with the explicit generation of Evidence Atoms - structured spatial-semantic triplets that enforce tight alignment between localization and semantic understanding. The model is trained through a hierarchical curriculum that progresses from reflective supervision construction to reinforcement learning, where reducing reasoning variance is explicitly rewarded. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EGVOR substantially improves reasoning stability under adverse conditions, providing a more robust framework for trustworthy multimodal cognition.

</details>


### [169] [CARE: Confidence-Aware Reasoning for Reliable Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10964)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,Zhenyuan Chen,Luyuan Chen,Rongyu Zhang,Jinjian Zhang,Wei Zhou,Zhijie Xu,Ming Kong,Zhan Zhou,Jie Liu,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE框架，一个两阶段的置信度感知医学推理框架，通过Medical-CoT数据合成与GRPO优化，联合优化医学多模态大语言模型在视觉问答中的诊断准确率和校准能力，在三个医学VQA基准上同时取得最高准确率、最低期望校准误差和最低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学MLLMs通过RFT能够生成CoT推理，但存在置信度校准偏差——模型表达的不确定性与其实际诊断准确率之间的系统性差距，这一问题会削弱临床场景中的信任度。现有方法未能在优化准确性的同时保证校准可靠性。

Method: CARE采用双阶段流水线：(1) 第一阶段通过可扩展的Medical-CoT合成，为监督微调提供结构化的冷启动训练数据；(2) 第二阶段使用GRPO强化学习，并引入新颖的Confidence-Aware Reward (CAR)机制，将模型的置信度与诊断正确性直接绑定到奖励信号中，从而联合优化准确率与校准。

Result: 在三个医学VQA基准上，CARE取得了最高的诊断准确率，同时获得了最低的期望校准误差（ECE）和最低的幻觉率，证明了该框架在医学多模态推理中的有效性与可靠性。

Conclusion: CARE表明将置信度感知机制融入强化学习奖励设计可以有效缓解医学MLLMs的置信度校准问题，为可信赖的临床决策支持系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) has enabled medical Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning for visual question answering, yet these models suffer from $\textit{confidence miscalibration}$---a systematic gap between expressed certainty and actual diagnostic accuracy that undermines clinical trust. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$, a $\textbf{C}$onfidence-$\textbf{A}$ware medical $\textbf{RE}$asoning framework that jointly optimizes accuracy and calibration through a dual-stage pipeline. First, a scalable Medical-CoT synthesis provides structured cold-start data for Supervised Fine-Tuning. Second, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel $\textbf{Confidence-Aware Reward (CAR)}$ mechanism ties the model's confidence to diagnostic correctness within the reward signal. Across three Medical VQA benchmarks, $\textbf{CARE}$ achieves the highest diagnostic accuracy while obtaining the lowest Expected Calibration Error and Hallucination Rate, establishing a foundation for trustworthy clinical decision support. Our code is available at https://github.com/anotherbricki/CARE.

</details>


### [170] [A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Music Recognition of String Quartet Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10978)
*Dongmin Kim,Brian Liu,Jose J. Valero-Mas,Dasaem Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OSSQ-OMR，这是首个面向多声部乐谱识别（OMR）的公开数据集，基于OpenScore String Quartet语料库构建，包含24,544张系统级图像和98,172张小节级图像，配以LMXE、**kern和ABC三种编码格式的转录，并提供了基准协议和两个代表性OMR模型的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMR在单声部与钢琴形式乐谱上已取得显著进展，但多声部乐谱转录仍未被充分探索，主要原因在于缺乏合适的数据集。现有资源无法支撑多声部OMR的研究与评估，因此亟需构建首个多声部OMR专用数据集以填补这一空白。

Method: 基于OpenScore String Quartet语料库，将数字编码乐谱与来自IMSLP的原始扫描版本配对，并对所有图像与转录进行视觉对齐。数据集提供系统级和声部级（staff-level）乐谱图像，以及三种编码格式（LMXE、**kern、ABC）的配对转录。同时制定基准协议，在四个随机乐谱级划分（测试集互斥）下评估两个代表性OMR模型的性能。

Result: OSSQ-OMR包含116部弦乐四重奏谱、24,544张系统图像和98,172张声部图像。基线模型在合成输入上OMR-NED最低达3.6%，在扫描输入上最低达5.9%。结果揭示了编码与切分选择的显著影响，其中基于LSTM的基线模型在扫描输入上的性能退化幅度约为Transformer基线模型的1/2.6（即退化约2.6倍小于后者）。

Conclusion: OSSQ-OMR填补了多声部OMR数据集的空白，为多声部乐谱识别提供了标准化评估基准。实验表明编码格式与分割策略对模型性能影响显著，且LSTM模型在扫描真实场景下的鲁棒性优于Transformer模型，为未来多声部OMR研究提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Optical music recognition (OMR) transcribes music scores into digital formats. While the field has advanced significantly on monophonic and piano-form scores, multi-part score transcription remains underexplored, largely due to the absence of a suitable dataset. We introduce OpenScore String Quartet for Optical Music Recognition (OSSQ-OMR), the first dataset dedicated to multi-part OMR. Built on the OpenScore String Quartet corpus, OSSQ-OMR pairs digitally encoded scores with their original scanned editions from IMSLP, with all images visually aligned to their transcriptions. The dataset is released with score images at system and staff levels, and paired transcriptions in three encoding formats: Extended Linearized MusicXML (LMXE), **kern, and ABC. In total, OSSQ-OMR contains 24,544 system images and 98,172 staff images drawn from 116 string quartet scores. We accompany the dataset with a benchmark protocol and baseline results from two representative OMR models, evaluated across four random score-level splits with mutually exclusive test sets. Baselines reach OMR-NED as low as 3.6% on synthetic and 5.9% on scanned inputs; results reveal substantial effects of encoding and segmentation choices, with the LSTM-based baseline degrading on scanned inputs roughly 2.6 times less than the Transformer-based baseline.

</details>


### [171] [ThinkAfford: Affordance-Centric Reasoning for Fine-Grained 3D Grounding in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10981)
*Xinrui Lin,Sha Zhang,Shumin Wang,Zenghuan Zhu,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkAfford将3D功能区域定位任务解耦为高召回率的候选提案生成与指令驱动的推理两个阶段，通过可学习的affordance提示生成细粒度提案，并用视觉提示推理模块结合GRPO策略优化实现精确的指令接地3D定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务驱动的3D功能区域定位方法存在两个交织的失败模式：预测或选择的区域可能遗漏目标交互区域或粒度不合适，且语言接地在关系型指令下可能混淆视觉上相似的替代区域。需要解耦生成与推理以分别解决召回率和选择性精度问题。

Method: ThinkAfford包含两个模块：(1) Affordance Proposal Generation (APG)使用可学习的affordance提示和多层级视觉特征预测交互条件热力图，提取可变数量的细粒度提案，无需解析对象或部件名称作为分割提示；(2) Visual-Prompted Affordance Reasoning (VPAR)基于标注的提案叠加图配合完整指令进行推理，以think-then-answer的结构化响应返回标识符。此外采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)使用从3D重叠提升得到的提案级奖励来对齐VPAR的选择与最终3D接地结果。

Result: 在SceneFun3D验证集上，ThinkAfford在官方评估器下达到10.69% AP50和25.46% AP25，优于可比较的3D开放词汇基线以及基于视觉语言模型的2D到3D基线。模块级诊断显示APG在25% IoU下达到77.5%召回率，GRPO训练的VPAR在APG覆盖的查询上达到72.1%的选择准确率，而监督微调仅为63.4%。

Conclusion: 解耦提案生成与指令推理的策略能有效缓解现有方法在3D功能区域定位中的两个失败模式。GRPO策略优化相比监督微调显著提升了语言推理模块的选择准确率，验证了提案级奖励对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Task-driven 3D affordance grounding aims to localize the functional region in a cluttered 3D scene that enables an action specified by a natural-language instruction. Existing methods either predict 3D masks directly or construct them by selecting and fusing intermediate 2D/3D regions. However, they remain vulnerable to two intertwined failure modes: the predicted or selected regions may miss the target interaction area or have unsuitable granularity, while language grounding may confuse visually similar alternatives under relational instructions. To this end, we introduce ThinkAfford, which decouples high-recall affordance proposal generation from instruction-grounded reasoning. Specifically, the Affordance Proposal Generation module first uses learnable affordance prompts and multi-level visual features to predict interaction-conditioned heatmaps, extracting a variable number of fine-grained proposals without parsed object or part names as segmentation prompts. Visual-Prompted Affordance Reasoning then reasons over labeled proposal overlays using the full instruction, returning identifiers in a structured "think-then-answer" response. Moreover, Group Relative Policy Optimization uses proposal-level rewards from lifted 3D overlap to align VPAR selection with final 3D grounding. On the SceneFun3D validation split, ThinkAfford achieves 10.69% AP50 and 25.46% AP25 under the official evaluator, outperforming comparable 3D open-vocabulary and vision-language-model-based 2D-to-3D baselines. Module-level diagnostics further show that APG attains 77.5% recall at 25% intersection-over-union, while GRPO-trained VPAR achieves 72.1% selection accuracy on APG-covered queries, compared with 63.4% under supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [172] [PEAK: Precise and Persistent Concept Erasure via k-Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10985)
*Man Jiang,Ouxiang Li,Weibao Xue,Zhenhua Tang,Yuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PEAK提出了一种基于k-Sparse自编码器(kSAE)的精准且持久的文本到图像扩散模型概念擦除框架。通过训练kSAE分解内部激活为可解释的稀疏特征，识别目标特定特征进行参数优化，将概念擦除嵌入模型参数中，实现无需推理时干预的持久擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法难以同时实现精准性和持久性：不准确的表示定位会导致语义干扰，而概念知识的未完全移除使得对抗性恢复成为可能。

Method: PEAK首先在扩散去噪网络的内部激活上训练kSAE，将密集表示分解为可解释的稀疏特征；通过对比目标和非目标提示诱导的稀疏激活，基于激活强度和频率识别紧凑的目标特定特征集；随后利用这些特征进行参数优化，选择性抑制目标相关激活同时保留互补的非目标激活，将概念擦除直接嵌入扩散参数中。

Result: 实验表明PEAK实现了有效且鲁棒的概念擦除。在I2P基准上，NudeNet检测从582降至6，平均攻击成功率(ASR)从96.52%降至5.63%；在MS-COCO上保持了近零KID的生成质量。

Conclusion: PEAK通过kSAE的稀疏特征定位与特征引导的参数优化，解决了概念擦除中精准性与持久性的矛盾，实现了无需推理时干预的高质量概念擦除。

Abstract: Erasing concepts from large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models has become increasingly crucial due to the growing concerns over copyright infringement, privacy violations, and offensive content. Existing approaches struggle to achieve both precise and persistent concept erasure: inaccurate localization of concept-related representations may cause unintended semantic interference, while incomplete removal of the underlying concept knowledge allows adversarial recovery. To address this dilemma, we propose PEAK, a \textbf{\textit{precise}} and \textbf{\textit{persistent}} concept erasure framework via k-Sparse Autoencoders (kSAEs). PEAK first trains a kSAE on internal activations of the diffusion denoising network to decompose dense representations into interpretable sparse features. By contrasting sparse activations induced by target and non-target prompts, PEAK identifies a compact set of target-specific features according to both activation strength and frequency. These localized features are then used for parameter optimization, where PEAK selectively suppresses target-related activations while preserving complementary non-target ones towards the original model. This feature-guided optimization embeds concept erasure directly into diffusion parameters, eliminating the need for additional inference-time intervention and facilitating effective persistence against adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PEAK achieves effective and robust concept erasure. On the I2P benchmark, PEAK reduces NudeNet detections from 582 to 6, lowers the average attack success rate (ASR) from 96.52\% to 5.63\%, and preserves general generation quality on MS-COCO with a near-zero KID. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/manmanTAT/PEAK

</details>


### [173] [Putting Registers to Work: Task Registers for Token Pruning in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10989)
*Hongsen Cao,Mona Jaber,Shanxin Yuan,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控探针实验系统研究了预训练Vision Transformer的token裁剪策略在不同任务（图像分类、语义分割、目标检测）间的迁移性，发现不同任务对裁剪标准、层位置敏感性和恢复端点有不同偏好。基于此提出Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP)，通过每任务一个可学习的任务寄存器来动态排序token、分配删除预算并设置密集特征恢复尺度，在保持ImageNet-1K竞争力的同时，在ADE20K和COCO上分别以1.30×和1.32×的编码器吞吐量达到47.0 mIoU和53.7 box AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token裁剪策略通常针对单一识别管线设计，但预训练的Vision Transformer会被复用到具有不同空间需求的任务中。作者希望回答：一个裁剪策略的哪些部分可以在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测之间迁移？这促使他们通过受控探针实验揭示各任务在裁剪偏好上的差异，进而驱动任务自适应的裁剪方案设计。

Method: 首先设计受控探针实验：冻结无裁剪的checkpoint，在单层应用一系列无参数化的裁剪准则且不进行重训练，以独立揭示三个差异：(1)分割和检测对裁剪准则的排序不同；(2)分类对最浅层的注意力裁剪尤为敏感；(3)密集任务偏好相反的恢复端点。基于这些探针发现，提出Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP)：引入每任务一个的任务寄存器token（区别于现有任务无关的register token），激活当前任务对应的寄存器，其演化状态用于三层决策——对token排序、在深度上分配精确的删除预算、为密集特征设置恢复尺度。联合适配版本TAP-J在最终保留率ρ=0.5下评估。

Result: 在最终保留率ρ=0.5时，联合适配的TAP-J在ADE20K上达到47.0 mIoU和1.30×编码器吞吐量，在COCO上达到53.7 box AP和1.32×编码器吞吐量，同时在ImageNet-1K上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: token裁剪策略的核心组件（准则排序、层敏感性、恢复端点）在不同视觉任务间并不通用，任务自适应设计（如TAP的每任务寄存器）是跨任务高效部署预训练ViT的关键。TAP通过单个可学习的任务寄存器统一了排序、预算分配和恢复三方面决策，实现在多个密集任务上显著的加速与可竞争的精度。

Abstract: Token-pruning policies are usually designed for a single recognition pipeline, but pretrained Vision Transformers are reused across tasks with different spatial demands. We ask which parts of a pruning policy transfer across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection. For each pipeline, controlled probes freeze the no-pruning checkpoint and apply a series of parameter-free reduction criteria at one eligible layer at a time without retraining. The probes reveal three differences: segmentation and detection rank the criteria differently, classification is especially sensitive to attention-based pruning in the earliest layers, and the dense tasks prefer opposite recovery endpoints. These findings motivate Task-Adaptive Pruning (TAP). Existing register tokens serve as task-agnostic storage for feature artifacts. TAP instead introduces one task register per task and activates only the current one. Its evolving state ranks tokens, distributes an exact removal budget over depth, and sets the recovery scale for dense features. At a final keep rate of $ρ=0.5$, our jointly adapted model, TAP-J, reaches $47.0$ mIoU at $1.30\times$ encoder throughput on ADE20K and $53.7$ box AP at $1.32\times$ encoder throughput on COCO while remaining competitive on ImageNet-1K.

</details>


### [174] [HNDiff: Haze-Noise Diffusion for Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10995)
*Jin-Ting He,Fu-Jen Tsai,Yan-Tsung Peng,Min-Hung Chen,Chia-Wen Lin,Yen-Yu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HNDiff，一种将大气散射模型作为归纳偏置嵌入扩散框架的图像去雾方法，通过联合雾-噪声扩散和雾感知噪声调度器，使去雾过程与雾的物理形成机制对齐，并进一步提出Latent HNDiff以集成到现有去雾网络中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像去雾方法通常忽略雾的物理形成机制，直接从纯高斯噪声重建干净图像，限制了其复原潜力。

Method: 1) 将大气散射模型作为归纳偏置嵌入扩散框架，使复原过程与雾的物理形成机制对齐；2) 正向过程中引入联合雾-噪声扩散与雾感知噪声调度器，根据雾浓度自适应调整噪声注入强度（浓雾区域注入更强噪声促进内容生成，清晰区域注入更轻噪声保留细节）；3) 反向过程中推导物理一致的去雾-去噪过程，同时去除雾和噪声；4) 提出Latent HNDiff，编译干净潜在先验并集成到现有去雾网络中提升性能。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著改进了主流去雾骨干网络，在基准数据集上取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 通过将物理先验融入扩散模型，HNDiff能够更准确地建模雾的形成过程，提升去雾效果，并具有良好的实用性与迁移能力。

Abstract: Existing diffusion-based methods have recently made significant progress in image dehazing. However, they typically neglect the physics of haze formation and reconstruct clean images from pure Gaussian noise, thereby limiting their restoration potential. To address this issue, we propose Haze-Noise Diffusion (HNDiff), a novel diffusion framework that embeds the atmospheric scattering model as an inductive bias. By grounding diffusion in physical principles, HNDiff ensures that the restoration aligns more closely with underlying mechanisms of haze formation. In its forward process, we introduce joint haze-noise diffusion with a haze-aware noise scheduler, which progressively adds both haze and noise to an image. Essentially, the scheduler adapts noise levels according to haze density, meaning that regions with heavier haze receive stronger noise injection to encourage content generation, while clearer regions receive lighter noise to better preserve details, which directly links the forward degradation process with the physics of haze. In the reverse process, we then derive a physically consistent dehazing-denoising process that simultaneously removes haze and noise to restore a clean image in a manner aligned with the forward degradation process. To further enhance practicality, we propose Latent HNDiff, which compiles clean latent priors that can be seamlessly integrated into existing dehazing networks to boost performance. Extensive experiments show that our work significantly improves leading dehazing backbones and achieves state-of-the-art results on benchmark datasets. The project page is available at https://jin-ting-he.github.io/HNDiff .

</details>


### [175] [Watching Synthetic Videos: Aligning Cross-modal Representations with Visual Synthesis for Zero-shot Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11013)
*Liangyu Fu,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Ya Jing,Xuecheng Wu,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WSV框架，通过两阶段训练实现零样本视频字幕生成：先利用预训练文生视频模型生成合成视频潜在表征，再用polisher弥合真实与合成视频分布差异，最后通过prompter将潜在表征条件化到GPT-2生成字幕。在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX数据集上B@4和CIDEr分别达到52和95.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视频字幕生成中，纯文本训练导致训练（文本）与推理（视频）之间存在跨模态鸿沟。以往方法仅通过简单线性变换来桥接这一鸿沟，但文本与视频之间固有的模态差异使得跨模态表示空间对齐不足，导致生成的字幕不准确。

Method: 提出WSV框架，包含两个训练阶段：(1) 第一阶段，通过预训练的文生视频模型生成对应的合成视频潜在表征，并提出一个polisher来缩小真实与合成视频分布之间的差距，增强潜在表征的真实性；(2) 第二阶段，设计一个prompter，将经polisher处理的潜在表征作为条件输入到GPT-2中以生成字幕。推理时，输入视频由预训练的3D因果VAE编码后直接送入prompter，引导GPT-2生成最终字幕。

Result: 在MSVD、MSR-VTT和VATEX三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在B@4和CIDEr指标上分别取得了52和95.7的成绩。

Conclusion: 通过引入文生视频模型生成合成潜在表征并配合polisher弥合分布差距，有效解决了纯文本训练范式下零样本视频字幕生成的跨模态对齐不足问题，取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Text-only training is a popular paradigm in zero-shot video captioning, where the video distribution is not available to the model during training, leading to a cross-modal gap between the training (text-only) and the inference (video-only). Previous works attempt to bridge the gap through simple linear transformations. However, the inherent gap between text and video makes cross-modal representation space alignment insufficient, resulting in inaccurate sentences. To address this issue, we propose a novel zero-shot video captioning framework (WSV) consisting of two training stages, which first generates corresponding synthetic video latent representations via a pretrained text-to-video generation model. To strengthen the fidelity of the latent representations, we propose a polisher capable of bridging the gap between real and synthetic video distributions. Subsequently, we design a prompter that conditions GPT-2 on the polished latent representations to generate the captions in the second training stage. During inference, an input video is encoded by a pretrained 3D Causal VAE and then fed directly into the prompter, which in turn guides GPT-2 to produce the final caption. Experimental results conducted on MSVD, MSR-VTT, and VATEX datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves scores of 52 and 95.7 on the B@4 and CIDEr metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [R4DSG: Relative 4D Scene Graph Memory for Object-Centric Question Answering in Long Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11017)
*Ke Ma,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Shuai Tan,Yijie Zhong,Hao Chen,Haofen Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R4DSG提出了一种面向长时程第一人称视频的相对4D场景图记忆方法，将视频转化为以时间、地点、持久化对象、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文索引的紧凑可查询记忆条目，无需全局对齐的世界模型或强几何假设（如点云、RGB-D、位姿等）。在EgoLifeQA的255个对象相关问题子集上，仅用问题检索即比EgoRAG-Text整体提升6.7分、在when类问题上提升12.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程第一人称视频是可穿戴AI助手的重要数据来源，但现有的字幕和转录记忆难以保持持久对象身份和结构化空间变化，无法回答"物品被移到哪里""何时最后改变状态""为什么被重新放置"等对象中心问题。现有长视频QA方法侧重时序定位和片段检索，而3D场景图方法又要求比自由运动可穿戴RGB视频更强的几何输入（点云、RGB-D、位姿视图、稀疏重建等），两者都无法满足实际需求。

Method: R4DSG引入相对4D场景图记忆：将视频转换为由时间、地点、持久对象、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文索引的紧凑可查询条目。核心思想是区分稳定锚点与动态对象，跨帧维持持久对象身份，并用锚点相对变换而非全局对齐世界模型来表达对象状态。系统构建于RGB-only的技术之上，包括可提示视频分割、时间传播和相对3D提升。

Result: 在EgoLifeQA中255个对象相关问题的子集上进行评估，在仅问题检索（question-only retrieval）设置下，整体性能比EgoRAG-Text提升6.7个百分点，在when类问题上提升12.5个百分点，验证了时间化组织的对象记忆的实用价值。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，相对4D场景图可作为可穿戴助手、AR系统和具身多模态智能体的实用记忆基础架构，为长时程第一人称视频下的对象中心问答提供了无需强几何先验的可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon egocentric video is a rich substrate for wearable AI assistants, but object-centric questions such as where an item was moved, when it last changed state, or why it was relocated remain difficult because caption- and transcript-based memories rarely preserve persistent object identity or structured spatial change. Existing long-video QA methods mainly emphasize temporal grounding and clip retrieval, while prior 3D scene-graph methods typically assume stronger geometry than free-motion wearable RGB video provides, including point clouds, RGB-D input, posed views, sparse reconstruction, or reconstructed scenes. R4DSG introduces a relative 4D scene graph memory for long egocentric video. Instead of storing raw graph sequences, R4DSG converts video into compact queryable memory entries indexed by time, place, persistent objects, anchor-relative change, and local interaction context. The main idea is to separate stable anchors from dynamic objects, maintain persistent object identity across frames, and represent object state through anchor-relative transitions rather than a globally aligned world model. Built on recent RGB-only advances in promptable video segmentation, temporal propagation, and relative 3D lifting, the method produces a retrieval-ready memory directly usable for long-horizon question answering. Evaluation on a 255-question object-related subset from EgoLifeQA shows, under question-only retrieval, a 6.7-point overall gain over EgoRAG-Text and a 12.5-point gain on when questions, which highlights the value of temporally organized object memory. These results position relative 4D scene graphs as a practical memory substrate for wearable assistants, AR systems, and embodied multimedia agents. GitHub Page: https://dualtransparency.github.io/R4DSG/.

</details>


### [177] [When Visual Signals Mislead: A Mechanistic Study of Attribute Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11024)
*Yufei Zhang,Chenlu Zhan,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISOR框架，通过基于零图像的诊断（VSNR）将VLM的预测分解为视觉信号和语言先验信号，发现属性幻觉主要由视觉信号驱动而非语言先验主导，并据此将失败模式分类后路由到校准、弃权或视觉适配等针对性修复操作，在三个VLM家族上有效减少属性假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性幻觉（模型识别出物体但错误描述其属性）在视觉语言模型（VLM）中普遍存在，但机制上理解不足。主流解释——语言先验主导——虽催生了抑制先验的方法，但从未在属性层面被直接验证。作者希望直接检验这一假设，并开发基于真实机制的修复方案。

Method: 提出VISOR框架，包含两部分：(1) VSNR诊断方法：利用零图像（null-image）对比，将每个预测分解为视觉logit信号和语言先验信号；(2) 路由式修复：基于诊断结果将失败划分两类——颜色/状态属性中的低margin但方向正确的视觉信号，以及材料属性中的低信噪比或错位视觉信号——并分别路由到三个算子：校准（处理阈值放置错误）、弃权（训练自由的低信噪比处理）、针对性视觉适配（修复材料属性失败，这是先验抑制无法解决的）。在10,791个负真值样本、三个VLM家族（Qwen、InternVL、LLaVA）和三种属性类型上验证。

Result: 实验表明视觉信号强烈预测假阳性，而语言先验信号接近随机水平，直接挑战了语言先验主导假说。VISOR在不同模型和属性类型上均减少了属性假阳性，且不需要依赖先验主导假设。

Conclusion: 属性幻觉的主要机制并非语言先验主导，而是视觉信号本身的缺陷（margin不足、信噪比低或错位）。基于诊断的路由修复比统一样式的先验抑制更有效，说明属性幻觉修复需要针对性的、以实际失败机制为导向的方法。

Abstract: Attribute hallucination---where vision-language models (VLMs) correctly identify an object but mischaracterize its properties---is prevalent yet mechanistically poorly understood. The dominant explanation, language-prior dominance, has motivated prior-suppression methods, but this explanation has not been directly tested at the attribute level. We present VISOR (Visual-Operational Remediation), a unified framework that couples null-image-based diagnosis with routed remediation. Its VSNR diagnostic decomposes each prediction into a visual logit signal and a language-prior signal. Across 10,791 negative-ground-truth samples from three VLM families and three attribute types, the visual signal strongly predicts false positives, whereas the language-prior signal is near chance. VISOR uses this diagnosis to separate two failure modes: low-margin but directionally correct visual signals in color/state attributes, and low-SNR or misaligned visual signals in material attributes. The same diagnosis routes each query to the appropriate operator: calibration for threshold-placement errors, abstention for training-free low-SNR handling, or targeted visual adaptation for material failures that prior suppression cannot correct. Across Qwen, InternVL, and LLaVA, VISOR reduces attribute false positives without relying on the prior-dominance assumption.

</details>


### [178] [Multi-Level Evidence Aggregation for Robust Facial Phenotype Retrieval in Rare Genetic Disorder Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11037)
*Alexander Hustinx,Carolin Kaffiné,Behnam Javanmardi,Tzung-Chien Hsieh,Peter Krawitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时的多层级证据聚合框架，在不修改底层GestaltMatcher-Arc编码器的情况下，通过聚合同一患者的多个面部图像、构建患者加权的疾病质心、以及混合个体-质心评分策略，显著提升了罕见遗传病面部表型检索的准确率（GMDB-Freq上top-1从38.52%提升到48.82%，GMDB-Rare上从19.38%提升到23.79%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GestaltMatcher检索框架采用逐点式（pointwise）比较方式，将每个测试图像与数据库中单个图库图像进行一一比较。这种公式化方法未能充分利用可用的证据信息，因为同一患者可能有多个面部图像，同一疾病也可能由多个已确诊的图库患者代表。这种信息利用不充分限制了罕见遗传病优先排序的性能。

Method: 提出一个推理时的多层级证据聚合框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 嵌入层面的患者聚合——对同一患者的多个图像进行特征聚合；(2) 患者加权的疾病质心——构建疾病级别的代表性特征；(3) 混合个体-质心评分——综合测试患者观测、疾病级别图库证据和局部最近邻证据进行最终排序。该框架无需重新训练编码器，是纯推理时的改进方案。

Result: 在GMDB v1.1.4上，基于统一图库（同时包含训练时的已知疾病和未见疾病）进行评估。多层级证据聚合在所有评估子集上均提升了平均每个疾病的top-N检索准确率。具体而言：GMDB-Freq的top-1准确率从38.52%提升到48.82%；GMDB-Rare从19.38%提升到23.79%；多图像子集上，GMDB-Multi-Freq从46.12%提升到60.94%，GMDB-Multi-Rare从18.54%提升到26.71%。

Conclusion: 推理时的证据聚合可以在不重新训练编码器的情况下显著提升下一代面部表型检索性能，支持从孤立的单图像匹配范式向多层级聚合患者与疾病证据的范式转变，为罕见遗传病优先排序提供更优解决方案。

Abstract: AI-assisted facial phenotyping supports rare genetic disorder prioritization by retrieving visually similar diagnosed cases from facial image reference databases such as the GestaltMatcher Database (GMDB). Existing GestaltMatcher-based retrieval frameworks compare each test image with individual gallery images in a facial phenotype embedding space. However, this pointwise formulation does not fully exploit available evidence, because patients may have multiple images and disorders may be represented by multiple diagnosed gallery patients.
  We propose an inference-time multi-level evidence aggregation framework that improves facial phenotype retrieval without modifying the underlying GestaltMatcher-Arc encoder. The framework combines embedding-level patient aggregation of multiple images from the same individual, patient-weighted disorder centroids, and hybrid individual-centroid scoring to integrate test-patient observations, disorder-level gallery evidence, and local nearest-neighbor evidence.
  We evaluated the approach on GMDB v1.1.4 across disorders represented during training (GMDB-Freq), unseen disorders (GMDB-Rare), and multi-image patient subsets, using a unified gallery containing both GMDB-Freq and GMDB-Rare disorders. Multi-level evidence aggregation improved mean per-disorder top-$N$ retrieval accuracy across all evaluation subsets. Top-1 accuracy increased from 38.52% to 48.82% on GMDB-Freq and from 19.38% to 23.79% on GMDB-Rare. On multi-image subsets, top-1 accuracy increased from 46.12% to 60.94% on GMDB-Multi-Freq and from 18.54% to 26.71% on GMDB-Multi-Rare.
  These findings show that inference-time aggregation can improve next-generation facial phenotype retrieval without retraining the encoder, supporting a shift from isolated single-image matching toward multi-level aggregation of patient and disorder evidence for rare-disorder prioritization.

</details>


### [179] [3D Weighted Geometric Graph Neural Networks for Sheep Facial Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11050)
*Alam Noor,Luis Almeida,Mohamed Daoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-SPFES系统，一种基于单目深度感知的几何图神经网络方法，将绵羊疼痛面部表情量表（SPFES）的标志点映射到3D欧几里得空间中，通过加权几何图神经网络进行疼痛程度分类，并输出归一化疼痛评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习系统主要在2D单图像域中处理面部，将面部视为单维表征，忽略了绵羊的3D解剖结构以及跨标志点之间的空间关系，而这些信息是临床验证的SPFES标准所固有的。

Method: 使用VideoDepthAnything从单目RGB相机估计深度，将每个SPFES面部标志点（如耳朵、眼睛、鼻子）映射到3D欧几里得空间。每个标志点节点包含3D坐标、估计表面法向量和面部属性类别嵌入的特征向量。边权重基于欧几里得距离与表面共面性的聚合度量。使用K=3层的加权几何图神经网络（WG-GNN），采用缩放点积注意力机制增强解剖学相关的标志点间信息传递。将节点嵌入聚为O=3个疼痛级别簇，并通过置信度加权的SPFES推导评分方法输出0-100%范围内的归一化疼痛分数（NPS）。

Result: 系统实现了三维疼痛面部表情识别，无需专用深度硬件即可从单一RGB摄像头获取3D信息，并以置信度加权的NPS形式输出可解释的疼痛评分。

Conclusion: 3D-SPFES通过将临床SPFES标准转化为3D几何图神经网络框架，为绵羊疼痛评估提供了更符合解剖学特征且避免专用深度硬件依赖的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning systems perform mainly within the 2D for a single image domain and take the face as a single-dimension representation, losing sight of the 3D anatomy of sheep and cross-landmark spatial relationships that are intrinsic to the clinically proven Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES). This paper presents the \textbf{3D Sheep Pain Facial Expression System (3D-SPFES)}, a novel, monocular depth-aware geometric graph neural network system that integrates each SPFES facial landmark, such as the ears, eyes, and nose, into 3D Euclidean space estimated from a single RGB camera by using VideoDepthAnything, thus preventing the need for specialized depth hardware. Each landmark node includes a feature vector containing its 3D spatial coordinates, estimated surface normal, and facial attribute class embedding. Edges linked to nodes are assigned weights based on an aggregate metric that combines both Euclidean distance and surface co-planarity in a 3D space. A Weighted Geometric Graph Neural Network (WG-GNN) studies this graph using $\mathcal{K} = 3$ geometry-aware message-passing layers enhanced by a scaled dot-product attention method that selectively enhances anatomically relevant inter-landmark messages. The resultant node embeddings are combined into $\mathcal{O} = 3$ pain-level clusters and integrated into a Normalized Pain Score (NPS) within the range of $[0, 100%]$ a confidence-weighted, SPFES-derived scoring method.

</details>


### [180] [HUI360: A 360° Egocentric Dataset and Baselines for Human-Robot Interaction Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11051)
*Raphael Lorenzo-Louis,Fabio Amadio,Bertrand Luvison,Serena Ivaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HUI360，这是目前最大的野外（in-the-wild）人机交互预测数据集，包含从移动机器人360度全景视角采集的1M预处理注释（2D姿态、面部关键点、分割掩码），并提供了自动化标注管道、基线基准以及首个跨数据集的交互预测评估（含SSUP-HRI数据集的6M额外注释）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在人类环境中日益普及，预测人类意图对于实现主动且具有社交意识的机器人行为至关重要。然而，现有的人机交互预测数据集规模有限且缺乏野外真实场景数据，难以评估模型的泛化能力，因此需要一个大规模、多样化的野外数据集来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 作者设计了一套自动化交互标注管道，用于在任意360度等距柱状投影视频中进行交互注释，并提供人工精炼接口。数据集通过移动机器人在3个月内的多天实地采集，覆盖多种环境，捕获路人和用户的自然自发行为。数据集包含1M预处理注释（2D姿态、面部关键点、分割掩码），使用最先进的计算机视觉方法获取并经过人工整理，同时发布原始全景图像（遵守GDPR）。此外还提供SSUP-HRI数据集的6M注释用于跨数据集评估。

Result: 发布了HUI360数据集及标注管道和代码，建立了交互预测的基准基线，并完成了该任务首次跨数据集评估（HUI360与SSUP-HRI之间）。

Conclusion: HUI360通过提供大规模、多样化的野外数据及完整工具链，填补了人机交互预测领域数据资源的空白，为评估和提升交互预测模型的泛化能力奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: As robots increasingly operate in human-populated environments, anticipating human intentions is essential for enabling proactive and socially aware behavior. Automatic anticipation of human-robot interactions is thus emerging as a crucial perception challenge for embodied agents. To this end, we introduce HUI360, the largest dataset for human-robot interaction anticipation in the wild and its set of baselines. The dataset was collected from a mobile robot, in the wild, over multiple days within a 3-month period, and in several environments, capturing natural, spontaneous behaviors from both passersby and users, and encompassing a diverse range of individuals. This variety enables evaluating and improving the generalization capabilities of interaction anticipation models. We designed a pipeline and share code for automatic interaction annotation in arbitrary 360-degree equirectangular videos, along with interfaces for manual refinement. Using this pipeline, we release the HUI360 open set of 1M pre-processed annotations, including detailed 2D poses, facial keypoints, and segmentation masks, obtained using state-of-the-art computer vision methods and manually curated to ensure high-quality tracking and interaction annotation. Additionally, we release the raw panoptic 360-degree images captured from the robot's egocentric viewpoint (on demand, for research purpose only in compliance with GDPR). Finally, we establish benchmark baselines for interaction anticipation, including the first cross-dataset evaluations for this task: to this end, we also release 6M annotations for another existing in-the-wild outdoor dataset collected from a mobile robot (SSUP-HRI). Dataset and code can be found at https://hucebot.github.io/hui360.

</details>


### [181] [A Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Object Detection Models on a Real-World Multi-Plant Dataset from Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11053)
*Ismail Ismail Tijjani,Sunusi Muhammad Ibrahim,Amina Ibrahim Khaleel,Lanre Olusegun Akinola,Fatima Isa Jibrin,Muhammad Bashir Aliyu,Abdullahi Abdussalam Dalhat,Abdullahi Suiudeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在尼日利亚农场真实采集的AgriAISeg数据集上，比较评估了六种目标检测模型（YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26、Faster R-CNN和RT-DETR）在芝麻、卷心菜和番茄作物检测中的性能。结果表明，RT-DETR整体性能最优（精确率0.768，mAP@0.5:0.95为0.624），YOLOv8和YOLO11表现稳定，而Faster R-CNN在复杂田间条件下准确率显著偏低。YOLO系列模型在训练效率上亦优于Faster R-CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业计算机视觉研究多依赖受控数据集，无法真实反映实际田间种植条件，尤其缺乏对非洲等代表性不足地区的覆盖。本研究旨在通过真实世界数据集评估多种目标检测模型在复杂田间环境下的表现，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建了从尼日利亚农场人工采集的AgriAISeg数据集，包含3,382张芝麻、卷心菜和番茄作物图像，覆盖光照变化、遮挡和视角变化等多种环境条件。对六个目标检测模型（YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26、Faster R-CNN和RT-DETR）进行训练，并使用精确率、召回率、mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.95作为评估指标进行对比分析。

Result: RT-DETR以0.768的精确率和0.624的mAP@0.5:0.95取得最高整体性能；YOLOv8和YOLO11也表现出强大且稳定的检测能力；Faster R-CNN整体mAP@0.5仅为0.466，准确率明显偏低；YOLO系列模型在训练效率上优于Faster R-CNN。

Conclusion: 现代单阶段和基于Transformer的检测器在真实农业环境中为植物检测提供了更可靠和高效的解决方案，优于传统两阶段检测器如Faster R-CNN。

Abstract: The application of computer vision in agriculture has shown significant potential for improving crop monitoring and precision farming. However, many existing approaches rely on controlled datasets that do not adequately represent realworld farming conditions, particularly in underrepresented regions such as Africa. This study presents a comparative evaluation of six object detection models YOLOv5, YOLOv8, YOLO11, YOLO26, Faster R-CNN, and RT-DETR using a real-world dataset, AgriAISeg 1 , collected manually from Nigerian farms. AgriAISeg comprises 3,382 images of sesame, cabbage, and tomato crops captured under varying environmental conditions, including changes in illumination, occlusion, and viewing perspectives. Models were trained, and performance was assessed using precision, recall, mAP@0.5, and mAP@0.5:0.95. The results show that RT-DETR achieved the highest overall performance with a precision of 0.768 and mAP@0.5:0.95 of 0.624, while YOLOv8 and YOLO11 also demonstrated strong and consistent performance. In contrast, Faster R-CNN recorded significantly lower accuracy, with an overall mAP@0.5 of 0.466, indicating reduced effectiveness under complex field conditions. In addition, YOLO-based models exhibited superior training efficiency compared to Faster R-CNN.These findings demonstrate that modern one-stage and transformer-based detectors provide more reliable and efficient solutions for plant detection in realworld agricultural environments.

</details>


### [182] [Entropy-Centric Explainable AI for Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11064)
*Ali Saleh,Abdul Karim Gizzini,Mohamad Ghassany,Ali J. Ghandour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法用于语义分割任务，并配套提出了一种新的XAI评估方法论，以高效衡量所高亮区域的相关性。实验结果表明，该方法优于近期为语义分割改编的XAI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在关键领域（如遥感）中因黑箱决策过程而缺乏透明度，削弱了用户信任并限制了模型的落地应用。尽管图像分类任务的可解释性研究已取得显著进展，但图像分割领域的可解释性仍有较大提升空间。

Method: 提出一种以熵为核心的XAI方法用于语义分割，并设计了一种新的评估方法论来有效量化所提出XAI方法高亮区域的相关性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的熵中心XAI方法在语义分割任务中优于近期为语义分割改编的其他XAI方法。

Conclusion: 基于熵的XAI方法能有效解释语义分割模型的决策过程，且配套的评估方法论能够可靠衡量解释结果的质量，从而提升AI模型在关键领域（如遥感）中的可信度与可采纳性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has become a powerful approach to solving complex problems in critical domains. Many concerns arise regarding the decision-making process of its models, mainly due to deep neural networks outperforming their peers at the cost of ambiguity in feature extraction and prediction. Consequently, in critical domains such as remote sensing, where high-resolution imagery must be analyzed using black-box models, the lack of transparency limits trust in these models and, thus, their adoption. In light of this reality, explaining and understanding the complex decision-making process of AI models has become essential. Explainable AI (XAI) aims to bridge this gap by providing insights into how and why certain decisions are made. While significant progress has been achieved in explaining image classification tasks, image segmentation still offers considerable room for improvement. In this context, this paper proposes an entropy-centric XAI method for semantic segmentation. Moreover, a new XAI evaluation methodology is proposed to efficiently measure the relevance of the regions highlighted by the proposed XAI method. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed XAI method compared with recently adapted XAI methods for semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [183] [CapProbe: Evaluating Detailed Image Captions via Full-Scene Dense Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11074)
*Mouxiao Huang,Qiangyu Yan,Borui Jiang,Han Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CapProbe 是一个全场景密集QA基准，将详细图像描述评估转化为区域对齐的事实核查任务。它包含346张图像、1,868个区域和25,650个问题，覆盖37个L1领域和219个L2子领域，通过语言评判器从描述中回答问题，结合Uncertain选项和Effective Accuracy指标区分未回答与错误回答的问题，并提供密度指标惩罚冗长但信息量低的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考基指标（如CIDEr、SPICE）和LLM-as-scorer协议难以验证密集事实主张，而QA替代方案存在探测密度低、领域覆盖窄或问题与图像区域缺乏显式对齐的问题。需要一种能进行密集、区域对齐的事实核查的评估方法。

Method: 将每张图像分解为覆盖前景和背景的粗粒度语义区域；对每个保留区域生成覆盖10个语义类别的多选题；采用37个L1领域和219个L2子领域的两级分类法组织数据；使用语言评判器仅从描述中回答，引入Uncertain选项和Effective Accuracy指标区分未回答与错误回答，密度指标惩罚冗长而无信息的描述。

Result: 在13个VLM上的实验显示：不同模型间Coverage差距显著，存在清晰的"能力-效率"权衡，且暴露了稀疏或基于重叠的评估经常遗漏的失败模式。

Conclusion: CapProbe通过将无约束的标量评分转化为结构化多选题阅读，降低了开放式评分偏差，在固定读者下保持评判器条件性并产生相对稳定的模型排名，是一种性价比高的密集描述评估协议。

Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>


### [184] [Static in Frames, Dynamic in Events: Rethinking Features in Event Cameras as Motion Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11075)
*Hesam Araghi,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tomen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析事件相机中用于角点检测的两个特征——结构张量的特征值和时空密度值——表明它们本质上是运动线索，并将这些特征与局部几何信息结合用于光流估计。通过理论分析、合成数据集受控实验以及DSEC基准上的真实实验，证明这些扩展特征能提供互补的运动信息，提升光流估计精度，尤其在数据稀缺场景和小容量模型中收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机异步捕捉强度变化，具有高时间分辨率，但与传统帧式视觉不同，其数据编码了场景动力学和物体运动信息，这使得事件数据中提取的特征可能展现出帧式视觉中无直接对应的行为。作者希望揭示这些特征的运动本质，并利用它们增强运动估计任务。

Method: 首先从理论上分析运动角点处的结构张量特征值与运动方向之间的关系；然后在合成数据集上设计受控实验，验证将特征值和密度值扩展至局部几何特征可以提供互补的运动信息，并检验其对纹理和散粒噪声的鲁棒性；最后将这些特征集成到最先进的事件光流网络中，在真实DSEC基准上进行评估。

Result: 在DSEC真实数据集上，添加所提出的特征持续提升了光流估计精度，且收益在数据稀缺场景和低容量模型中最为显著。

Conclusion: 事件数据中的角点检测特征（结构张量特征值和密度值）本质上是运动线索，将其与局部几何特征结合可以显著增强事件光流估计性能，尤其在数据不足或模型容量受限的情况下收益最大。

Abstract: Event cameras capture intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, requiring novel preprocessing methods for downstream tasks. Unlike static intensity snapshots, event data inherently encode information about scene dynamics and object motion, meaning that features derived from events can exhibit behaviors with no direct analogue in frame-based vision. In this paper, we analyze two features used in event-based corner detection---the eigenvalues of the structure tensor and the spatiotemporal density values---and show that they are \emph{motion cues}. We hypothesize that these features, combined with local geometric information, can enhance motion estimation tasks. To validate this, we first theoretically analyze how the eigenvalues of the structure tensor at moving corner points relate to the direction of motion. We then design controlled experiments on a synthetic dataset, confirming that extending local geometric features with eigenvalues and density values provides complementary motion information and is robust to texture and shot noise. Finally, we integrate the proposed features into a state-of-the-art event-based optical flow network and evaluate on the real-world DSEC benchmark, where the added features consistently improve accuracy, with the largest gains in data-scarce scenarios and for lower-capacity models. The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [185] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FEEDS（基于基础模型的效率数据采样），一种标签和计算高效的训练策略，利用视觉基础模型嵌入从未标注的PET/CT临床数据中选择最有信息量和多样性的病例进行专家标注，在减少70%标注负担的情况下达到与全标注（100%）训练相当的病灶分割性能，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA和Dartmouth-Hitchcock医学中心三个独立测试集上展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT成像中的自动病灶分割需要大量专家标注的数据集，但创建这样的数据集既耗时又对专业知识要求高。在有限标注数据上训练的模型缺乏临床所需的准确性和泛化能力，因此亟需一种标签和计算高效的策略来应对标注稀缺问题。

Method: FEEDS是一种一步式训练范式，使用视觉基础模型（vision foundation model）的嵌入来评估未标注样本的信息量和多样性，从而优先选择最具代表性的病例进行专家标注。与无监督、半监督和主动学习方法不同，FEEDS仅需有限的代表性训练集即可完成训练，无需迭代式的多阶段流程，同时节约了计算和标注资源。训练和验证基于AutoPET-III数据集，并在三个留存测试集上进行评估。

Result: 在体素、病灶和解剖区域三个层面评估临床效用，FEEDS优于随机采样标注、基于伪标签的半监督学习以及仅有少量标注数据训练的基线方法。它在三个测试集、FDG和PSMA两种示踪剂以及多种疾病上均展现良好泛化能力，仅用30%的标注数据即可匹配100%全标注训练的性能。

Conclusion: FEEDS有效解决了自动病灶分割框架中的标签稀缺问题，为从大型未标注临床存储库构建代表性且多样的标注队列提供了实用方案，有望在减少标注负担的同时保持临床级的分割性能。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [186] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LGS框架，通过高斯密度化策略（Gaussian Densify Policy）学习剪枝/添加分数，在推理时动态调整高斯结构，同时设计紧凑的跨时间点查询（Cross-Time Point Query）显式聚合多帧邻域特征以提升属性预测精度，在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的前馈高斯重建方法保留观测点与高斯原语间的初始对应关系，将初始化集视为最终表示，无法像优化式3DGS那样通过训练积累梯度来决定表示的密度化；同时共享稀疏骨干仅隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，缺乏对单个原语的跨时间证据的显式聚合。

Method: LGS包含两个核心组件：(1) 高斯密度化策略——基于"剪枝或添加操作引起的局部梯度响应变化可反映结构调整是否有益"这一关键观察，学习由剪枝分数和添加分数构成的密度化图，在推理时直接调整高斯结构的增密/剪枝；(2) 紧凑的跨时间点查询——显式检索并聚合其他时间戳高斯原语的邻域特征，用于可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS在场景重建任务上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式学习高斯结构密度化策略并引入跨时间特征聚合，LGS有效弥补了前馈LiDAR高斯重建方法在结构自适应性上的不足，为场景重建提供了更优的表示能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [187] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过跨通道能量重分配、交错通道分组和双层双分支自回归结构，解决了学习型图像压缩在卫星/应急通信中易受数据包丢失影响的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型图像压缩（LIC）虽在率失真性能上表现出色，但在卫星通信和应急通信等场景中极易受到数据包丢失的影响。其脆弱性源于两个层面：打包阶段信息分布不均匀，以及熵编码阶段存在顺序解码依赖，导致级联错误传播。

Method: 方法包含三个关键组件：(1) 跨通道重分配（ICR）机制，在打包前重新分配通道能量，防止关键信息集中在少数通道中；(2) 交错通道分组（ICG）策略，以跨步方式划分潜在通道，将信息分散到多个受大小约束的数据包中；(3) 双层双分支自回归结构，缩短依赖链以限制丢包引发的级联错误。

Result: 在20%丢包率下，平均PSNR比LossResilientLIC提升1.84 dB，且PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅用均匀随机丢包训练的模型可泛化至Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发丢包场景，且表现优于专门针对该条件训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方案通过同时解决打包阶段的信息分布问题和熵编码阶段的解码依赖问题，显著提升了学习型图像压缩在丢包环境下的重建质量和稳定性，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [188] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX 是一个数据增强库，通过将变换列表、概率、标注设置和随机种子统一封装在单个 Compose 对象中，确保增强时随机值一次生成并应用于训练样本的所有部分（图像、掩码、框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧或体数据），避免了图像与其标注因独立随机变换而导致的对齐错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强可能因为图像及其标注（掩码、边界框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧、体数据）接收到不同的随机变换而破坏训练样本。例如裁剪操作必须对图像和掩码使用相同的坐标。如果代码路径分别选择这些值，会导致数据静默错位。

Method: 将变换列表、概率、标注设置和随机种子统一保存在一个 Compose 对象中。每次调用时随机值只生成一次，并应用到训练样本的所有受支持部分。库将每个对象的掩码、边界框和标签保持在一起，并允许项目添加自定义变换。同时支持保存流水线定义、展示单次调用的处理过程并重复执行该调用。

Result: AlbumentationsX 被设计为在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch 将样本分组为批次之前的位置执行声明的变换。相比现有方案（如 Albumentations）中图像和标注的变换路径可能分开处理，该方法确保所有数据部分共享同一随机采样结果。

Conclusion: 该库保障了数据对齐的完整性，但实践经验仍然需要由使用者判断翻转、裁剪、颜色变化等操作对于其特定任务是否保持正确的标签语义。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [189] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPC16K大规模真实场景伪装目标检测基准数据集（含16,245张图像，涵盖伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三类），并基于该基准提出OPCNet，一种具有存在感知能力的伪装网络，将COD从纯分割任务重构为"目标定位+伪装存在推理"的联合问题，有效降低开放世界场景下的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法大多基于封闭世界假设，默认每张输入图像都包含伪装目标，忽略了现实中可能存在纯背景或非伪装目标的场景，导致模型在开放世界部署时产生严重误报。

Method: 提出OPC16K基准数据集，包含14个来源的16,245张图像，组织为伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三类。在此基础上提出OPCNet，包含三个核心组件：1）层次化存在推理，区分CO、BG和NOCOD三种场景；2）相似性感知伪装关系建模，捕捉前景-背景伪装线索；3）存在感知特征细化，利用存在性预测调节分割特征。

Result: 在OPC16K上的大量实验表明，OPCNet在提出的现实COD评估协议下取得优越性能，在显著降低负样本误报的同时保持了对伪装目标的准确分割。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入大规模现实基准和存在感知网络，推动COD从封闭世界假设走向开放世界场景，为实现可靠的实际部署提供了数据和模型基础。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [190] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管线，通过掩码和课程学习改进时间预文本任务，以捕捉农作物物候特征。在SICKLE基准上，作物类型映射IoU达84.9%，超过光学基线15.3个百分点和现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时序、物候和气候动态挑战，对粮食安全至关重要。现有SAR基础模型要么依赖光学影像进行多模态融合（如TerraMind、CopernicusFM），要么聚焦于目标检测、洪水制图和土地覆盖分类（如SAR-JEPA、SARMAE），缺乏专门面向农业应用、仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管线，通过改进时间预文本任务，引入掩码（masking）和课程学习（curriculum learning）机制来增强预训练管线捕捉SAR物候特征的能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型映射任务上达到84.9% IoU，优于光学基线15.3个百分点，优于现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验证明所提出的自监督预训练管线对SAR强度编码器在农业监测中的应用是有效的，显著提升了作物类型映射性能，为仅依赖SAR影像的农业监测提供了新的预训练范式。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [191] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU，一个用于评估现实场景下无语料多模态机器遗忘的基准，重点关注人物相关知识的删除。同时提出SGPE（相似度门控投影编辑）作为轻量级无语料遗忘基线方法，实验表明现有方法存在遗忘与局部性保持的权衡问题及多模态知识再激活的脆弱性，而SGPE提供了更好的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘方法通常假设可以访问原始遗忘和保留语料库，但在现实删除场景中这些数据往往不可获得。此外，已有工作忽视了人物相关自然习得知识的无数据遗忘评估，导致缺乏现实且可扩展的多模态机器遗忘评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建PRMU基准：聚焦自然习得的人物知识，通过多样化的文本和视觉探针（含对抗评估和细粒度局部性分析）评估模型能否删除目标知识同时保留相关知识。2) 提出SGPE基线：采用相似度门控投影编辑，包含知识位移、受保护的参数空间编辑、以及局部性感知的多模态控制机制。

Result: 在代表性MLLM上的大量实验表明：现有遗忘方法常面临不利的遗忘-局部性权衡，在激进遗忘设置下局部性显著退化，且易受多模态知识再激活攻击；SGPE则在目标遗忘、局部性保持和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU推动了现实和可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究，为无语料场景下的人物知识删除提供了系统化评估基准和有效基线方法，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [192] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出推理式三维高斯分割任务，构建Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个基准来评估常识、空间、功能与反事实推理，并提出了CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与三维场景图结合，实现显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理的解耦，在推理基准和标准分割任务上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的开放词汇场景理解方法仅局限于显式查询，无法处理实际具身交互所需的隐式意图理解、复杂空间约束和常识推理等需求，存在显著的能力缺口。

Method: 提出CausalSplat框架，通过将视觉语言模型（VLMs）与三维场景图（3D scene graphs）相结合，将显式的结构感知与隐式的逻辑推理进行解耦，从而在三维高斯场景中实现多层次的推理分割。

Result: 在推理式三维高斯分割任务中，现有SOTA方法表现不佳，而CausalSplat在Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet基准上取得SOTA性能，同时在标准指代分割和开放词汇分割任务上表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: CausalSplat通过解耦显式感知与隐式推理，有效解决了3DGS场景理解中隐式意图与复杂推理难以处理的问题，为具身智能场景下的三维分割提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [193] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang,Shangyu Xing,Zhen Wu,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMCS（多模态代码切换）预训练范式，通过在训练数据中用视觉对象替换文本实体来实现对象级别的显式对齐监督，仅用50K样本即可匹敌或超越600K图文对训练的效果，并持续提升模型的视觉锚定与感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要依赖图像-文本对的全局表示进行模态对齐预训练，但这种图像级对齐存在指代歧义（referential ambiguity）问题：模型难以从全局表示推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下且语义锚定效果不佳。

Method: 提出MMCS预训练范式，借鉴语言学中的代码切换（code-switching）现象，将文本实体替换为其对应的视觉对象，实现视觉与语言的交错排列，从而强制执行局部视觉-语言锚定。同时开发了一个可扩展的数据合成流水线，生成包含77.3万样本、具有精确对象-实体对应关系的预训练数据集。

Result: 实验表明MMCS具有极高的数据效率：仅用50K样本即可匹敌或超越在600K图像-文本对上训练的模型；并且在不同模型规模上均能一致地提升视觉锚定和感知能力。

Conclusion: 对象级别的显式对齐监督比全局图像级对齐更有效，MMCS范式通过提供精确的局部视觉-语言对应关系，显著提升了多模态预训练的数据效率和语义锚定质量。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


### [194] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个测试时自进化框架（Test-Time Self-Evolving），通过探索、评估、反思、内化的闭环机制，使GUI视觉定位模型在部署后无需人工标注真值即可持续改进，平均准确率提升7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉定位模型部署后参数冻结，无法适应未见过的界面；虽然近期有方法通过测试时强化学习进行适配，但它们无法从未成功的探索中进行反思和纠正。

Method: 提出测试时自进化框架，构建探索-评估-反思-内化的闭环：(1) 探索：代理预测未见界面的定位坐标；(2) 评估：引入MLLM-based Reflector评估生成结果并提供推理反思；(3) 内化：提出Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation，通过条件自教模型将高层推理转化为稠密的token级监督；(4) 校准：设计Contrastive Calibration方法，防止错误的自回归前缀在失败探索中污染监督信号。

Result: 在6个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，相比基础模型平均准确率提升7.4%。这是首个成功将on-policy self-distillation应用于GUI视觉定位测试时自适应的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了GUI智能体部署后适应性的空白，完善了GUI代理的自进化能力，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>


### [195] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1框架，通过强化学习联合优化预测标签与支撑证据，并引入基于时间定位的"证据引导奖励再分配"机制，实现对AI生成视频的鲁棒且可泛化的伪造检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频生成检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度的监督信号限制了模型对未见场景和新出现的视频生成器的泛化能力。文本推理存在幻觉和语义偏差的脆弱性，缺乏客观可验证的证据信号及有效的证据集成机制。

Method: 1) 首次将元检测范式引入AI生成视频检测，在强化学习中联合优化预测标签与支撑证据；2) 提出自动化数据构建流程，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段来生成配对的真实-伪造视频对；3) 提出Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution（证据引导奖励再分配），根据证据质量在标签正确的响应间重新分配奖励，实现证据感知的信用分配。

Result: 大量实验表明VidForensics-M1能够利用可验证的时间证据实现鲁棒且可泛化的AI生成视频检测，在未见场景和新生成器上均表现出色。

Conclusion: 时间定位相比文本推理更适合作为视频伪造检测的可验证证据信号，通过证据引导的奖励再分配机制，模型可以在保持可靠标签监督的同时获得细粒度、可验证的伪造定位能力，从而提升检测的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction.
