Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [196] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对足球场景中3D骨骼人体运动的自监督表示学习框架，以未来运动预测为学习目标。通过引入条件模块对未来运动进行概率建模，显式监督多模态未来轨迹，在提高运动预测精度的同时，学到的表示可迁移至多种足球下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球场景中3D骨骼人体运动理解需要有效的表示学习，而人体运动天然具有不确定性（多模态未来）。现有的自监督方法往往将未来预测视为确定性任务，忽略了多种可能的未来，难以充分捕捉运动动力学。因此需要一个能建模多模态未来、并对未来轨迹显式监督的自监督框架。

Method: 1) 构建自监督表示学习框架，以未来运动预测为核心代理任务；2) 引入条件模块（conditioning module），在3D欧几里德空间中对离散化未来运动建模概率分布；3) 利用未来真实轨迹作为显式监督信号，学习运动中的多模态性。

Result: 在大规模足球运动员跟踪数据上的实验表明：1) 该框架显著提升了运动预测精度；2) 学到的表示能够有效迁移到多种足球下游应用任务，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将未来运动预测建模为多模态概率分布学习问题，并辅以显式的未来轨迹监督，本文自监督架构能够有效捕捉足球场景中人体运动的动态特性，且学得的通用表示具有良好的跨任务迁移性，为足球领域下游应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [197] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性Fréchet距离（AdvFD），通过在学习到的自适应特征空间中对抗性最大化真实与生成样本间的Fréchet差异，同时让生成器在该特征空间中最小化该差异，并引入真实特征白化以稳定min-max优化，从而解决 Fréchet post-training中的"Fréchet hacking"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Fréchet距离的生成器post-training损失过于依赖静态预训练特征空间，这些特征空间提供了不完整且固定的真实与生成分布差异视图，导致直接优化Fréchet目标时出现"Fréchet hacking"——目标指标持续提升但视觉质量或其他特征空间中的Fréchet对齐停滞甚至恶化。

Method: AdvFD在静态Fréchet目标（FD-Loss）基础上，引入一个可学习的对抗性表示：该表示通过对抗性方式最大化真实与生成样本间的Fréchet差异，而生成器则在自适应的特征空间中最小化该差异。为防止对抗表示通过特征放大简单增大目标，引入真实特征白化（real-feature whitening）来归一化其尺度和协方差几何，从而稳定min-max优化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两种backbone上以及不同模型规模下，均能持续提升一步生成器的post-training效果。

Conclusion: 通过动态可学习的对抗特征空间替代单一的静态特征空间，辅以特征白化稳定训练，AdvFD有效克服了Fréchet hacking问题，成为一种更鲁棒的分布级生成器post-training目标。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [World-First SEM-based Recovery of Crash EDR Data from the EEPROM of a Severely Damaged SRS Module Using CrashScan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10152)
*Sergei Skorobogatov,Peter Vertal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对汽车安全气囊（SRS）控制模块中数据存储芯片永久性损坏后的数据恢复方法。即使在处理器和Flash EEPROM芯片因严重事故遭受电气损伤（局部温度超过3000°C，硅基底部分蒸发）的情况下，该方法仍以100%的成功率从真实事故模块中提取了全部关键数据，并通过标准EDR提取工具生成了完整的事故重建报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前，若因事故导致SRS模块中的存储芯片损坏，没有任何可行且经济实惠的方法能够完整提取或恢复其中的数据。这些数据对事故重建和法医鉴定至关重要，因此亟需一种可靠且成本可控的解决方案。

Method: 采用创新的样品制备和成像技术，通过将数据从损坏的原始芯片恢复到供体单元（donor unit）中，再利用标准台式EDR提取工具（Collision Sciences CrashScan）完成完整的数据提取与事故重建。该方法利用了创新的专有样品制备和成像流程，即使芯片遭受机械、电气或火灾损伤也能提取和验证全部存储数据。

Result: 从一次严重事故后的真实SRS模块中成功提取了全部关键信息，成功率100%。尽管芯片遭受了过电压导致的高电流损伤，局部温度超过3000°C，且损坏区域距离片上EEPROM阵列仅几百微米，但没有任何一位数据丢失。使用供体单元配合标准EDR提取工具成功生成了完整的EDR报告。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了即使在存储芯片遭受严重物理和电气损伤的情况下，通过创新的样品制备和成像技术仍能实现可靠的数据恢复。这一方法不仅适用于汽车行业的SRS模块数据恢复，还可推广至医疗、航空和航天等工业领域，为严重损坏器件中的数据恢复提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces fully working and affordable approach to data recovery from automotive SRS (airbag control) module after permanent failure of its data storage memory chip. All essential information was successfully extracted with 100% success rate from a real SRS module after severe accident which resulted in both its Processor and Flash EEPROM chip electrically damaged with visible holes in their packages. Further investigation revealed that the damage was likely caused by overvoltage and subsequent high-current-flow through silicon die of chips. This not only caused some internal metal wires to melt and evaporate but also caused partial evaporation of silicon substrate and the created cavity was filled with melted gold from a bonding wire. This reveals the nature of the failure with local temperatures exceeding 3000°C. Despite to the fact that the on-chip EEPROM array was only a few hundred micrometers away from the damaged area, it did not lose any single bit of information. This proved the approach to be resilient for successful data recovery from severely damaged devices in the future.
  With the donor unit containing the recovered data, standard bench-top EDR extraction was performed using Collision Sciences CrashScan, yielding a full EDR report. The forensic significance of the recovered data, the technical novelty of the methodology, and the complete accident reconstruction are presented and discussed.
  Until now, if because of an accident the memory chip was damaged, there was no feasible or affordable solution for full data extraction or recovery. With the use of innovative and proprietary sample preparation and imaging techniques, it became possible to extract and verify all the data stored inside chips after permanent mechanical, electrical or fire damage. This could help not only automotive but also medical, aviation and aerospace industries.

</details>


### [199] [MarkNull: Model-Agnostic Watermark Removal in AI-Generated Images via On-Manifold Latent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10166)
*Jie Cao,Qi Li,Zelin Zhang,Xiaodong Wu,Lingshuang Liu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MarkNull，一种模型无关的、基于流形潜空间操作的AI图像水印移除攻击方法，通过引入噪声-潜变量对齐分数（NLAS）选择性去相关嵌入水印，在保持语义保真度的前提下将平均比特准确率降至53.14%（接近随机猜测50%）；同时提出摊销变体MarkNull-A实现单次前向传播的高效攻击（0.50秒/图）。该攻击成功攻破Google的SynthID-Image系统，可迁移至视频水印，并配套提出攻击检测机制作为防御对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像的水印移除攻击要么仅对特定生成模型有效，要么在移除水印时造成严重的视觉质量退化。此外，针对现实场景中模型无关（model-agnostic）的水印移除攻击的鲁棒性研究仍十分匮乏，这促使作者探索一种既能跨模型泛化又能保持图像视觉质量的通用水印移除方法。

Method: 核心方法包含三部分：(1) 关键观察：水印图像在生成潜表示与嵌入的初始噪声之间存在强统计依赖关系；(2) 提出噪声-潜变量对齐分数（NLAS）以量化该依赖，并构建优化目标，选择性去相关潜表示与嵌入水印，同时保持语义保真；(3) 提出MarkNull-A摊销变体，将攻击蒸馏为单次前向传播，避免逐图优化，实现0.50秒/图的效率。最后提出攻击检测机制作为防御端。

Result: 在包括事后水印、微调型水印和初始噪声型水印等多类水印范式上的广泛评估表明，MarkNull将平均比特准确率降至53.14%（接近随机猜测的50%），且无可感知的图像质量退化。MarkNull-A实现0.50秒/图的摊销推理速度。攻击成功攻破Google的SynthID-Image系统并保持高视觉质量，同时有效迁移至视频水印场景。

Conclusion: MarkNull证明了基于潜空间操作的水印移除攻击能够模型无关地高效运行，揭示现有水印方案在潜空间层面的脆弱性；同时强调未来水印设计需具备对抗该类潜在空间攻击的鲁棒性，并提供了攻击检测机制作为对应的防御手段。

Abstract: Digital watermarking has emerged as a critical technique for provenance and copyright attribution in AI-generated imagery, yet its robustness against realistic, model-agnostic removal attacks remains poorly explored. Existing attacks either succeed only against specific generative models or achieve removal at the cost of severe visual degradation. In this paper, we propose MarkNull, a model-agnostic watermark removal attack via on-manifold latent manipulation. MarkNull is grounded in a key observation: watermarked images exhibit a strong statistical dependency between the generated latent representation and the embedded initial noise. To quantify this dependency, we introduce the Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS) and formulate an optimization objective that selectively decorrelates the latent representation from the embedded watermark while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive evaluations across different categories of watermarking paradigms, including post-hoc, fine-tuning-based, and initial-noise-based schemes, demonstrate that MarkNull reduces average bit accuracy to 53.14%, approaching random-guessing (50%), without perceptible image degradation. To further improve scalability, we propose MarkNull-A, an amortized, optimization-free variant that distills the attack into a single forward pass, achieving 0.50 s/image with modest computational overhead. Notably, our attacks successfully compromise Google's SynthID-Image system while preserving high visual quality and transfer effectively to video watermarking. Finally, we present an attack detection mechanism as a defensive counterpart to MarkNull and MarkNull-A, highlighting the necessity of developing watermark designs resilient to model-agnostic latent-space attacks.

</details>


### [200] [Withholding the Completing Chunk: Deterministic Pair-Completion Guardrails for Streaming LLM Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10279)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究流式语言模型输出中的一个即时释放问题：完整响应的审核在文本流出后才生效，而重复对部分文本做语义分类成本高且不稳定。作者构建了一种窄而确定性的防护机制，其中每个危险签名是两个词法谓词的合取。防护器在每次释放前扫描累积前缀，并扣留第一个使两个谓词都可观察到的数据块。实验显示，在四个签名族、八个块大小和32个机制试验中，流式决策与缓冲扫描器完全匹配，并扣留了所有补全配对的数据块；八个单谓词对照组全部通过。另一次512次试验的策略比较中，全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检测到所有配置配对，512字符窗口检测到96/128，块局部扫描检测到38/128。固定配对在338个人类衍生的安全响应中标记0个，在394个陪审团标注的不安全响应中检测到0个，确认其覆盖范围是窄的而非通用的。校准的Llama Guard 3 1B基线将310/338安全响应分类为安全，202/394不安全响应分类为不安全。在16,384字符响应上，重复前缀扫描器时间在13.261 ms至829.640 ms之间。因此，配对补全是一个针对小型固定策略的精确释放边界后盾，而非语义审核的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语言模型输出存在"释放时机"问题：完整的响应级审核在文本流出后才生效，已经无法撤回；而对部分文本反复进行语义分类不仅代价高昂，且结果不稳定。需要一个在每次释放前进行确定性检查的机制来弥补这一漏洞。

Method: 构建一个窄而确定性的防护构造：每个预定义的"危险签名"被定义为两个词法谓词的合取（conjunction）。守卫在每次释放文本块之前扫描累积前缀（accumulated prefix），一旦发现某个块使得两个谓词均可被观察到（即该块"补全了配对"），则扣留该块，阻止其流出。

Result: （1）在4个签名族、8种块大小、32个机制试验中，流式决策与缓冲扫描器完全一致，扣留了所有补全配对的块；8个单谓词对照组全部通过。（2）512次策略对比中：全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检测到全部128对，512字符窗口检测到96/128，块局部扫描仅检测到38/128。（3）固定配对在338个安全响应中标出0个，在394个不安全响应中检测到0个，确认覆盖极窄。（4）校准的Llama Guard 3 1B基线上，安全/不安全分类分别为310/338和202/394。（5）16,384字符响应上，重复前缀扫描耗时13.261ms至829.640ms，取决于块大小。

Conclusion: 配对完成机制是一个针对小型固定策略的精确释放边界后盾（exact release-boundary backstop），能够零延迟扣留配对补全块且与缓冲基准完全一致，但它只覆盖固定义务的窄策略，不能替代基于语义的全面审核。其价值在于为特定签名提供确定、廉价的即时拦截，而非通用安全防线。

Abstract: Streaming language-model output creates a release-timing problem: complete-response moderation acts after streamed text has escaped, whereas repeated semantic classification of partial text can be costly and unstable. We study a narrow deterministic construction in which each committed danger signature is the conjunction of two lexical predicates. The guard scans the accumulated prefix before every release and withholds the first chunk that makes both predicates observable. Across four signature families, eight chunk sizes, and 32 mechanism trials, streaming decisions matched the buffered scanner and withheld every pair-completing chunk; eight single-predicate controls passed. In a separate 512-trial strategy comparison, full-prefix scanning and complete buffering detected all configured pairs, a 512-character window detected 96/128, and chunk-local scanning detected 38/128. Fixed pairs flagged 0/338 human-derived safe responses and detected 0/394 jury-labelled unsafe responses, confirming narrow rather than general harm coverage. A calibrated official Llama Guard 3 1B baseline classified 310/338 safe responses as safe and 202/394 unsafe responses as unsafe. Repeated-prefix scanner time on 16,384-character responses ranged from 13.261 ms to 829.640 ms across tested chunk sizes. Pair completion is therefore an exact release-boundary backstop for a small fixed policy, not a substitute for semantic moderation.

</details>


### [201] [From Prompt Injection to Web Exploitation: Revisiting Classic Vulnerabilities in LLM-Integrated Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10281)
*Spiros Tsigkopoulos,Christoforos Ntantogian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化研究了LLM中介型Web攻击（LLM2X），即攻击者控制的输入经LLM集成应用处理后触及传统Web应用危险函数的一类攻击。作者提出了包含LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS、LLM2SSTI、LLM2CommandInjection等八种攻击变体的系统化框架，并通过TicketOracle实验平台验证了LLM2SSRF在五种攻击场景和七个LLM上的可行性，最后提出跨提示、模型、应用和网络层的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛集成到Web应用中（如聊天机器人、工具调用管道和智能体工作流），用户输入不仅影响生成文本，还可能触发后端操作（数据库查询、HTTP请求、文件操作、模板渲染或API调用），形成新的攻击面。然而，现有研究缺乏对这一攻击面的系统性理解，特别是LLM在攻击链中所扮演的"中介层"角色尚未被充分揭示。

Method: 首先对LLM中介型Web攻击进行分类和系统化，提出LLM2X变体家族（LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS、LLM2SSTI、LLM2CommandInjection、LLM2IDOR、LLM2CSRF、LLM2XXE、LLM2SSRF）；随后实现TicketOracle——一个基于Flask的LLM集成Web应用，作为实验案例在五种攻击场景和七个LLM上评测LLM2SSRF攻击的可行性与模型间的差异性。

Result: 分析表明LLM通常并不创造底层漏洞本身，而是作为中介层（mediation layer），在工具启用场景中甚至充当"困惑代理"（confused deputy），将攻击者影响传递到信任模型生成内容的组件中。TicketOracle实验显示不同LLM在SSRF攻击场景下的敏感度存在显著差异，攻击利用同时取决于不安全的应用架构和模型特定行为。

Conclusion: LLM中介型Web攻击构成了现实且复杂的威胁，攻击的成败取决于应用架构缺陷与模型行为的交互作用。建议在提示层、模型层、应用层和网络层采取多层次缓解策略来防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly integrated into web applications through chatbots, tool-calling pipelines, and agentic workflows. In these systems, user input may influence not only generated text, but also backend actions such as database queries, HTTP requests, file operations, template rendering, or API calls. This paper introduces LLM-mediated web attacks, a class of attacks in which attacker-controlled input is transformed by an LLM-integrated application and then reaches traditional web-application sinks. We systematize this attack surface through representative LLM2X variants, including LLM2SQLi, LLM2XSS, LLM2SSTI, LLM2CommandInjection, LLM2IDOR, LLM2CSRF, LLM2XXE, and LLM2SSRF. Our analysis shows that the LLM usually does not create the underlying vulnerability itself; rather, it acts as a mediation layer, and in some tool-enabled settings as a confused deputy, carrying attacker influence into components that trust model-generated or model-influenced content. As an experimental case study, we implement TicketOracle, a Flask-based LLM-integrated web application for evaluating LLM2SSRF across five attack scenarios and seven LLMs. Our results show substantial variation in susceptibility across models, suggesting that exploitation depends both on insecure application architecture and model-specific behavior. We conclude with mitigation strategies across the prompt, model, application, and network layers.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Detection Accuracy: Measuring Explanation Cost, Stability, and Utility for Resource-Aware IoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10349)
*Abdurrahman Tolay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文联合评估了IoT入侵检测中ML模型的预测有效性、解释成本（TreeSHAP）、局部解释稳定性与选择性解释策略，发现XGBoost整体预测最强、Random Forest假阳性率最低且解释稳定性最好，选择性解释在平衡数据上可节省28-32%计算开销，表明可操作的可解释IoT入侵检测需综合考虑多个维度而非仅关注检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习入侵检测研究过度强调预测精度，将解释生成视为计算上免费的后处理步骤，忽略了解释成本、稳定性与选择性调用的实际工程价值。本文旨在填补这一空白，对预测与解释维度进行联合评估。

Method: 构建泄漏安全的CICIoT2023语料库，采用39特征精确哈希、非有限值处理、精确特征去重、保守标签冲突移除及确定性哈希级划分。评估LR、决策树、随机森林和XGBoost在自然与平衡测试分布上的表现，测量TreeSHAP成本，在保持预测不变的扰动下评估稳定性，并使用验证校准策略分配解释工作负载。

Result: XGBoost整体预测画像最强，随机森林假阳性率最低。5000样本下TreeSHAP对随机森林需700.759秒、对XGBoost仅需1.471秒。随机森林总体基础解释稳定性最强；XGBoost保持较高秩与方向一致性但顶层特征更替和归因幅度漂移更明显。平衡测试下约90%假阴性解释覆盖率可节省28-32%计算量，95%覆盖率节省15-23%；攻击密集的自然分布下节省量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 操作上有用的可解释IoT入侵检测取决于预测质量、解释成本、局部稳定性、工作负载流行度与选择性调用等多重因素，而非单纯依赖检测精度。本研究为综合考虑多目标权衡的XAI入侵检测提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Machine-learning intrusion-detection studies commonly emphasize predictive accuracy while treating explanation generation as a computationally free post-processing step. This study jointly evaluates predictive effectiveness, explanation cost, local explanation stability, and selective explanation for binary Internet of Things (IoT) intrusion detection. A leakage-safe CICIoT2023 corpus was constructed using exact 39-feature hashes, non-finite-value handling, exact-feature deduplication, conservative label-collision removal, and deterministic hash-level partitioning. Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, Random Forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on natural and balanced test distributions. TreeSHAP cost was measured, stability was assessed under prediction-preserving perturbations, and validation-calibrated policies were used to allocate explanation workload. XGBoost provided the strongest overall predictive profile, while Random Forest produced the lowest false-positive rate. At 5,000 samples, TreeSHAP required 700.759 s for Random Forest and 1.471 s for XGBoost. Random Forest showed the strongest overall base-level explanation stability; XGBoost retained high rank and directional consistency but showed greater top-feature turnover and attribution-magnitude drift. On the balanced test, about 90% false-negative explanation coverage permitted 28-32% compute savings, while about 95% coverage permitted 15-23% savings. Savings were much smaller under the attack-heavy natural prevalence. These results show that operationally useful explainable IoT intrusion detection depends on predictive quality, explanation cost, local stability, workload prevalence, and selective invocation rather than detection accuracy alone.

</details>


### [203] [Synthesizing Probabilistic Saturating Counters with Differentially Private Formal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10521)
*Zhiming Chi,Lutan Zhao,Depeng Liu,Yong Li,Pengfei Yang,Bow-Yaw Wang,Rui Hou,Cheng-Chao Huang,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,Naijun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于差分隐私对概率饱和计数器（PSC）进行形式化分析，将其与Prime+Probe攻击建模为概率Moore机，推导最优攻击策略并量化攻击者的区分能力，进而综合出满足目标纯DP保证且保持竞争性预测性能的增强PSC参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典饱和计数器是确定性的，容易受到侧信道攻击（攻击者可操纵计数器状态并推断受害进程的分支方向）。虽然已有概率饱和计数器（PSC）被提出以缓解此类泄漏，但现有评估主要是经验性的，缺乏形式化的安全性保证。

Method: 将PSC及相应的Prime+Probe攻击策略建模为概率Moore机，推导最优攻击策略，通过差分隐私量化攻击者的区分能力；基于此综合满足目标纯DP保证的增强PSC参数，并推导平稳误预测率以评估实用性。

Result: 在Prime+Probe观测模型下给出了PSC原语的DP保证；合成后的PSC相比确定性及现有概率饱和计数器，提供了形式化安全保证，同时保持了有竞争力的预测性能；理论预测在基准程序上得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文首次为PSC提供基于差分隐私的形式化安全分析，弥合了经验评估与形式化保证之间的差距；但端到端安全（完整分支预测器在重复或自适应攻击下）仍是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Branch predictors improve instruction-level parallelism in modern processors and are commonly modeled using saturating counters. However, classical saturating counters are deterministic and thus vulnerable to side-channel attacks: an attacker can manipulate the counter state and infer the branch direction of a victim process. Probabilistic saturating counters (PSCs) have been proposed to mitigate this leakage by randomizing counter updates, but existing evaluations are mainly empirical. In this paper, we give a formal analysis based on differential privacy (DP): we model PSCs and the corresponding Prime+Probe attack strategies as probabilistic Moore machines, derive optimal attack strategies, and quantify the attacker's distinguishing power through DP. Our DP guarantee applies to the PSC primitive under the Prime+Probe observation model; end-to-end security for a full branch predictor under repeated or adaptive attacks is an important direction for future work. We then synthesize parameters for an enhanced PSC that satisfies a target pure DP guarantee. To evaluate utility, we derive the stationary misprediction rate and validate the theoretical predictions on benchmark programs. Compared to deterministic and existing probabilistic saturating counters, the synthesized PSCs provide formal security guarantees while preserving competitive prediction performance.

</details>


### [204] [On Understanding, Identifying, and Mitigating Vulnerabilities in Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10530)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对2023-2025年间85篇论文的系统文献综述（PRISMA 2020），量化了智能体LLM安全研究的现状，揭示了攻击研究（3.9:1）远超防御研究，感知层漏洞占66%而行动层漏洞仅占4.7%的研究分布与真实风险严重错配，并提出了四层分类法（感知、大脑、行动、交互）映射13种漏洞类型及七个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已从无状态对话接口演变为具备多步规划、工具调用、代码执行和持久记忆的自主智能体。当这些智能体以真实世界权限运行时（调用API、修改文件、查询数据库），一个被攻陷的推理步骤可能引发未授权数据访问、不可逆状态更改或级联故障，但安全研究社区尚未跟上这一演进步伐。

Method: 按照PRISMA 2020指南在六个数据库中进行系统文献综述，筛选了743条记录，最终保留85篇（2023-2025年）关于智能体LLM安全的论文，并构建四层分类法（感知、大脑、行动、交互层）映射13种漏洞类型。

Result: 攻击研究超过防御研究3.9:1；感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占论文的66%，而行动层漏洞（工具滥用、代码注入、沙箱逃逸）仅占4.7%；代码执行安全占3.5%，工具增强智能体占12%。研究分布与实际世界风险严重错配。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM的不安全性源于架构耦合——弱隔离允许漏洞跨层传播。需要将研究和防御工作重点转向行动层漏洞及跨层防护，聚焦于包含（containment）机制来解决七个开放问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have undergone a shift from stateless conversational interfaces to autonomous agents capable of multi-step planning, tool invocation, code execution, and maintaining persistent memory. When these agents operate with real-world privileges---calling APIs, modifying files, and querying databases---a compromised reasoning step can trigger unauthorized data access, irreversible state changes, or cascading failures, yet the security research community has not kept pace. To quantify the state of the field, we conducted a systematic literature review under PRISMA 2020 guidelines across six databases, screening 743 records and retaining 85 papers (2023--2025) on agentic LLM security. Attack research outpaces defense work by 3.9:1. Perception-layer vulnerabilities (prompt injection, jailbreaking, adversarial perturbations) dominate, accounting for 66\% of papers, while action-layer vulnerabilities (tool misuse, code injection, sandbox escape) appear in only 4.7\%, misaligned with real-world risk. Code execution security accounts for 3.5\%, and tool-augmented agents 12\%. We contribute a four-layer taxonomy mapping 13 vulnerability types across perception, brain, action, and interaction layers, and identify seven open problems centered on containment. Agentic LLM insecurity stems from architectural coupling, where weak isolation allows vulnerabilities to propagate across layers.

</details>


### [205] [Trigger the Straggler: Load Hijack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10614)
*Rui Zhang,Wenbo Jiang,Hongwei Li,Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Chaoshun Zuo,Jianfei Sun,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Load Hijack"攻击，恶意模型提供者通过仅修改MoE模型检查点中的路由器权重并保留私有触发器，当触发器出现时，将token-to-expert分配集中到同一GPU上，使该GPU成为落后者（straggler）并迫使其他设备等待，而普通输入的路由行为仍接近干净参考。这是一种利用专家并行（EP）服务调度的供应链攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行（EP）是服务大规模MoE模型的常见策略，但路由器决策同时决定专家选择和GPU执行工作负载，这暴露了服务调度中的供应链攻击面。研究动机在于揭示恶意模型提供者如何通过仅篡改检查点的路由器权重，在模型分发后被用作触发器控制的设备调度器，从而造成服务性能降级。

Method: Load Hijack采用三阶段优化流程来解决一个核心冲突：奖励触发输入使用目标专家的目标函数也可能使普通输入路由偏向同一专家。该方法通过精心设计的三阶段优化，在保证普通输入路由接近干净参考的同时，实现强触发器相关的token分配集中。实验覆盖三个MoE模型族和四个语料库。

Result: 在三个MoE模型族和四个语料库上，Load Hijack将92.3%至95.6%的触发token分配给目标专家。在实时EP服务中，触发流量导致time-to-first-token增加1.43倍，吞吐量降低至普通流量的0.86倍。结果表明中毒路由器可以作为触发器控制的设备调度器。

Conclusion: 路由器权重本身可被武器化用作触发器控制的设备调度器，从而对EP服务实施性能降级攻击。该研究强调了检查点审计中路由权重和运行时负载审计的重要性，以防御此类供应链攻击。

Abstract: Expert parallelism (EP) is a common strategy for serving large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models across multiple GPUs by distributing experts among devices. Router decisions then determine both which experts process each token and which GPUs execute the resulting work. This procedure exposes a supply-chain attack surface in the serving schedule. We introduce Load Hijack, in which a malicious model provider modifies only a checkpoint's router weights, distributes the poisoned checkpoint, and retains a private trigger. When the trigger appears, the poisoned router concentrates token-to-expert assignments on experts co-located on one GPU. The resulting load makes that GPU a straggler and forces peer devices to wait, while routing on ordinary inputs remains near the clean reference. We find this conditional behavior difficult to achieve because an objective that rewards target-expert use on triggered inputs can also bias ordinary-input routing toward the same experts. To resolve this conflict, Load Hijack employs a three-stage optimization procedure that produces strong trigger-dependent concentration while keeping ordinary-input routing close to the clean reference. Across three MoE families and four corpora, Load Hijack directs 92.3% to 95.6% of triggered token assignments to the target experts. In live EP serving, triggered traffic produces 1.43x the time-to-first-token and 0.86x the throughput measured under ordinary traffic. These results show that poisoned routers can act as trigger-controlled device schedulers and motivate checkpoint audits of routing and runtime load.

</details>


### [206] [A Lightweight Fault-Detection Scheme for Barrett Modular Multiplication Using Multiple Conditional Reduction Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10736)
*Rourab Paul,Paresh Baidya,Krishnendu Guha,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对格基后量子密码（PQC）和全同态加密（FHE）中Barrett模乘（BMM）单元面临的安全威胁（侧信道攻击、硬件木马、老化等引入的故障），提出了一种统计缩减监控（SRM）方法，以极低的硬件开销高效检测随机和突发性故障，保护系统安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式乘法是格基PQC和FHE中资源、时间和能耗最关键的运算，BMM因其硬件友好性和高效模归约能力被广泛应用。然而，BMM单元作为PQC和FHE基础设施中最关键的组件之一，可能遭受侧信道攻击和硬件木马引入的故意故障，以及老化等因素导致的非故意故障，这些故障可能导致信息泄漏并危及系统安全，因此需要有效的防护措施。

Method: 论文采用统计缩减监控（SRM）方法保护BMM单元，通过统计监测模归约过程中的缩减操作来检测异常故障，实现对随机和突发性故障的有效识别。

Result: 所提出的SRM方法在引入最小硬件开销的同时，能够高效检测随机和突发性故障（论文内容在描述结果部分被截断，但总体表明该方法在开销与安全性之间取得了良好平衡）。

Conclusion: SRM方法为BMM单元提供了轻量级且高效的安全防护方案，能够在低硬件开销下检测多种类型故障，从而增强格基PQC和FHE系统的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is the most resource-, time-, and energy-critical operation in lattice-based Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes. Lattice-based PQC schemes such as Kyber and Dilithium have already been standardized, while lattice- based FHE schemes such as BGV, BFV, and CKKS are widely recognized as leading candidate in FHE area. Barrett Modular Multiplication (BMM) for polynomial multiplication is widely adopted in PQC and FHE hardware accelerators due to its hardware friendly nature and efficient modular reduction capabilities. However, Side-Channel Attacks (SCAs) and Hardware Trojans may introduce intentional faults, while aging and various other factors can cause unintentional faults. These faults may target the BM M unit, one of the most critical components of PQC and FHE infrastructures, potentially leading to information leakage and compromising system security. In this paper, we employ a Statistical Reduction Monitoring (SRM) method to protect the BM M unit against such adversarial conditions. The proposed approach incurs minimal hardware overhead while providing efficient detection of both random and bur

</details>


### [207] [A Gateway Architecture for Enterprise MCP Authentication: Unifying Heterogeneous Auth, Identity Delegation, and the User / Non-User Persona Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10760)
*Suraj Kumar,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个企业级集中式MCP（模型上下文协议）网关的产业部署，解决了MCP服务器大规模采用后出现的认证碎片化和治理危机。该网关作为统一聚合、治理和认证层，前置所有下游MCP服务器，贡献了包括双轴认证模型、网关认证层（支持三种企业SSO授权和三种令牌供应模型）、三种端到端身份流程以及部署演进路径在内的四项基于生产经验的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP已成为连接LLM代理与企业工具的事实标准接口，一年内大型组织从零部署到数十个内部MCP服务器，快速扩张导致治理危机：每个团队独立实现认证（有的无认证、有的API密钥、有的完整OAuth），形成碎片化格局，缺乏统一的调用方授权方式、操作追踪手段以及员工离任时的全局权限回收机制。

Method: 提出集中式MCP网关架构，包含四项核心贡献：(1) 双轴认证模型——按用户画像（交互式用户vs自动化非用户）和凭据类型（无认证、静态/动态API密钥、PKCE、客户端凭据、平台应用上下文）交叉分类；(2) 网关认证层——支持三种企业SSO授权类型和三种令牌供应模型（BYOT自带令牌、GYOT生成自有令牌、基于RFC 8693令牌交换的委托OAuth）；(3) 三种端到端身份流——User-to-OAuth2、Non-user-to-Service-Account、User-to-Service-Account，通过客户端-网关-服务器组合实现；(4) 部署演进——从CDN/WAF/边缘边界演进到私有MCP隧道和企业级连接器。

Result: 该架构已投入生产运行，前置数十个MCP服务器，覆盖Web、桌面、自定义SDK和低代码客户端等多种客户端类型，有效解决了大规模MCP部署中的认证碎片化与治理问题。

Conclusion: 集中式MCP网关是解决企业MCP生态治理危机的可行且已验证的架构方案，通过统一认证、授权和治理层，为MCP服务器的规模化落地提供了可复制的生产级参考路径。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become the de-facto interface for connecting LLM agents to enterprise tools, and adoption has been explosive: within a year, large organizations went from zero to dozens of internally built MCP servers. That speed created a governance crisis. Each team implemented authentication independently -- some with no auth, some with API keys, some with full OAuth -- producing a fragmented landscape with no consistent way to authorize callers, track who did what, or offboard a departing employee across the fleet. This paper reports an industry deployment that resolves the crisis with a centralized MCP gateway: a single aggregation, governance, and authentication layer that fronts every downstream MCP server.
  We make four contributions grounded in production experience. First, a two-axis authentication model crossing persona (interactive user vs. automated non-user) with credential type (no-auth, static/dynamic API key, PKCE, client credentials, platform app-context). Second, a gateway authentication layer supporting three enterprise SSO grants and three token-provisioning models: Bring-Your-Own-Token, Generate-Your-Own-Token, and delegated OAuth via RFC 8693 token exchange. Third, three end-to-end identity flows -- User-to-OAuth2, Non-user-to-Service-Account, and User-to-Service-Account -- composing client, gateway, and server. Fourth, the deployment evolution from CDN/WAF/edge perimeter to private MCP tunnels and enterprise-wide connectors. The architecture is in production, fronting dozens of MCP servers across web, desktop, custom-SDK, and low-code clients.

</details>


### [208] [Strategies to Avoid Illegal Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11153)
*Muhammad Mubeen,Arslan Bisharat,Giri Anandhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了企业防止未经授权数据访问的三种主要方法：技术解决方案（防火墙、入侵检测系统、加密）、员工培训和政策执行，并指出通过这些措施的组合应用，企业可以有效降低数据泄露风险并维持监管合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术发展，未经授权的数据访问风险不断增加，数据安全已成为各类企业的首要优先事项。企业需要采取主动措施来防止数据被非法访问，以保护自身数据资产。

Method: 本文从三个维度提出防止未经授权数据访问的方法：（1）技术解决方案：防火墙作为内网与互联网之间的保护边界，入侵检测系统在发现可疑行为时通知管理员，加密确保即使数据被截获也无法被读取；（2）员工培训：教育员工识别网络钓鱼邮件和可疑网站等威胁，并教授正确的密码使用及其他安全措施；（3）政策执行：制定并实施数据与系统使用的适当指导方针，明确违规后果，并定期评估政策以保持其时效性和有效性。

Result: 研究指出，通过综合运用技术解决方案、员工培训和法规政策执行这三种方法，企业能够有效防止未经授权的数据访问，从而降低数据泄露风险并维持监管合规性。

Conclusion: 企业可以通过部署技术解决方案、培训员工和执行政策这三种措施来防止未经授权访问数据，最终达到降低数据泄露风险和维持监管合规的目标。

Abstract: For companies of all sizes, data security is a top priority. The chance of unauthorized data access increases as technology develops. To prevent unwanted access to their data, businesses must be proactive. This study examines technology solutions, personnel training, and policy enforcement as methods to prevent unauthorized data access. Data may be protected from illegal access using technological solutions like firewalls, intrusion detection systems, and encryption. Intrusion detection systems notify the administrator when suspicious behavior is found, while firewalls serve as a protective border between the internal network and the internet. Even if data is intercepted, encryption makes sure it is safe. Another effective method of avoiding unauthorized data access is employee education. Employees must be taught how to spot hazards like phishing emails and shady websites and react to them. Additionally, they should be taught the right way to utilize passwords and other security precautions. To secure data, organizations should create and implement policies. Policies should set out appropriate data and system use guidelines and provide repercussions for noncompliance. Policies should be evaluated regularly to ensure that they are current and useful. Businesses may prevent unwanted access to their data by installing technology solutions, training staff, and enforcing regulations. Organizations may reduce data breach risk and maintain regulatory compliance by taking these precautions. ...

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Deciding When to Switch: E-Processes for Adaptive Minimax Training for Generative Adversarial Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10096)
*Hyunjoo Kim,Sicheng Wu,Agastya Venkatraman,Guang Lin,Sehwan Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于e-process的适应性训练方法用于生成对抗网络（GANs）等随机极大极小优化问题中的动态切换决策，将判别器与生成器的更新切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验，并通过条件e-值累积提供任意时刻有效的I类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据科学中许多假设检验问题无法在预设统计模型的参数框架内自然表达，例如优化算法的动态评估。GAN训练需要在判别器与生成器更新之间反复做出切换决策，而现有方法通常依赖固定更新比例或启发式准则，缺乏理论保障的适应性切换机制。

Method: 将GAN中判别器与生成器的切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验：判别器更新阶段，一个e-process检验"判别器诱导的分离度低于目标水平"的原假设；生成器更新阶段，第二个e-process检验"分离度高于刷新水平"的逆向原假设。证明在条件于观测训练样本时，新鲜的样本索引和潜在变量抽取可产生条件e-值，并累积为e-process，在自适应模型更新和数据依赖切换下提供任意时刻有效的I类错误控制。

Result: 在多模态合成分布和图像基准数据集上，所提出的方法在多种广泛使用的GAN目标函数下匹配或优于最佳固定比例基线。

Conclusion: 提出了一种具有理论保障（任意时刻有效的错误控制）的数据驱动适应性GAN训练切换策略，打破了对固定更新比例的依赖，为随机极大极小优化中的自适应决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern data science increasingly gives rise to hypothesis-testing problems that are not naturally formulated in terms of parameters within prespecified statistical models. One important example is the dynamic evaluation of optimization algorithms, where decisions must be made during training about whether further updates remain beneficial or the algorithm should switch to a different phase. This issue is particularly relevant in stochastic min-max optimization. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) provide a canonical example, as their training requires repeated decisions about when to switch between discriminator and generator updates, yet existing methods typically rely on fixed update ratios or heuristic criteria. We formulate this switching problem as sequential hypothesis testing and develop an e-process-based adaptive training procedure. During discriminator updates, one e-process tests the null that the discriminator-induced separation between the empirical data distribution and the generator law remains below a target level. During generator updates, with the discriminator fixed, a second e-process tests the reverse null that this separation remains above a refresh level. Conditional on the observed training sample, we prove that fresh empirical indices and latent draws yield conditional e-values that can be accumulated into e-processes, providing anytime-valid Type I error control under adaptive model updates and data-dependent switching. Across multimodal synthetic distributions and image benchmark datasets, the proposed method matches or outperforms the best fixed-ratio baselines under several widely used GAN objectives.

</details>


### [210] [Iterative Erasure Count Is Not an Affine-Invariant Concept Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10566)
*Tingan Jin,Shuhang Dong,Haosong Li,Chung-Hsien Chou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过理论构造（高斯模型）与经验实验（视频接触预测）证明：神经网络表征中所谓"概念维度"的迭代擦除计数和累计秩，在保信息可逆重参数化（如剪切变换）下并非不变量，因此不是概念的内在维度。作者区分了模型定义的总体量（生成维度、充分线性维度、最小守卫秩）与过程定义量（停止计数、累计编辑秩），并证明前者在剪切下保持，后者可变。完整累计度量-QR轨迹在一致迁移度量、探针、正则化与打破平局规则时具有仿射等变性，且实验显示不同剪切系数下Adam/QR校准的接受更新次数显著不同。结论：迭代擦除返回的是由表征几何与完整测量过程共同决定的过程相对估计量，而非语义维度本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究反复用"擦除探针方向并计数"的方法来估计神经网络表征中某个概念使用的方向数（维度），但该方法隐含假设该计数是概念的内在几何属性。作者质疑这一假设，试图检验停止计数与累计秩是否在保信息的重参数化下保持不变，从而判断其结果是否具有内在语义意义。

Method: 作者采用三类方法：(1) 在高斯总体构造中，构建保预测问题的可逆剪切变换，比较欧几里得擦除计数从1变到2，覆盖Moore-Penrose OLS及所有有限非负岭权重；(2) 对两输出全QR过程（视频分析动机）展示累计编辑秩从2变到环境维度4；(3) 进行经验实验，在冻结V-JEPA2特征上进行受控重参数化（剪切系数a∈{.5,.75,1,1.25,2}），比较Adam/QR校准的接受更新次数（20次运行），并验证秩零预测保持但欧几里得轨迹改变。

Result: 理论结果：在高斯总体构造中，剪切保信息且保持预测问题、生成维度、充分线性维度与最小守卫秩，但将欧几里得擦除计数从1变为2；对全QR过程累计编辑秩从2变为4。完整累计度量-QR轨迹在度量、探针、正则化与打破平局一致迁移时是仿射等变的（精确协方差只是推论之一，非规范语义度量）。经验结果：身份混合在全部20次大样本运行中仅接受1次更新，而每个剪切系数均接受至少2次更新；受控重参数化保持秩零预测但改变后续欧几里得轨迹。

Conclusion: 迭代擦除测量的不是概念的内在语义维度，而是由表征几何与完整测量过程（探针、正则化、优化器、打破平局规则等）共同决定的过程相对估计量。研究者不应将擦除计数或累计秩直接解读为"概念维度"，除非明确区分并论证其与模型定义的总体量（如生成维度）的一致性。视觉接触实验应被视为对方法的压力测试，而非对接触维度的估计。

Abstract: How many directions does a neural representation use to encode a concept? A common answer repeatedly erases probe directions and reports the stopping count or cumulative removed rank. We show that both quantities can change under an information-preserving invertible reparameterization, so neither is intrinsically a concept dimension. We distinguish model-defined population quantities (generating dimension, sufficient linear dimension, and minimum guarding rank) from procedure-defined quantities such as stopping count and cumulative edit rank. In a population Gaussian construction, an invertible shear preserves the prediction problem and all three quantities, yet changes the cumulative Euclidean erasure count from one to two. The separation holds for Moore--Penrose ordinary least squares and every finite nonnegative ridge weight. For a two-output full-QR procedure matching our motivating video analysis, cumulative edit rank similarly changes from two to the ambient dimension four. Conversely, the complete cumulative metric-QR trajectory is affine-equivariant when its positive-definite metric, probe, regularizer, and tie-breaking are transported consistently; exact covariance is one corollary, not a canonical semantic metric. In a known-rank finite-sample Adam/QR calibration, identity mixing stops after one accepted update in all 20 large-sample runs, whereas each tested shear $a\in\{.5,.75,1,1.25,2\}$ accepts at least two updates in all 20 runs. Controlled reparameterizations of frozen V-JEPA2 features preserve rank-zero predictions yet alter later Euclidean trajectories under practical optimization. These visual contact experiments are stress tests, not estimates of contact dimension. Iterative erasure therefore returns a procedure-relative estimand jointly determined by representation geometry and the full measurement procedure, not a semantic dimension by itself.

</details>


### [211] [Spectral Embeddings of Degree-$α$ Laplacians in Random Dot Product Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10845)
*John Park,Ning Hao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了一族度归一化谱嵌入（包括邻接矩阵和对称拉普拉斯矩阵作为特例），在随机点积图模型下推导出行向中心极限定理，并利用投影高斯贝叶斯误差指标比较不同归一化方法在双社区随机块模型中的表现，发现没有单一归一化普遍最优，选择取决于网络密度、社区失衡度和块概率结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱聚类方法在社区网络分析中广泛使用，但不同的矩阵表示（如邻接矩阵与对称拉普拉斯矩阵）对应的度归一化选择缺乏统一的分布理论分析。此前不清楚度归一化如何影响嵌入的几何结构和节点的局部不确定性，也无法为实践者提供选择何种归一化的理论指导。

Method: 研究一族连续的度归一化谱嵌入，将邻接矩阵和对称拉普拉斯矩阵作为该族中的特例。在随机点积图模型下推导该族嵌入的行向中心极限定理，并利用投影高斯分布下的贝叶斯误差指标，对双社区随机块模型中的不同归一化方法进行理论比较。

Result: 中心极限定理显式描述了度归一化如何同时影响总体几何和嵌入节点的局部不确定性。在双社区随机块模型下的比较表明，不存在统一最优的归一化选择；归一化的优劣取决于网络密度、社区失衡程度和块概率结构。一般而言，在低密度或社区失衡更严重的场景中，更强的归一化更为有利。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的分布视角，解释了不同归一化方法在何时以及为何能够改进谱聚类的表现，为实践者在具体网络场景中选择合适的度归一化策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Spectral clustering methods for network data are commonly based on a few matrix representations, such as the adjacency matrix and the symmetric Laplacian. We study a continuum of degree-normalized spectral embeddings that includes these commonly used choices as special cases. Under a random dot product graph model, we establish a row-wise central limit theorem for this family of embeddings. The result provides an explicit description of how degree normalization affects both population geometry and the local uncertainty of embedded nodes. We use the limiting distributions to compare different normalizations in two-community stochastic block models through a projected-Gaussian Bayes-error diagnostic. These comparisons show that no single normalization is uniformly preferred. Instead, the favored normalization depends on network density, community imbalance, and block-probability structure. Typically, stronger normalization is favored in lower-density or more imbalanced settings. These results provide a unified distributional understanding of when and why alternative normalizations may improve spectral clustering.

</details>


### [212] [Self-Normalized Inference for Constant-Stepsize Temporal-Difference Learning under Markovian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10896)
*Min Zeng,Yichen Zhang,Xiaofeng Shao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对固定步长线性时序差分（TD）学习算法，在单条马尔可夫轨迹上建立了泛函中心极限定理，并提出基于Richardson-Romberg（RR）递归与Brownian-bridge自归一化的推断方法，无需估计长期协方差即可得到渐近枢轴置信域，同时研究了从RR稳态目标到Bellman解的推断方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定步长TD学习在策略评估中具有吸引力，但从单条马尔可夫轨迹进行推断时必须同时处理序列相关性和步长相关的稳态目标。现有推断方法往往需要估计长期协方差或选择带宽/批次长度，这在实际应用中较为繁琐且不稳定。

Method: 作者为固定步长线性TD建立了泛函中心极限定理，其协方差保留由随机TD矩阵和稳态迭代误差所引入的乘性分量。随后推导了由同一轨迹驱动的并行Richardson-Romberg递归的联合泛函极限。利用Brownian-bridge自归一化器构造渐近枢轴置信域，避免了长期协方差估计或带宽/批次长度选择。对于预设的状态值对比，该方法可单遍实现且内存不随轨迹长度增长。此外还研究了水平索引设计，其中步长在每次运行内保持不变并随更长水平递减。

Result: 在固定步长下，推断中心为RR稳态目标。在显式RR依赖的速率窗口下，RR目标偏移、乘性余项和初始效应在根号n尺度上可忽略，从而实现对投影Bellman解的推断。FrozenLake和Garnet实验验证了稳态目标覆盖率、RR目标校正以及水平索引设计的有限样本行为。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套完整的固定步长TD推断框架，在不牺牲推断有效性的前提下避免了传统方法中繁琐的调参环节，并通过RR加速与水平索引设计实现了从稳态目标到真实Bellman解的推断。

Abstract: Constant-stepsize temporal-difference (TD) learning is attractive for policy evaluation, but inference from a single Markov trajectory must account for serial dependence and a stepsize-dependent stationary target. For fixed-stepsize linear TD, we establish a functional central limit theorem whose covariance retains the multiplicative component induced by the random TD matrix and the stationary iterate error. We then derive a joint functional limit for parallel Richardson--Romberg (RR) recursions driven by the same trajectory. A Brownian-bridge self-normalizer yields asymptotically pivotal confidence regions for prespecified state-value contrasts without estimating the long-run covariance or selecting a bandwidth or batch length. For such a contrast, the procedure admits a one-pass implementation whose memory does not grow with the trajectory length. At a fixed stepsize, the inferential center is the RR stationary target. We also study horizon-indexed designs in which the stepsize remains constant within each run and decreases across longer horizons. Under an explicit RR-dependent rate window, the residual RR target shift, multiplicative remainder, and initialization effect are negligible at the root-$n$ scale, yielding inference for the projected Bellman solution. Experiments on FrozenLake and Garnet illustrate stationary-target coverage, RR target correction, and the finite-sample behavior of the horizon-indexed design.

</details>


### [213] [Conditional Independence Tests for Constraint-Based Causal Discovery: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11156)
*Pavel Averin,Theodoros Moysiadis,Ioannis Katakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于条件独立性（CI）检验的综述，聚焦于基于约束的因果发现算法（如PC和FCI）中CI检验的假设、鲁棒性和可扩展性。文章将CI方法分为六大家族（偏相关、列联表、回归、最近邻、核方法和机器学习方法），并分析了每类方法的失败模式及其对骨架恢复和v结构定向等图级错误的影响，同时比较了主流R和Python库的采用情况，并总结了混合类型CI检验、小样本误差控制等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI检验是基于约束的因果发现算法（PC、FCI）的核心统计引擎，但缺乏对各类CI方法的系统比较，尤其是在生物医学领域常见的高维和混合数据类型场景下，不同方法在假设条件、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面的权衡尚未被全面梳理。

Method: 以综述形式，将CI检验方法系统地分为六个家族（偏相关、列联表、回归、最近邻、核方法和机器学习方法），针对每个家族分析其假设条件、适用场景、失效模式以及鲁棒性增强层，并建立测试级性质（如随条件集大小增长的检验功效衰减、I/II类错误不对称后果）与图级错误（骨架恢复、v结构定向）之间的关联，最后对主流软件库采用情况和开放挑战进行总结。

Result: 为每个CI检验家族提供了系统性的比较框架，阐明了各方法在何种数据生成分布下可靠、在何种情况下失效；建立了检验级属性到图级因果发现错误的映射关系；总结了R和Python生态中CI检验工具的支持现状；识别出混合类型数据CI检验（不做离散化）、小样本误差控制和CI检验可扩展性三大开放挑战。

Conclusion: CI检验方法的选择需要根据数据特征（维度、分布类型、样本量）和因果发现目标进行权衡，当前仍缺乏统一适用于混合类型数据、小样本和高维场景的万能CI检验方法，这是该领域未来的关键研究方向。

Abstract: Conditional Independence (CI) tests are the statistical engine of constraint-based causal discovery: in algorithms such as PC (Peter-Clark) and FCI (Fast Causal Inference), skeleton pruning and key orientations follow directly from CI decisions. This survey reviews CI testing with emphasis on assumptions, robustness, and scalability in high-dimensional and mixed-type settings common in biomedical domains. The survey organizes widely used CI methods into six families: partial-correlation, contingency-table, regression, nearest-neighbor, kernel, and machine-learning-based. Special emphasis is provided on the robustness layers that address the limitations of these families. For each family, the survey examines when CI decisions reflect the data-generating distribution and when they fail. By this, we link test-level properties, including power decay with conditioning set size and asymmetric type I/II error consequences, to graph-level errors in skeleton recovery and v-structure orientation. The survey also compares adoption across major R and Python libraries and summarizes open challenges, including mixed-type CI testing without discretization, small-sample error control, and strategies for improving scalability of CI-testing.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Optimal Inference with Black-box Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10155)
*Lucas Kania,Abhinav Chakraborty,Edward Kennedy,Larry Wasserman,Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在高维高斯序列模型下刻画了黑箱预测辅助统计推断的信息论极限，并在预测精度已知（正交预测情形下未知）时给出了理论表征，进而开发了能自适应未知预测精度且利用预测间强对齐关系的实用假设检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量工作提出将观测数据与黑箱预测结合进行有效统计推断的方法，但该领域缺乏一个统一的理论原则来解释假设检验应如何整合数据与预测，才能同时保证有效性和效率。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者在高维高斯序列模型框架下，刻画了预测精度已知时（及正交预测且预测精度未知时）利用黑箱预测进行推断的信息论极限；基于这些理论表征，构建了能够自适应未知预测精度、同时利用预测间强对齐关系获益的实用假设检验方法。

Result: 给出了黑箱预测辅助推断的信息论下界/表征，并开发出在实际中可行、能自适应预测精度且受益于预测间强对齐的假设检验策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为融合黑箱预测与观测数据进行有效统计推断提供了统一的理论框架，填补了该领域缺乏统一原则的空白，并为构建实用且理论上有保证的检验方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Powerful black-box predictive models have motivated many proposals for combining observed data with predictions to perform valid statistical inference. Despite this progress, the field lacks a unifying principle that explains how hypothesis tests should integrate data and predictions in a way that is both valid and efficient. In this work, we address this gap in the high-dimensional Gaussian sequence model. We characterize the information-theoretic limits of inference with black-box predictions when their accuracies are known and, for orthogonal predictions, when they are unknown. Building on these characterizations, we develop practical hypothesis tests that adapt to the unknown accuracies of the predictions while benefiting from strong alignment among them.

</details>


### [215] [Tensor-normal maximum likelihood estimation at the operator-norm sample threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10488)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文回答了一个关于张量正态最大似然估计（MLE）的条件数无关非渐近保证的开放问题。作者将Franks等人(2026)的样本阈值从nD ≳ k²d_max³改进为nD ≥ Ck²d_max²t²，消除了Frobenius到算子范数的损失，并将最大维度因子的误差界建立了与高斯极小极大下界匹配的尖锐Thompson界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franks等人(2026)在nD ≳ k²d_max³的样本阈值下建立了张量正态MLE的条件数无关非渐近保证，但他们遗留了一个开放问题：立方依赖d_max³能否被改进为算子范数尺度d_max²。作者的目标是解决这个显式开放的优化问题，消除Frobenius到算子范数转换带来的额外d_max因子。

Method: 作者提出了以下证明策略：(1) 将随机Gram界从局部群轨道方向扩展到完整局部李代数；(2) 通过精确共轭将其转换到固定的Thompson球；(3) 结合约束MLE的敏感性分析、等变Kirszbraun延拓以及Gaussian浓度不等式。这一组合方法消除了Frobenius-to-operator损失。

Result: 主要结果为：当nD ≥ Ck²d_max²t²时，MLE以高概率存在且唯一；全精度误差d_FR(Θ̂,Θ) ≤ Ct√k·d_max/√n；每个最大维度模式的因子误差d_FR(Θ̂_a,Θ_a) ≤ Ct√(kd_a)·d_max/√(nD)；且对最大维度模式获得尖锐Thompson界d_op(Θ̂_a,Θ_a) ≤ Ct·d_max/√(nD)。阈值在d_max上具有信息论最优阶，固定k时全精度和最大因子的收敛率与Gaussian极小极大下界匹配至√k因子。

Conclusion: 该论文肯定地回答了Franks等人提出的开放问题，证明立方依赖d_max³确实可以改进为算子范数尺度d_max²，达到了信息论最优的样本阈值。研究不假设稀疏性、条件数界或热启动，所得的收敛率在固定k时匹配Gaussian极小极大下界，因此该结果在张量正态MLE的样本复杂度上是最优的。

Abstract: Let $X_1,\ldots,X_n$ be independent Gaussian tensors in $\mathbb{R}^{d_1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbb{R}^{d_k}$ whose covariance is a Kronecker product of $k$ unknown positive-definite factors, and put $D=\prod_{a=1}^k d_a$ and $d_{\max}=\max_a d_a$. A recent result of Franks et al. (2026) established condition-number-free nonasymptotic guarantees for the tensor-normal maximum likelihood estimator under the sample threshold $nD\gtrsim k^2 d_{\max}^3$. They asked whether the cubic dependence on $d_{\max}$ could be replaced by the operator-norm scale $d_{\max}^2$. We answer this question affirmatively. We prove that, for $t\geq 1$, the maximum likelihood estimator exists uniquely with high probability whenever $nD\geq Ck^2 d_{\max}^2 t^2$, and satisfies $d_{\mathrm{FR}}(\widehatΘ,Θ)\leq Ct\sqrt{k}\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{n}$ and $d_{\mathrm{FR}}(\widehatΘ_a,Θ_a)\leq Ct\sqrt{k d_a}\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{nD}$. For every mode of largest dimension, we also obtain the sharp Thompson bound $d_{\mathrm{op}}(\widehatΘ_a,Θ_a)\leq Ct\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{nD}$. No sparsity, condition-number bound or warm start is assumed. For fixed $k$, the threshold has the information-theoretically optimal dependence on $d_{\max}$, and the displayed rates for the full precision and the largest factor match Gaussian minimax lower bounds up to a factor $\sqrt{k}$. The proof extends a random Gram bound for local group-orbit directions to the full local Lie algebra, transports it to a fixed Thompson ball by exact conjugation, and combines sensitivity of a constrained maximum likelihood estimator with an equivariant Kirszbraun extension and Gaussian concentration. This removes the Frobenius-to-operator loss responsible for the previous extra factor $d_{\max}$ and resolves the explicit open problem posed in the earlier work.

</details>


### [216] [Posterior contraction rates in Sobolev norms and Bayesian derivative estimation for infinite-dimensional exponential families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11130)
*Emanuele Dolera,Stefano Favaro,Matteo Giordano*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限维指数族在正阶Sobolev范数下的后验收缩与贝叶斯导数估计问题。通过将自然参数嵌入Hilbert尺度并用高斯级数先验建模，在Fisher信息满足双边链接条件和局部正则性假设下，证明了光滑度匹配的先验可在任意Hilbert尺度范数下达到极小极大最优的后验收缩率。方法基于Dolera等人最新提出的Wasserstein距离后验收缩框架，结合拉普拉斯型估计与混合几何估计。理论被应用于逻辑参数化密度估计、指数链接的泊松强度估计和高斯白噪声模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限维贝叶斯非参数推断中，后验收缩率理论通常局限于特定范数（如L2范数），而实际应用中Sobolev范数下的导数估计同样重要。作者的目标是建立更一般的理论框架，使得在Hilbert尺度下的任意范数中都能获得极小极大最优的后验收缩率，从而支持密度得分函数和泊松强度的导数的高效贝叶斯恢复。

Method: 将自然参数嵌入Hilbert尺度，在由特征基生成的尺度上使用高斯级数先验建模。在Fisher信息满足双边链接条件（two-sided link condition）和局部正则性假设下，采用Dolera等人(2024)提出的基于Wasserstein距离的后验收缩新方法。该方法结合两类关键技术：(1)针对后验核相关的无限维积分的精细拉普拉斯型估计；(2)控制数据波动下后验稳定性的混合几何估计，后者建立在对真值邻域条件下的后验分布的定制Poincaré不等式之上。

Result: 在Hilbert尺度范数中（达到真实函数正则性为止），光滑度匹配的先验可实现极小极大最优后验收缩率。将该理论应用于三个具体设置：逻辑参数化密度估计、指数链接泊松强度估计、高斯白噪声模型，均获得Sobolev范数下的极小极大收缩率，并实现了密度得分函数和泊松强度导数的最优恢复。

Conclusion: 本文建立了无限维指数族在Sobolev范数下后验收缩的一般性理论，将Wasserstein距离方法扩展到了更广泛的Hilbert尺度框架，为贝叶斯导数估计提供了最优性保证。这一理论覆盖了多种经典统计模型，展示了统一的极小极大最优性结果。

Abstract: We study posterior contraction in positive-order Sobolev norms and Bayesian derivative estimation for infinite-dimensional exponential families. We embed the natural parameter in a Hilbert scale and model it via a standard Gaussian series prior expanded in the eigenbasis generating the scale. Under a two-sided link condition on the Fisher information and suitable local regularity assumptions, we show that smoothness-matching priors achieve minimax-optimal posterior contraction rates in any Hilbert scale norm up to the regularity of the ground truth. Our analysis builds on the novel approach to posterior contraction based on the Wasserstein distance recently introduced by Dolera et al. (2024). It combines refined Laplace-type estimates for infinite-dimensional integrals associated to the posterior kernels with a mixed-geometry estimate controlling their stability under fluctuations in the data, itself resting on a tailored Poincaré inequality for posterior distributions conditioned on neighbourhoods of the truth. We apply the general theory to density estimation with a logistic parametrisation, Poisson intensity estimation with an exponential link, and the Gaussian white-noise model, yielding minimax contraction rates in Sobolev norms across all three settings. In particular, these yield optimal recovery of density score functions and derivatives of Poisson intensities.

</details>


### [217] [Limit theorems for a class of martingale arrays with applications in nonlinear cointergating regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10322)
*Qiying Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为一大类鞅建立了新的渐近理论，证明其收敛到涉及随机积分泛函的极限分布，并将其应用于非平稳时间序列的非线性回归模型，为非线最小二乘估计提供了严格推断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鞅渐近理论对依赖结构有较多限制，无法覆盖更广泛的依赖性情形。作者旨在扩展鞅极限定理的适用范围，并为非平稳时间序列下的非线性回归推断建立理论基础。

Method: 发展新的渐近理论，针对更广泛的鞅类别证明其收敛到涉及随机积分泛函的极限分布；然后将该理论应用于非平稳时间序列的非线性回归模型，建立非线性最小二乘估计的渐近推断框架。

Result: 提出了一类更广泛的鞅极限定理，显著扩展了现有鞅渐近理论对依赖结构的覆盖范围；并成功应用于非平稳非线性回归模型，为非线最小二乘估计提供了严格的渐近推断框架。

Conclusion: 该研究通过扩展鞅渐近理论，为非平稳时间序列下的非线性回归模型推断提供了统一且严谨的数学基础，具有重要的理论和方法论贡献。

Abstract: This paper develops a new asymptotic theory for a broad class of martingales, establishing convergence to limiting distributions that involve a functional of stochastic integrals. The proposed limit theorem substantially extends existing martingale asymptotic theory by accommodating a wider class of dependence structures. As a primary application, the theory is applied to nonlinear regression models with nonstationary time series, yielding a rigorous framework for asymptotic inference on nonlinear least square estimators.

</details>


### [218] [Predicting Diagonalizability of a Mean Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10482)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文肯定地回答了Wu和Santhanam提出的问题：从递增的i.i.d.二元随机矩阵样本中，能否几乎必然地在仅犯有限次错误的情况下判定未知均值矩阵是否可对角化（实数或复数域上）。核心发现是一个一般性原则：固定维度的有界均值参数的每个半代数性质最终几乎必然可预测。作者给出了自包含的收缩置信集证明，以及通过多项式符号检验得到的显式预测器。利用Tarski–Seidenberg量词消去法证明实/复可对角化轨迹都是半代数的（尽管既非闭也非开）。结果进一步推广到具有任意固定r>1阶有限矩的无界观测（利用Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund强大数律），并结合Dembo–Peres拓扑准则得到尖锐对比：对于仅可积矩阵律的全体，当维度至少为2时，可对角化性并非最终几乎必然可预测。构造对固定维度有效，但不主张实际复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wu和Santhanam提出了一个基本的序贯推断问题：能否仅从递增的i.i.d.样本中，以几乎必然有限误差的方式判定矩阵序列的均值矩阵是否可对角化。这一问题的困难在于可对角化轨迹在矩阵空间中既非闭集也非开集，使得基于常规拓扑方法的判定策略失效。

Method: 本文的方法基于三个关键工具：(1) 一个一般性原则——固定维度有界均值参数的每个半代数性质最终几乎必然可预测，通过自包含的收缩置信集证明；(2) 利用Tarski–Seidenberg量词消去法证明实/复可对角化轨迹均为半代数集；(3) 通过多项式符号检验构造显式预测器，并利用Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund强大数律将结果推广到具有任意r>1阶有限矩的无界观测情形。

Result: (1) 对任意维度，可对角化性（实或复）可以从递增i.i.d.样本中以几乎必然有限误差预测；(2) 该结果可推广到任意r>1阶有限矩的无界观测；(3) 结合Dembo–Peres拓扑准则，对于仅可积的矩阵律，维度至少为2时可对角化性不可预测，从而划出了结果的最优边界。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Wu–Santhanam提出的可对角化性判定问题，建立了"半代数性质可预测性"这一一般原理，并刻画了可预测性的精确条件（需要r>1阶矩），揭示了可对角化判定问题中矩条件与拓扑条件之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Wu and Santhanam asked whether one can determine, from an increasing i.i.d. sample of binary random matrices, whether the unknown mean matrix is diagonalizable while making only finitely many errors almost surely. We answer this question affirmatively, for diagonalizability over either $\mathbb{R}$ or $\mathbb{C}$. The main observation is a general principle: every semialgebraic property of a fixed-dimensional bounded mean parameter is eventually almost surely predictable. We give a self-contained shrinking-confidence-set proof and an explicit predictor obtained from polynomial sign tests. Tarski--Seidenberg quantifier elimination shows that both the real- and complex-diagonalizable loci are semialgebraic, despite being neither closed nor open. We further extend the positive result to unbounded observations with any fixed finite moment of order $r>1$, using the Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund strong law. Combined with the Dembo--Peres topological criterion, this yields a sharp contrast: over the class of all merely integrable matrix laws, diagonalizability is not eventually almost surely predictable when the dimension is at least two. The construction is effective for fixed dimension, although no practical complexity bound is claimed.

</details>


### [219] [Central limit theory for serial tail dependence estimators in heavy-tailed long memory linear time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10944)
*Ioan Scheffel,Marco Oesting,Gilles Stupfler*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了重尾长记忆线性时间序列中序列尾部相依估计量的多个中心极限定理，其核心工具是两种新颖的多变量约化原理，分别针对滑动窗口上的部分和以及随样本量增长的门槛之上的部分和。理论允许创新过程具有无限方差，且第二个约化原理在门槛上一致成立，从而支持使用样本分位数作为门槛的经验极差图的中心极限理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列极值相依估计（如序列极差图）在长记忆重尾时间序列中的极限分布尚未得到充分研究。特别是以样本分位数为随机门槛的经验极差图版本在实际中广泛应用，但在现有文献中缺乏相应的中心极限理论支持。同时，需要处理无限方差、非线性与样本量相关的多变量从属机制、多个重叠线性过程之间的依赖等根本性困难。

Method: 提出了两种新颖的多变量约化原理：(1) 针对重尾长记忆线性时间序列在滑动窗口上的部分和；(2) 针对门槛随样本量增长的部分和，且该约化原理在门槛上一致成立。通过处理非线性、样本量相关及多变量的从属机制，以及多个重叠线性过程之间的依赖关系，在缺少边际分布高阶矩的条件下建立中心极限定理。

Result: 建立了序列尾部相依估计量在重尾长记忆设置下的多个中心极限定理，特别涵盖了以样本分位数为随机门槛的经验极差图的极限理论。与短程相依情况相比，发现了显著不同的收敛速率和极限分布。

Conclusion: 本文在温和假设下（允许创新过程具有无限方差）为长记忆时间序列的序列极值相依估计提供了完整的中心极限理论基础，弥补了该领域关于随机门槛经验极差图的空白，并揭示了长记忆与短程相依设置在收敛速率和极限法则上的本质差异。

Abstract: We prove multiple central limit theorems for serial tail dependence estimators in heavy-tailed long memory linear time series. The main theoretical tools are two novel multivariate reduction principles for partial sums of heavy-tailed long memory linear time series, subordinated over sliding windows and above a threshold growing with sample size. This requires addressing several substantial difficulties, including handling a nonlinear, sample-size dependent, and multivariate subordination mechanism, the dependence between several overlapping linear processes, and the lack of higher-order moments of the marginal distribution. Despite these obstacles, our assumptions are mild and, in particular, the innovation process is allowed to have infinite variance. A key feature of our theory is that our second reduction principle holds uniformly in the threshold, allowing central limit theory for empirical extremograms with sample quantiles as thresholds. This question has received little attention in the literature on serial extremal dependence estimation even though the version of empirical extremograms with random thresholds is ubiquitous in practice. We compare our results in several respects with those that may be obtained under short-range dependence, thereby discovering markedly different convergence rates and limit laws in our long memory setting.

</details>


### [220] [Quantifying the noise sensitivity of the Wasserstein metric for images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01015)
*Erik Lager,Gilles Mordant,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wasserstein度量在图像处理中对像素级加性噪声的敏感性。作者推导了高斯噪声模型下的有限样本期望界，证明带符号的2-Wasserstein差异的误差随噪声标准差的平方根缩放，优于欧几里得度量（线性缩放），从理论上解释了最优传输距离在噪声环境中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein度量作为图像相似度分数被日益广泛采用，但其对像素级加性噪声的敏感性缺乏理论分析。需要从理论上理解最优传输距离在噪声条件下为何优于欧几里得度量，为实际应用提供理论依据。

Method: 将图像视为像素网格上的离散测度，针对高斯噪声模型推导有限样本期望界。核心贡献是证明带符号2-Wasserstein差异的误差与噪声标准差呈平方根关系（O(√σ)），而欧几里得度量误差为线性关系（O(σ)）。同时通过实验验证理论发现，并指出一个反直觉现象：噪声水平增加可能反而降低Wasserstein距离。

Result: 证明了带符号2-Wasserstein差异对噪声的敏感性优于欧几里得度量（平方根vs线性缩放）。实验验证了理论结果，并发现增加噪声可能降低Wasserstein距离的奇特现象。在冷冻电镜（cryo-EM）图像案例中，Wasserstein度量在高噪声条件下仍能捕捉数据流形的几何结构，而欧几里得度量失效。

Conclusion: Wasserstein度量在高噪声图像分析中具有理论上的鲁棒性优势，尤其适用于冷冻电镜等真实高噪声场景。平方根缩放规律为最优传输距离在噪声图像处理中的优势提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Wasserstein metrics are increasingly adopted as similarity scores for images. We consider the sensitivity of Wasserstein metrics with respect to pixel-wise additive noise when the images are treated as discrete measures on the pixel grid. We derive finite-sample expectation bounds for a Gaussian noise model. Among other results, we prove that the error in the signed 2-Wasserstein discrepancy scales with the square root of the noise standard deviation. This is favorable compared to the Euclidean metric that scales linearly, and thus provides a theoretical basis for the benefits of optimal transport distances in noisy settings. We present experiments that support our theoretical findings and point to a peculiar phenomenon where increasing the level of noise can decrease the Wasserstein distance. A case study on cryo-electron microscopy images demonstrates that the Wasserstein metric can capture the geometry of the data manifold in high noise settings even when the Euclidean metric fails.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路径积分值匹配（PI-VM）算法，通过截断和边缘化原始路径积分公式，推导出值函数的时序递归形式，并采用时序差分学习和Girsanov定理结合经验回放实现离策略训练。实验表明，PI-VM在低维场景中能以数量级的效率提升匹配SOTA精度，在高维场景中有效缓解模式崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性二次随机最优控制（LQ-SOC）是控制噪声动力学系统的基本框架，在机器学习社区重新受到关注。然而，现有的基于策略的方法（policy-based）因严重依赖全轨迹仿真而遭受高昂的计算成本和稳定性问题。作者希望克服这些局限，提出一种基于价值（value-based）的新范式。

Method: PI-VM的核心方法包括：(1) 对标准路径积分控制（PIC）的路径积分公式进行截断和边缘化，推导出值函数的时序递归形式；(2) 利用时序差分（TD）学习近似递归值动态；(3) 整合Girsanov定理与经验回放（experience replay）实现离策略（off-policy）训练。

Result: 在多个SOC基准测试和采样任务上，PI-VM在低维设置中以数量级的效率增益匹配SOTA策略方法的精度，在高维场景中有效缓解模式崩溃（mode collapse），展现出显著的计算效率优势。

Conclusion: PI-VM提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，用于求解复杂的SOC问题，标志着从基于策略的方法向基于价值方法的范式转变，兼具精度与效率优势。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [222] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了多类学习最优超额风险的“乐观率”空白：对于任意固定的预言机风险L*，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n) + d_DS/n)，统一于字母表大小，不依赖L*和置信度。上下界分别通过一个新的比较器面向的相对压缩定理和pair-Assouad方案实现，并推广到列表学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类学习的最坏情况界无法在最优分类器已经近似正确时随之缩小，缺少一个随预言机风险本身波动的乐观率。已知两个端点（可实现情形d_DS/n与不可知情形√(d_N/n)+d_DS/n）的最优超额风险，但中间的过渡区间是开放的，本文旨在闭合这一间隙。

Method: 上界采用覆盖-菜单-压缩架构（CEH+26），结合可实现速率（Pab26），并引入一个新的比较器面向的相对压缩定理：一个大小为k的压缩规则若经验上支配比较器h，则其群体风险至多为L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)，其中Γ=(k log n + log(1/δ))/n，无需稳定性。下界通过在任意固定L*处使用单一类和单一分布，采用校准于L*的pair-Assouad方案以及伪立方体上的纤维论证来同时迫使两个项。两大定理均推广至列表学习。

Result: 在任意固定预言机风险L*处，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，统一于字母表大小。该率由不知道L*也不知道置信度的学习者达到。两个定理均扩展到列表学习：针对最优r元组的假设，相同架构和两个引擎产生乐观率及相同形状的下界，迫使波动项存在并移除已知可实现列表下界中的因子r。

Conclusion: 本文成功闭合了多类学习在可实现与完全不可知两端点之间的乐观率间隙，给出了统一的乐观超额风险刻画，并通过新型相对压缩定理将二元锐利理论中的比较原理推广至多类设置（无需布尔立方体几何），同时将结果延伸至列表学习，证明其最优性。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [223] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵结构的逆强化学习（IRL）双层级优化求解方法。作者证明在内层策略优化达到最优时，内层目标的Hessian矩阵与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成正比，从而得到结构化Fisher超梯度（近似自然超梯度下降）。为应对大Fisher矩阵的存储与计算瓶颈，采用流式谱草图（streaming spectral sketch）近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免了显式构造Fisher矩阵。在离散与连续控制环境中的实验表明，该方法在策略性能与奖励排序质量上与一阶随机双层级基线相当，同时降低了曲率存储复杂度并提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRL的自然方式是将其建模为双层优化：内层在习得奖励下进行策略优化，外层衡量诱导策略与专家示教数据的差异。然而该公式在实践中计算困难，因为外层更新需要涉及内层目标逆Hessian-向量积的超梯度，计算成本极高。本文旨在解决这一计算挑战。

Method: 1) 理论贡献：证明在内层最优解处，内层目标的Hessian矩阵与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成正比，从而将逆Hessian-向量积转化为逆Fisher-向量积，得到Fisher化的超梯度，与自然超梯度下降（Natural Hypergradient Descent）紧密相关。2) 计算贡献：为应对大Fisher矩阵的规模瓶颈，采用流式谱草图技术近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造与存储Fisher矩阵。

Result: 在离散与连续控制环境中，与一阶随机双层级基线相比，该方法取得了有竞争力的策略性能和较强的奖励排序质量。Fisher草图技术降低了曲率存储复杂度，相比显式Fisher求解器可提升计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过Fisher信息矩阵与内层Hessian的结构化关联，配合流式谱草图近似，可有效解决IRL双层优化中超梯度计算的瓶颈，在保持性能的同时大幅降低存储与计算开销。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [224] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TGO-IV，一个基于持久同调的拓扑框架，用于分析Transformer各层中表征点云（representation point cloud）的演化过程，旨在揭示原始输入表征如何逐步转化为任务相关特征表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整体网络的表征，但忽略了单个表征及其流形在跨层间的发育演化过程。作者旨在通过提供跨层表征演化的全面分析，更接近地回答"Transformer是如何学习的"这一关键问题，特别是确定原始输入表征何时以及如何演化为任务相关特征。

Method: TGO-IV采用拓扑学框架，通过持久同调（Persistent Homology）从token级表征点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并追踪其持久拓扑特征在Transformer各层间的演化。框架包含多种互补的拓扑观测器：持久图（Persistence Diagrams）、条形图（Barcode Diagrams）、Betti曲线（Betti Curves）、持久景观（Persistence Landscapes）、瓶颈距离（Bottleneck Distance）和Wasserstein距离，从而对表征点云全局拓扑在前向传播中的发展进行综合分析。

Result: 该框架提供了对Transformer表征跨层演化的全面拓扑分析手段，能够识别出表征从原始输入到任务相关特征转变的关键层或趋势，补充了传统仅关注局部几何性质的可解释性研究。

Conclusion: TGO-IV通过拓扑学视角为理解Transformer表征的逐层演化提供了新的分析工具，有望帮助研究者定位表征转换的关键层并揭示学习机制，为Transformer可解释性研究开辟了基于持久同调的新方向。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [225] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于直觉模糊集理论的深度随机神经网络框架IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL，通过联合考虑样本的隶属度与非隶属度，在核空间中利用样本邻域信息对训练样本分配自适应权重，从而有效区分干净样本、噪声和离群点，提升模型在含噪真实数据上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁地处理，当应用于包含噪声和离群点的真实数据集时鲁棒性和有效性受限；此外，污染特征在隐藏层间的传播会负面影响模型的决策能力。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，将直觉模糊理论融入深度随机神经网络。具体地：在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，同时考虑每个样本的隶属度与非隶属度；隶属度基于样本到其类别质心的距离计算，非隶属度量化样本在局部邻域内的异质性；这些度量用于为训练样本分配自适应权重，以区分干净样本、噪声点和离群点。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上开展了大量实验（含/不含高斯噪声场景），结果表明所提出的IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型在性能上优于现有的SOTA模糊与非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 基于直觉模糊理论的自适应加权策略能够有效提升深度随机神经网络的鲁棒性，尤其在处理含噪声和离群点的真实数据集时表现出显著优势。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [226] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurveFP 是一种封闭乘积（closed-product）码本族，通过交错对数曲线和紧凑块尺度分布量化幅值，使用有理基数调节动态范围与局部分辨率，使非零乘积在代数上封闭（符号 XOR 加整数索引更新）。实例化为 CurveFP 八（E4C3/E5C2，训练）和 CurveFP 七（E3C3，部署），在多个 7B-9B 模型及 128.3M 参数预训练实验中，以更少比特达到或超越 FP8 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数据类型能降低大语言模型成本，但现有格式大多只优化标量保真度（scalar fidelity），而由其乘积诱导的算术运算保持不变。这意味着量化乘积仍需浮点或整数乘加运算，无法从算术层面降低计算复杂度。作者希望设计一种数据类型，使乘积本身在码本内封闭，从而简化乘积路径。

Method: 提出 CurveFP 码本族：将量化幅值分布在交错的对数曲线上，由紧凑块尺度管理。采用有理基数（rational radix）以在动态范围和局部分辨率之间权衡；统一曲线索引保证任何非零乘积仍落在码本内（代数封闭）。乘积计算退化为精确的符号 XOR 和整数索引加法，并由推导出的有限相位计数（finite phase count）决定累加调度。实例化两种格式：CurveFP 八（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练，CurveFP 七（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP 七在四个 7B-9B 模型上以少一个元素位击败张量级 FP8 的困惑度，且与原生质量差距在 1.32% 以内。CurveFP 八在全部 36 组前向/反向 GEMM 对比中降低操作数 NMSE。在三组匹配的 128.3M 参数三元组实验中，每个模式完成 3B-token 预训练；CurveFP 八达到平均 BF16 推理困惑度 22.5366（FP8 为 22.5407），且在全部三个种子中格式诱导惩罚更低。36 格下游矩阵中，CurveFP 八训练的检查点在全部 12 个种子-格式比较中 WikiText-103 困惑度更低。

Conclusion: CurveFP 是一种算术协同设计（arithmetic co-design），兼具 FP8 级数值行为、七位推理能力以及大幅简化的乘积通路，验证了在数据类型层面同时优化数值与算术结构是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [227] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SFRL（Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning）框架，一种基于层流（sheaf）理论的联邦表示学习方法。该方法不假设全局共享潜在空间，而是通过学习可学习的层流限制映射，在相邻潜在表示之间施加几何对齐正则化（利用层流拉普拉斯算子诱导的二次粘合正则项），在异构联邦系统中实现表示学习与交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统面临数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度以及本地学习目标等方面的差异，传统的联邦学习方法通常假设存在全局共享的潜在空间，这一假设在高度异构的场景下不成立，因此需要一种更通用的表示学习框架来应对这些挑战。

Method: SFRL框架通过以下关键组件实现：1) 联合优化本地目标与基于流形约束的几何对齐正则化器；2) 利用正交变换和等距嵌入实现邻居潜在表示的几何对齐；3) 由层流拉普拉斯算子诱导的二次粘合正则项，其可学习限制映射使几何自适应于观测数据；4) 仅在一小组共享的 pilot 样本上评估惩罚项以保证可扩展性；5) 提出Sheaf-FRL去中心化算法，交替进行本地模型梯度更新和边级限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新；6) 在确定性和随机设置下均证明其收敛到一阶稳定点。

Result: 在语义通信背景下的协同分类任务中，面对模型和数据异构性，Sheaf-FRL在本地及通信后分类准确率上均优于基线方法，在不同本地分布偏移水平下表现更优，并且对潜在空间维度压缩具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SFRL是一种无需共享全局潜在空间假设的通用联邦表示学习框架，通过层流理论驱动的几何对齐实现了异构联邦系统中的有效学习，兼具理论收敛保证和实际场景中的应用优势。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [228] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究了工具文档信息对LLM智能体性能的影响，发现不同信息字段的效果高度依赖任务领域、LLM主干和智能体范式，不存在通用最优文档。据此提出DocsChisel自适应工具文档优化框架，通过分析失败执行轨迹识别文档相关问题并迭代增删改进信息字段，相比原始文档提升95.89%的任务成功率，相比现有基线平均提升75.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，而将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管少数近期工作尝试通过重写或压缩优化文档，但对工具文档中包含的信息如何在不同设置下影响智能体性能仍缺乏系统认识。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究，揭示现有工具文档信息字段的异质性及其效果对任务领域、LLM主干和智能体范式的高度依赖性。基于此发现，提出DocsChisel框架：通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹来识别文档相关问题，然后对每个工具迭代地进行信息字段的添加、移除和精炼优化。

Result: DocsChisel相比原始工具文档将LLM智能体任务成功率提升95.89%，相比两个SOTA基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时仅引入有限的优化时间和token开销。
